    1: import type { ReportEvidenceRendererProps } from "../report-design-component-types";
    2: 
    3: export function MoeOfficial2024EvidenceRenderer({
    4:   block,
    5:   items,
    6:   startIndex,
    7:   placeholderMode,
    8:   gridStyle,
    9:   getImageUrl,
   10:   getFigureStyle,
   11:   getImageStyle,
   12:   getImageClassName,
   13:   getImageHeightClassName,
   14:   renderTitle,
   15: }: ReportEvidenceRendererProps) {
   16:   if (block.evidenceLayout === "ATTACHMENT_LIST") {
   17:     return (
   18:       <section className="moe24-report-section">
   19:         {renderTitle()}
   20:         <div
   21:           className="moe24-evidence-grid"
   22:           style={{ gap: "calc(5mm * var(--report-evidence-spacing-scale, 1))" }}
   23:         >
   24:           {items.map((evidence, index) => (
   25:             <div key={evidence.id || String(index)} className="moe24-file-attachment">
   26:               {evidence.caption || evidence.title || `مرفق ${startIndex + index + 1}`}
   27:             </div>
   28:           ))}
   29:         </div>
   30:       </section>
   31:     );
   32:   }
   33: 
   34:   return (
   35:     <section className="moe24-report-section">
   36:       {renderTitle()}
   37:       <div
   38:         className="moe24-evidence-grid"
   39:         style={{
   40:           ...gridStyle,
   41:           gap: `calc(${String(gridStyle.gap || "5mm")} * var(--report-evidence-spacing-scale, 1))`,
   42:         }}
   43:       >
   44:         {items.map((evidence, index) => {
   45:           const imageUrl = getImageUrl(evidence);
   46: 
   47:           return (
   48:             <figure
   49:               key={evidence.id || imageUrl || String(index)}
   50:               style={getFigureStyle()}
   51:               className="moe24-evidence-figure"
   52:             >
   53:               {imageUrl && !placeholderMode ? (
   54:                 <img
   55:                   src={imageUrl}
   56:                   alt={evidence.title || `شاهد ${startIndex + index + 1}`}
   57:                   style={getImageStyle()}
   58:                   className={getImageClassName()}
   59:                 />
   60:               ) : (
   61:                 <div
   62:                   data-report-design-real-evidence={placeholderMode ? undefined : "true"}
   63:                   className={`report-design-evidence-fallback ${getImageHeightClassName()} flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-center bg-transparent text-center`}
   64:                 >
   65:                   <p>{placeholderMode ? "مساحة شاهد للمعاينة" : evidence.title || evidence.caption || "مرفق محفوظ"}</p>
   66:                 </div>
   67:               )}
   68: 
   69:               {block.evidenceShowCaptions !== false ? (
   70:                 <figcaption
   71:                   style={{
   72:                     fontSize: "calc(1em * var(--report-evidence-caption-scale, 1))",
   73:                   }}
   74:                 >
   75:                   {evidence.caption || evidence.title || `شاهد ${startIndex + index + 1}`}
   76:                 </figcaption>
   77:               ) : null}
   78:             </figure>
   79:           );
   80:         })}
   81:       </div>
   82:     </section>
   83:   );
   84: }

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
    4: 
    5: import {
    6:   isReportDesignId,
    7:   reportDesignTemplates,
    8: } from "../report-design-registry";
    9: import { getReportDesignImplementation } from "../report-design-implementations";
   10: import type { ReportDesignId } from "../report-design-types";
   11: 
   12: export {
   13:   reportDesignTemplates,
   14:   selectableReportDesignTemplates,
   15:   SELECTABLE_REPORT_DESIGN_IDS,
   16:   isSelectableReportDesignId,
   17: } from "../report-design-registry";
   18: export type { ReportDesignId } from "../report-design-types";
   19: 
   20: import { getReportHeaderSettingsStyle } from "./report-header";
   21: import type { PreviewCaseData, ReportDesignRendererProps } from "./report-types";
   22: 
   23: import { getReportDesignSignatureStyleText } from "./report-signatures";
   24: import { SmartPhysicalReportComposer } from "../smart-layout/report-smart-physical-pages";
   25: import type { ReportTwoPhysicalNavigationItem } from "../smart-layout/report-smart-physical-types";
   26: 
   27: 
   28: export function ReportDesignRenderer({
   29:   designId,
   30:   template,
   31:   activePage,
   32:   activePageId,
   33:   context,
   34:   previewCase,
   35:   onActivePageChange,
   36:   onAddPage,
   37:   onMovePage,
   38:   onDeletePage,
   39:   canMovePage,
   40:   canDeletePage,
   41:   renderMode = "single",
   42:   chromeLayout = "joined",
   43:   suppressAutoEvidencePages = false,
   44: }: ReportDesignRendererProps) {
   45:   const selectedDesign = normalizeDesignId(
   46:     designId || template?.designTemplateId || "ministry-form",
   47:   );
   48:   const implementation = getReportDesignImplementation(selectedDesign);
   49: 
   50:   const logoStyleText = getReportDesignLogoStyleText(
   51:     context,
   52:     implementation.defaultLogoWidthPx || 96,
   53:     implementation.defaultLogoHeightPx || 56,
   54:   );
   55:   const headerStyleText = getReportHeaderSettingsStyle(
   56:     template?.designConfig?.header,
   57:   );
   58:   const signatureStyleText = getReportDesignSignatureStyleText();
   59:   const pages = useMemo<any[]>(
   60:     () => template?.pages || [],
   61:     [template?.pages],
   62:   );
   63:   const [physicalNavigationItems, setPhysicalNavigationItems] = useState<
   64:     ReportTwoPhysicalNavigationItem[]
   65:   >([]);
   66:   const [activePhysicalPageId, setActivePhysicalPageId] = useState("");
   67: 
   68:   useEffect(() => {
   69:     setPhysicalNavigationItems([]);
   70:     setActivePhysicalPageId("");
   71:   }, [pages, selectedDesign]);
   72: 
   73:   const handlePhysicalPagesChange = useCallback(
   74:     (items: ReportTwoPhysicalNavigationItem[]) => {
   75:       setPhysicalNavigationItems((current) => {
   76:         const currentKey = current
   77:           .map((item) => `${item.physicalPageId}:${item.label}`)
   78:           .join("|");
   79:         const nextKey = items
   80:           .map((item) => `${item.physicalPageId}:${item.label}`)
   81:           .join("|");
   82: 
   83:         return currentKey === nextKey ? current : items;
   84:       });
   85:       setActivePhysicalPageId((current) => {
   86:         const currentItem = items.find(
   87:           (item) => item.physicalPageId === current,
   88:         );
   89: 
   90:         if (currentItem?.sourcePageIds.includes(activePageId)) {
   91:           return current;
   92:         }
   93: 
   94:         return (
   95:           items.find((item) =>
   96:             item.sourcePageIds.includes(activePageId),
   97:           )?.physicalPageId || items[0]?.physicalPageId || ""
   98:         );
   99:       });
  100:     },
  101:     [activePageId],
  102:   );
  103: 
  104:   useEffect(() => {
  105:     if (!physicalNavigationItems.length) {
  106:       return;
  107:     }
  108: 
  109:     setActivePhysicalPageId((current) => {
  110:       const currentItem = physicalNavigationItems.find(
  111:         (item) => item.physicalPageId === current,
  112:       );
  113: 
  114:       if (currentItem?.sourcePageIds.includes(activePageId)) {
  115:         return current;
  116:       }
  117: 
  118:       return (
  119:         physicalNavigationItems.find((item) =>
  120:           item.sourcePageIds.includes(activePageId),
  121:         )?.physicalPageId || current
  122:       );
  123:     });
  124:   }, [activePageId, physicalNavigationItems]);
  125: 
  126:   const pageNavigationItems = useMemo<ReportTwoPhysicalNavigationItem[]>(
  127:     () =>
  128:       physicalNavigationItems.length > 0
  129:         ? physicalNavigationItems
  130:         : pages.map((page: any, index: number) => ({
  131:             physicalPageId: "",
  132:             corePhysicalPageId: "",
  133:             sourceLogicalPageId: page.id,
  134:             sourcePageIds: [page.id],
  135:             label: page.title,
  136:             physicalPageIndex: index,
  137:             physicalIndexWithinLogicalPage: 1,
  138:             role: "primary" as const,
  139:           })),
  140:     [pages, physicalNavigationItems],
  141:   );
  142: 
  143:   const logoStyle = <style>{logoStyleText}</style>;
  144:   const headerStyle = headerStyleText ? <style>{headerStyleText}</style> : null;
  145:   const signatureStyle = <style>{signatureStyleText}</style>;
  146: 
  147:   const controls = (
  148:     <>
  149:       <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
  150:         <div>
  151:           <h2 className="text-lg font-black text-slate-900">
  152:             المعاينة الرسمية A4
  153:           </h2>
  154:           <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
  155:             التصميم الحالي: {getDesignName(selectedDesign)}
  156:           </p>
  157:         </div>
  158: 
  159:         <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-black text-slate-600">
  160:           {previewCase ? "Case ID فعلي" : "بيانات تجريبية"}
  161:         </span>
  162:       </div>
  163: 
  164:       <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
  165:         {pageNavigationItems.map((navigationItem, index) => {
  166:           const page = pages.find(
  167:             (candidate: any) =>
  168:               candidate.id === navigationItem.sourceLogicalPageId,
  169:           );
  170:           const active = navigationItem.physicalPageId
  171:             ? navigationItem.physicalPageId === activePhysicalPageId
  172:             : navigationItem.sourceLogicalPageId === activePageId;
  173:           const isLogicalPageControl =
  174:             navigationItem.physicalIndexWithinLogicalPage === 1;
  175:           const canMovePrevious =
  176:             page && isLogicalPageControl
  177:               ? canMovePage?.(page.id, "previous") ??
  178:                 pages.findIndex((candidate: any) => candidate.id === page.id) > 0
  179:               : false;
  180:           const canMoveNext =
  181:             page && isLogicalPageControl
  182:               ? canMovePage?.(page.id, "next") ??
  183:                 pages.findIndex((candidate: any) => candidate.id === page.id) < pages.length - 1
  184:               : false;
  185:           const canDelete =
  186:             page && isLogicalPageControl
  187:               ? canDeletePage?.(page.id) ?? false
  188:               : false;
  189: 
  190:           return (
  191:             <div
  192:               key={`${navigationItem.physicalPageId || navigationItem.sourceLogicalPageId}-${index}`}
  193:               className={[
  194:                 "inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-2xl border px-2 py-1 text-xs font-black transition",
  195:                 active
  196:                   ? "border-emerald-600 bg-emerald-700 text-white shadow-sm"
  197:                   : "border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 hover:bg-emerald-50",
  198:               ].join(" ")}
  199:             >
  200:               <button
  201:                 type="button"
  202:                 onClick={() => {
  203:                   if (navigationItem.physicalPageId) {
  204:                     setActivePhysicalPageId(navigationItem.physicalPageId);
  205:                   }
  206: 
  207:                   if (navigationItem.sourceLogicalPageId !== activePageId) {
  208:                     onActivePageChange(navigationItem.sourceLogicalPageId);
  209:                   }
  210:                 }}
  211:                 className="max-w-[220px] truncate px-2 py-1"
  212:                 title={navigationItem.label}
  213:               >
  214:                 {navigationItem.label}
  215:               </button>
  216: 
  217:               {active && page && isLogicalPageControl ? (
  218:                 <>
  219:                   <button
  220:                     type="button"
  221:                     disabled={!canMovePrevious}
  222:                     onClick={(event) => {
  223:                       event.stopPropagation();
  224:                       onMovePage?.(page.id, "previous");
  225:                     }}
  226:                     className="inline-flex h-6 min-w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/15 px-1 text-[11px] font-black hover:bg-white/25 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-30"
  227:                     title="تحريك الصفحة للخلف"
  228:                   >
  229:                     ↑
  230:                   </button>
  231: 
  232:                   <button
  233:                     type="button"
  234:                     disabled={!canMoveNext}
  235:                     onClick={(event) => {
  236:                       event.stopPropagation();
  237:                       onMovePage?.(page.id, "next");
  238:                     }}
  239:                     className="inline-flex h-6 min-w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/15 px-1 text-[11px] font-black hover:bg-white/25 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-30"
  240:                     title="تحريك الصفحة للأمام"
  241:                   >
  242:                     ↓
  243:                   </button>
  244: 
  245:                   {canDelete ? (
  246:                     <button
  247:                       type="button"
  248:                       onClick={(event) => {
  249:                         event.stopPropagation();
  250:                         onDeletePage?.(page.id);
  251:                       }}
  252:                       className="inline-flex h-6 min-w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-red-500/90 px-1 text-[11px] font-black text-white hover:bg-red-600"
  253:                       title="حذف الصفحة"
  254:                     >
  255:                       ×
  256:                     </button>
  257:                   ) : null}
  258:                 </>
  259:               ) : null}
  260:             </div>
  261:           );
  262:         })}
  263: 
  264:         <button
  265:           type="button"
  266:           onClick={onAddPage}
  267:           className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-black text-emerald-800 transition hover:bg-emerald-100"
  268:         >
  269:           + صفحة محتوى
  270:         </button>
  271:       </div>
  272:     </>
  273:   );
  274: 
  275:   const preview = (
  276:     <div className="report-design-logo-control-style">
  277:       <SmartPhysicalReportComposer
  278:         designId={selectedDesign}
  279:         pages={pages}
  280:         activePageId={activePageId}
  281:         activePhysicalPageId={activePhysicalPageId}
  282:         context={context}
  283:         previewCase={previewCase}
  284:         fallbackPageLabel={activePage?.title || "التقرير"}
  285:         renderMode={renderMode}
  286:         suppressAutoEvidencePages={suppressAutoEvidencePages}
  287:         onPhysicalPagesChange={handlePhysicalPagesChange}
  288:       />
  289:     </div>
  290:   );
  291: 
  292:   if (chromeLayout === "none") {
  293:     return (
  294:       <>
  295:         {logoStyle}
  296:         {headerStyle}
  297:         {signatureStyle}
  298:         {preview}
  299:       </>
  300:     );
  301:   }
  302: 
  303:   if (chromeLayout === "split") {
  304:     return (
  305:       <div className="space-y-4">
  306:         {logoStyle}
  307:         {headerStyle}
  308:         {signatureStyle}
  309: 
  310:         <section className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
  311:           {controls}
  312:         </section>
  313: 
  314:         <section className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
  315:           {preview}
  316:         </section>
  317:       </div>
  318:     );
  319:   }
  320: 
  321:   return (
  322:     <section className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
  323:       {logoStyle}
  324:       {headerStyle}
  325:       {signatureStyle}
  326: 
  327:       <div className="report-design-logo-control-style">
  328:         <div className="mb-5">{controls}</div>
  329:         {preview}
  330:       </div>
  331:     </section>
  332:   );
  333: }
  334: export function FinalReportDesignRenderer({
  335:   template,
  336:   previewCaseData,
  337:   editorialBlocks = {},
  338:   identity = {},
  339: }: {
  340:   template: any;
  341:   previewCaseData: PreviewCaseData | null;
  342:   editorialBlocks?: Record<string, string>;
  343:   identity?: Record<string, any>;
  344: }) {
  345:   const selectedDesign = normalizeDesignId(
  346:     template?.designTemplateId || template?.designTheme || "ministry-form",
  347:   );
  348: 
  349:   const normalizedTemplate = normalizeFinalReportTemplate(
  350:     template,
  351:     editorialBlocks,
  352:     previewCaseData,
  353:   );
  354: 
  355:   const context = buildFinalReportContext(previewCaseData, identity);
  356:   const headerStyleText = getReportHeaderSettingsStyle(
  357:     template?.designConfig?.header,
  358:   );
  359:   const signatureStyleText = getReportDesignSignatureStyleText();
  360: 
  361:   const pages = normalizedTemplate.pages?.length
  362:     ? normalizedTemplate.pages
  363:     : [
  364:         {
  365:           id: "final-preview-fallback",
  366:           title: normalizedTemplate.name || "التقارير",
  367:           kind: "content",
  368:           blocks: [
  369:             {
  370:               id: "fallback-title",
  371:               kind: "hero-title",
  372:               title: "عنوان التقارير",
  373:               content: "{{case.title}}",
  374:               variant: "hero",
  375:               align: "center",
  376:               showTitle: false,
  377:               placement: "flow",
  378:             },
  379:             {
  380:               id: "fallback-fields",
  381:               kind: "dynamic-fields",
  382:               title: "بيانات الحالة",
  383:               content: "",
  384:               variant: "card",
  385:               align: "right",
  386:               showTitle: true,
  387:               placement: "flow",
  388:             },
  389:           ],
  390:         },
  391:       ];
  392: 
  393:   return (
  394:     <section className="space-y-4 bg-transparent print:space-y-0" dir="rtl">
  395:       {headerStyleText ? <style>{headerStyleText}</style> : null}
  396:       <style>{signatureStyleText}</style>
  397:       <SmartPhysicalReportComposer
  398:         designId={selectedDesign}
  399:         pages={pages}
  400:         context={context}
  401:         previewCase={previewCaseData}
  402:         fallbackPageLabel={normalizedTemplate.name || "التقارير"}
  403:         renderMode="stack"
  404:       />
  405:     </section>
  406:   );
  407: }

   52: export function PageBlocks({
   53:   page,
   54:   context,
   55:   previewCase,
   56:   designId,
   57:   className,
   58: }: {
   59:   page?: any;
   60:   context: Record<string, string>;
   61:   previewCase: PreviewCaseData | null;
   62:   designId: ReportDesignId;
   63:   className: string;
   64: }) {
   65:   const blocks = (page?.blocks || []).filter((block: any) => block.visible !== false);
   66:   const flowBlocks = blocks.filter(
   67:     (block: any) =>
   68:       isSignatureGridDesignBlock(block) || (block.placement || "flow") === "flow",
   69:   );
   70:   const fixedBlocks = blocks.filter(
   71:     (block: any) =>
   72:       !isSignatureGridDesignBlock(block) && (block.placement || "flow") !== "flow",
   73:   );
   74: 
   75:   return (
   76:     <main className={`relative flex flex-col ${className}`}>
   77:       <div className="flex min-h-[inherit] flex-1 flex-col gap-[var(--report-block-gap,1rem)]">
   78:         {flowBlocks.flatMap((block: any) => [
   79:           isSignatureGridDesignBlock(block) ? (
   80:             <div
   81:               key={`${block.id}-bottom-reservation`}
   82:               aria-hidden="true"
   83:               className="min-h-0 flex-1"
   84:               data-report-signature-bottom-reservation
   85:             />
   86:           ) : null,
   87:           <div
   88:             key={block.id}
   89:             data-report-smart-block={block.kind || "content"}
   90:             data-report-smart-placement={block.placement || "flow"}
   91:             data-report-smart-movable={
   92:               block.kind === "evidence-gallery" ? "evidence" : "content"
   93:             }
   94:             data-report-priority-block={
   95:               isSignatureGridDesignBlock(block) ? "signature" : undefined
   96:             }
   97:             className={
   98:               isSignatureGridDesignBlock(block)
   99:                 ? "break-inside-avoid pt-[var(--report-signature-top-gap,1.5rem)]"
  100:                 : ""
  101:             }
  102:             style={
  103:               isSignatureGridDesignBlock(block)
  104:                 ? { breakInside: "avoid", pageBreakInside: "avoid" }
  105:                 : undefined
  106:             }
  107:           >
  108:             <DesignBlock block={block} context={context} previewCase={previewCase} designId={designId} />
  109:           </div>,
  110:         ])}
  111:       </div>
  112: 
  113:       <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0">
  114:         {fixedBlocks.map((block: any) => (
  115:           <div
  116:             key={block.id}
  117:             data-report-smart-block={block.kind || "fixed-content"}
  118:             data-report-smart-placement={block.placement || "flow"}
  119:             data-report-smart-fixed="true"
  120:             className={getPlacementClass(block.placement || "flow")}
  121:           >
  122:             <div className="pointer-events-auto">
  123:               <DesignBlock block={block} context={context} previewCase={previewCase} designId={designId} />
  124:             </div>
  125:           </div>
  126:         ))}
  127:       </div>
  128:     </main>
  129:   );
  130: }

  134: function isDynamicFieldsDesignBlock(block: any) {
  135:   const kind = String(block?.kind || "").trim();
  136:   const smartKind = String(block?.settings?.smartBlockKind || "").trim();
  137:   const title = String(block?.title || "").trim();
  138: 
  139:   return (
  140:     kind === "dynamic-fields" ||
  141:     kind === "field-list" ||
  142:     kind === "case-meta" ||
  143:     kind === "student-summary" ||
  144:     kind === "service-summary" ||
  145:     smartKind === "dynamic-fields" ||
  146:     smartKind === "field-list" ||
  147:     title.includes("حقول") ||
  148:     title.includes("بيانات الحالة") ||
  149:     Array.isArray(block?.dynamicFields)
  150:   );
  151: }
  152: function getDynamicFieldCardsForBlock(block: any, previewCase: any) {
  153:   const configuredItems = Array.isArray(block?.dynamicFields)
  154:     ? block.dynamicFields
  155:     : [];
  156: 
  157:   if (configuredItems.length) {
  158:     return configuredItems
  159:       .map((item: any, index: number) => {
  160:         const id =
  161:           cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.id) ||
  162:           cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.key) ||
  163:           `dynamic-field-${index + 1}`;
  164:         const valueItems = Array.isArray(item.valueItems)
  165:           ? translateWorkflowDynamicValueItems(item.valueItems)
  166:           : translateWorkflowDynamicValueItems(item.value);
  167: 
  168:         return {
  169:           id,
  170:           key:
  171:             cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.key) ||
  172:             id,
  173:           label:
  174:             item.label !== undefined
  175:               ? cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.label)
  176:               : `حقل ${index + 1}`,
  177:           value:
  178:             item.value !== undefined
  179:               ? cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.value)
  180:               : "",
  181:           valueItems,
  182:           visible: item.visible !== false,
  183:         };
  184:       })
  185:       .filter((item: any) => item.visible && item.label)
  186:       .filter(
  187:         (item: any) =>
  188:           !previewCase?.hasStudentDataTable || !isStudentIdentityField(item),
  189:       );
  190:   }
  191: 
  192:   return getWorkflowDynamicFieldCards(previewCase)
  193:     .map((item: any, index: number) => {
  194:       const id =
  195:         cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.key) ||
  196:         cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.label) ||
  197:         `workflow-field-${index + 1}`;
  198: 
  199:       return {
  200:         id,
  201:         key: id,
  202:         label: cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.label),
  203:         value: cleanWorkflowDynamicText(item.value),
  204:         valueItems: Array.isArray(item.valueItems)
  205:           ? item.valueItems.map((valueItem: unknown) => cleanWorkflowDynamicText(valueItem)).filter(Boolean)
  206:           : [],
  207:         visible: true,
  208:       };
  209:     })
  210:     .filter((item: any) => item.visible && item.label && item.value)
  211:     .filter(
  212:       (item: any) =>
  213:         !previewCase?.hasStudentDataTable || !isStudentIdentityField(item),
  214:     );
  215: }
  216: function isSignatureGridDesignBlock(block: any) {
  217:   const kind = String(block?.kind || "").trim();
  218:   const smartKind = String(block?.settings?.smartBlockKind || "").trim();
  219:   const title = String(block?.title || "").trim();
  220: 
  221:   return (
  222:     kind === "signature-grid" ||
  223:     kind === "signatures" ||
  224:     kind === "approval-signatures" ||
  225:     smartKind === "signature-grid" ||
  226:     smartKind === "signatures" ||
  227:     title.includes("توقيع") ||
  228:     title.includes("اعتماد") ||
  229:     Array.isArray(block?.signatures)
  230:   );

  232: 
  233: function getDesignSignatureCards(block: any) {
  234:   const signatures = Array.isArray(block?.signatures) ? block.signatures : [];
  235: 
  236:   return signatures
  237:     .map((signature: any, index: number) => ({
  238:       key: String(signature?.key || `signature-${index + 1}`),
  239:       label: String(signature?.label || "التوقيع"),
  240:       signerName: String(signature?.signerName || ""),
  241:       signerTitle: String(signature?.signerTitle || ""),
  242:       imageUrl: String(signature?.imageUrl || ""),
  243:       required: Boolean(signature?.required),
  244:     }))
  245:     .filter((signature: any) => signature.label || signature.signerName || signature.imageUrl);
  246: }
  247: function DesignBlock({
  248:   block,
  249:   context,
  250:   previewCase,
  251:   designId,
  252: }: {
  253:   block: any;
  254:   context: Record<string, string>;
  255:   previewCase: PreviewCaseData | null;
  256:   designId: ReportDesignId;
  257: }) {
  258:   if (block.hideWhenMissing && block.boundFieldKey) {
  259:     const value = String(context[`field.${block.boundFieldKey}`] || "").trim();
  260:     if (!value) return null;
  261:   }
  262: 
  263:   const rendered = renderText(block.content || "", context);
  264:   const textAlign = block.align === "center" ? "text-center" : "text-right";
  265:   const implementation = getReportDesignImplementation(designId);
  266:   const accent = implementation.palette;
  267:   const BlockRenderer = implementation.BlockRenderer;
  268: 
  269:   if (BlockRenderer) {
  270:     return (
  271:       <BlockRenderer
  272:         block={block}
  273:         renderedContent={rendered}
  274:         textAlign={textAlign}
  275:         context={context}
  276:         splitLines={splitLines}
  277:         splitParagraphs={splitParagraphs}
  278:       />
  279:     );
  280:   }
  281: 
  282:   if (block.kind === "hero-title") {
  283:     return (
  284:       <section className={getBlockShellClass(implementation, "hero", "text-center")}>
  285:         <p
  286:           className={["text-sm font-black", accent.subtleTextClass].join(" ")}
  287:           style={{
  288:             fontSize: "calc(0.875rem * var(--report-content-font-scale, 1))",
  289:             lineHeight: "var(--report-content-line-height, 1.75)",
  290:           }}
  291:         >
  292:           {context["service.name"]}
  293:         </p>
  294:         <h1
  295:           className="mx-auto max-w-[145mm] font-black text-slate-950"
  296:           style={{
  297:             marginTop: "var(--report-heading-gap, 0.75rem)",
  298:             fontSize: `calc(1.875rem * var(--report-content-font-scale, 1) * ${getReportFontSizeMultiplier(getBlockSetting(block, "titleFontSize"))})`,
  299:             lineHeight: "var(--report-heading-line-height, 1.65)",
  300:           }}
  301:         >
  302:           {rendered}
  303:         </h1>
  304:       </section>
  305:     );
  306:   }
  307: 
  308:   if (block.kind === "meta-strip") {
  309:     return (
  310:       <section className={getBlockShellClass(implementation, "soft", textAlign)}>
  311:         <div
  312:           className="grid md:grid-cols-2"
  313:           style={{
  314:             gap: "var(--report-value-grid-gap, 0.5rem)",
  315:           }}
  316:         >
  317:           {splitLines(rendered).map((line) => (
  318:             <div
  319:               key={line}
  320:               className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white text-sm font-bold text-slate-700"
  321:               style={{
  322:                 paddingInline: "calc(1rem * var(--report-field-spacing-scale, 1))",
  323:                 paddingBlock: "calc(0.75rem * var(--report-field-spacing-scale, 1))",
  324:               }}
  325:             >
  326:               {line}
  327:             </div>
  328:           ))}
  329:         </div>
  330:       </section>
  331:     );
  332:   }
  333: 
  334:   if (block.kind === "bullet-list") {
  335:     const customBulletList = implementation.renderBulletList?.({
  336:       block,
  337:       renderedContent: rendered,
  338:       textAlign,
  339:       context,
  340:       splitLines,
  341:       splitParagraphs,
  342:     });
  343: 
  344:     if (customBulletList !== undefined) return customBulletList;
  345: 
  346:     const contentFontSizeClass = getReportFontSizeClass(
  347:       getBlockSetting(block, "contentFontSize"),
  348:       "text-sm",
  349:     );
  350: 
  351:     return (
  352:       <section className={getBlockShellClass(implementation, block.variant, textAlign)}>
  353:         {block.showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={block.title} fontSize={getBlockSetting(block, "titleFontSize")} /> : null}
  354:         <ul
  355:           style={{
  356:             display: "grid",
  357:             gap: "var(--report-bullet-gap, 0.5rem)",
  358:           }}
  359:         >
  360:           {splitLines(rendered).map((line) => (
  361:             <li
  362:               key={line}
  363:               className={["flex text-slate-700", contentFontSizeClass].join(" ")}
  364:               style={{
  365:                 gap: "var(--report-field-gap, 0.5rem)",
  366:                 lineHeight: "var(--report-bullet-line-height, 1.75)",
  367:                 fontSize: "calc(0.875rem * var(--report-bullet-font-scale, 1))",
  368:               }}
  369:             >
  370:               <span className={["mt-2 h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full", accent.dotClass].join(" ")} />
  371:               <span>{line}</span>
  372:             </li>
  373:           ))}
  374:         </ul>
  375:       </section>
  376:     );
  377:   }
  378:   if (block.kind === "multi-paragraph") {
  379:     const customNarrative = implementation.renderNarrative?.({
  380:       block,
  381:       renderedContent: rendered,
  382:       textAlign,
  383:       context,
  384:       splitLines,
  385:       splitParagraphs,
  386:     });
  387: 
  388:     if (customNarrative !== undefined) return customNarrative;
  389: 
  390:     return (
  391:       <section className={getBlockShellClass(implementation, block.variant, textAlign)}>
  392:         {block.showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={block.title} fontSize={getBlockSetting(block, "titleFontSize")} /> : null}
  393:         <div
  394:           style={{
  395:             display: "grid",
  396:             gap: "var(--report-paragraph-gap, 0.75rem)",
  397:           }}
  398:         >
  399:           {splitParagraphs(rendered).map((paragraph) => (
  400:             <p
  401:               key={paragraph}
  402:               className={["text-slate-700", getReportFontSizeClass(getBlockSetting(block, "contentFontSize"), "text-base")].join(" ")}
  403:               style={{
  404:                 margin: 0,
  405:                 lineHeight: "var(--report-narrative-line-height, 2)",
  406:                 fontSize: `calc(1rem * var(--report-content-font-scale, 1) * ${getReportFontSizeMultiplier(getBlockSetting(block, "contentFontSize"))})`,
  407:               }}
  408:             >
  409:               {paragraph}
  410:             </p>
  411:           ))}
  412:         </div>
  413:       </section>
  414:     );
  415:   }
  416:   if (block.kind === "report-one-table" || block.kind === "structured-table") {
  417:     const tableBlock = block.kind === "structured-table"
  418:       ? normalizeStructuredTableBlockPresentation(block)
  419:       : block;
  420:     const columns = tableBlock.kind === "structured-table"
  421:       ? (Array.isArray(tableBlock.columns) ? tableBlock.columns : [])
  422:       : Array.isArray(tableBlock.columns) && tableBlock.columns.length
  423:         ? tableBlock.columns
  424:         : ["المجال", "الإجراء", "ملاحظات"];
  425: 
  426:     const rows = tableBlock.kind === "structured-table"
  427:       ? (Array.isArray(tableBlock.rows) ? tableBlock.rows : [])
  428:       : Array.isArray(tableBlock.rows) && tableBlock.rows.length
  429:         ? tableBlock.rows
  430:         : [["", "", ""]];
  431: 
  432:     const tableSettings = tableBlock.tableSettings || {};
  433:     const compact = Boolean(tableSettings.compact);
  434:     const rounded = tableSettings.rounded !== false;
  435:     const highlightHeader = tableSettings.highlightHeader !== false;
  436:     const highlightFirstColumn = Boolean(tableSettings.highlightFirstColumn);
  437:     const stripedRows = Boolean(tableSettings.stripedRows);
  438:     const colorTheme = tableSettings.colorTheme || "light-gray";
  439:     const columnWidths = Array.isArray(tableBlock.columnWidths) ? tableBlock.columnWidths : [];
  440: 
  441:     const themeColors: Record<string, { border: string; headerBg: string; firstColBg: string; stripedBg: string; headerText: string; cellText: string; firstColText: string }> = {
  442:       "light-gray": { border: "border-slate-200", headerBg: "bg-slate-50", firstColBg: "bg-slate-50", stripedBg: "bg-slate-50/40", headerText: "text-slate-800", cellText: "text-slate-600", firstColText: "text-slate-900" },
  443:       "soft-blue": { border: "border-sky-200", headerBg: "bg-sky-50", firstColBg: "bg-sky-50", stripedBg: "bg-sky-50/40", headerText: "text-sky-900", cellText: "text-slate-700", firstColText: "text-sky-900" },
  444:       "green": { border: "border-emerald-200", headerBg: "bg-emerald-50", firstColBg: "bg-emerald-50", stripedBg: "bg-emerald-50/40", headerText: "text-emerald-900", cellText: "text-slate-700", firstColText: "text-emerald-900" },
  445:       "none": { border: "border-slate-200", headerBg: "bg-white", firstColBg: "bg-white", stripedBg: "bg-white", headerText: "text-slate-800", cellText: "text-slate-600", firstColText: "text-slate-900" },
  446:     };
  447:     const tc = themeColors[colorTheme] || themeColors["light-gray"];
  448:     return (
  449:       <section
  450:         dir="rtl"
  451:         data-report-structured-table={tableBlock.kind === "structured-table" ? tableBlock.sourceTableId || "true" : undefined}
  452:         className={
  453:           tableBlock.kind === "structured-table"
  454:             ? `mb-3 mt-5 break-inside-avoid ${textAlign}`
  455:             : getBlockShellClass(implementation, tableBlock.variant, textAlign)
  456:         }
  457:         style={{ breakInside: "avoid" }}
  458:       >
  459:         {tableBlock.showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={tableBlock.title} fontSize={getBlockSetting(tableBlock, "titleFontSize")} /> : null}
  460: 
  461:         <div className={rounded ? `overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border ${tc.border}` : `overflow-hidden border ${tc.border}`}>
  462:           <table className="w-full table-fixed border-collapse text-xs">
  463:             <thead
  464:               className={highlightHeader ? tc.headerBg : "bg-white"}
  465:               style={{ display: tableSettings.repeatHeader === false ? undefined : "table-header-group" }}
  466:             >
  467:               <tr>
  468:                 {columns.map((column: string, columnIndex: number) => (
  469:                   <th
  470:                     key={`${column}-${columnIndex}`}
  471:                     className={[
  472:                       `border ${tc.border} text-center font-black ${tc.headerText}`,
  473:                     ].join(" ")}
  474:                     style={{
  475:                       ...(Number(columnWidths[columnIndex]) > 0
  476:                         ? { width: `${Number(columnWidths[columnIndex])}%` }
  477:                         : {}),
  478:                       paddingInline: `calc(var(--report-table-padding-x, 0.75rem) * ${compact ? 0.78 : 1})`,
  479:                       paddingBlock: `calc(var(--report-table-header-padding-y, 0.75rem) * ${compact ? 0.72 : 1})`,
  480:                       fontSize: "calc(0.75rem * var(--report-table-font-scale, 1))",
  481:                       lineHeight: "calc(1.25rem * var(--report-narrative-density-scale, 1))",
  482:                     }}
  483:                   >
  484:                     {column}
  485:                   </th>
  486:                 ))}
  487:               </tr>
  488:             </thead>
  489: 
  490:             <tbody>
  491:               {rows.map((row: string[], rowIndex: number) => (
  492:                 <tr
  493:                   key={`row-${rowIndex}`}
  494:                   className={stripedRows && rowIndex % 2 === 1 ? tc.stripedBg : "bg-white"}
  495:                   style={{ breakInside: "avoid", pageBreakInside: "avoid" }}
  496:                 >
  497:                   {columns.map((_: string, columnIndex: number) => (
  498:                     <td
  499:                       key={`cell-${rowIndex}-${columnIndex}`}
  500:                       className={[
  501:                         `break-words whitespace-pre-wrap border ${tc.border} text-center font-bold ${tc.cellText}`,
  502:                         highlightFirstColumn && columnIndex === 0
  503:                           ? `${tc.firstColBg} font-black ${tc.firstColText}`
  504:                           : "",
  505:                       ].join(" ")}
  506:                       style={{
  507:                         paddingInline: `calc(var(--report-table-padding-x, 0.75rem) * ${compact ? 0.78 : 1})`,
  508:                         paddingBlock: `calc(var(--report-table-padding-y, 0.75rem) * ${compact ? 0.72 : 1})`,
  509:                         fontSize: "calc(0.75rem * var(--report-table-font-scale, 1))",
  510:                         lineHeight: "calc(1.5rem * var(--report-narrative-density-scale, 1))",
  511:                       }}
  512:                     >
  513:                       {String(row?.[columnIndex] || "—")}
  514:                     </td>
  515:                   ))}
  516:                 </tr>
  517:               ))}
  518:             </tbody>
  519:           </table>
  520:         </div>
  521:       </section>
  522:     );
  523:   }
  524:   if (isDynamicFieldsDesignBlock(block)) {
  525:     const dynamicFieldItems = getDynamicFieldCardsForBlock(block, previewCase);
  526: 
  527:     if (!dynamicFieldItems.length && previewCase?.hasStudentDataTable) {
  528:       return null;
  529:     }
  530: 
  531:     return (
  532:       <section
  533:         className={getBlockShellClass(implementation, block.variant, textAlign)}
  534:         data-report-dynamic-fields
  535:       >
  536:         {block.showTitle ? (
  537:           <BlockTitle title={block.title} fontSize={getBlockSetting(block, "titleFontSize")} />
  538:         ) : null}
  539: 
  540:         {dynamicFieldItems.length ? (
  541:           <DesignValueGrid
  542:             implementation={implementation}
  543:             block={block}
  544:             items={dynamicFieldItems.map(({ id, label, value, valueItems }: any) => ({
  545:               key: id,
  546:               label,
  547:               value: Array.isArray(valueItems) && valueItems.length ? valueItems : value,
  548:               valueItems,
  549:             }))}
  550:           />
  551:         ) : (
  552:           <p
  553:             className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 text-sm font-bold text-slate-500"
  554:             style={{
  555:               paddingInline: "calc(1rem * var(--report-field-spacing-scale, 1))",
  556:               paddingBlock: "calc(0.75rem * var(--report-field-spacing-scale, 1))",
  557:               fontSize: "calc(0.875rem * var(--report-field-value-scale, 1))",
  558:             }}
  559:           >
  560:             لا توجد حقول ظاهرة داخل هذا البلوك.
  561:           </p>
  562:         )}
  563:       </section>
  564:     );
  565:   }
  566:   if (block.kind === "field-list") {
  567:     const values = getFinalFieldListItems(block, previewCase);
  568: 
  569:     return (
  570:       <section className={getBlockShellClass(implementation, block.variant, textAlign)}>
  571:         {block.showTitle ? (
  572:           <BlockTitle title={block.title} fontSize={getBlockSetting(block, "titleFontSize")} />
  573:         ) : null}
  574: 
  575:         {values.length ? (
  576:           <DesignValueGrid
  577:             implementation={implementation}
  578:             block={block}
  579:             items={values.map((item: any) => ({
  580:               key: item.key || item.label || item.fieldKey || item.fieldLabel,
  581:                 label: item.label || item.fieldLabel || "تفصيل",
  582:               value: Array.isArray(item.valueItems) && item.valueItems.length
  583:                 ? item.valueItems
  584:                 : item.value || "غير متوفر",
  585:               valueItems: item.valueItems,
  586:             }))}
  587:           />
  588:         ) : (
  589:           <p
  590:             className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 text-sm font-bold text-slate-500"
  591:             style={{
  592:               paddingInline: "calc(1rem * var(--report-field-spacing-scale, 1))",
  593:               paddingBlock: "calc(0.75rem * var(--report-field-spacing-scale, 1))",
  594:               fontSize: "calc(0.875rem * var(--report-field-value-scale, 1))",
  595:             }}
  596:           >
  597:             لا توجد قيم مرتبطة بهذا البلوك.
  598:           </p>
  599:         )}
  600:       </section>
  601:     );
  602:   }
  603:   if (isSignatureGridDesignBlock(block)) {
  604:     const signatures = getDesignSignatureCards(block);
  605: 
  606:     if (!signatures.length) return null;
  607: 
  608:     const SignatureRenderer = implementation.SignatureRenderer;
  609:     if (SignatureRenderer) {
  610:       return (
  611:         <SignatureRenderer
  612:           block={block}
  613:           items={signatures}
  614:           renderTitle={() => block.showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={block.title || "تواقيع الاعتماد"} fontSize={getBlockSetting(block, "titleFontSize")} /> : null}
  615:         />
  616:       );
  617:     }
  618: 
  619:     return (
  620:       <section
  621:         data-report-design-signature-block
  622:         className="report-design-signature-grid-block break-inside-avoid"
  623:         style={{
  624:           breakInside: "avoid",
  625:           pageBreakInside: "avoid",
  626:         }}
  627:       >
  628:         {block.showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={block.title || "تواقيع الاعتماد"} fontSize={getBlockSetting(block, "titleFontSize")} /> : null}
  629: 
  630:         <div
  631:           className={[
  632:             signatures.length === 2
  633:               ? "mx-auto grid w-[72%] max-w-[132mm] grid-cols-2 items-end gap-x-[8mm]"
  634:               : "flex w-full items-end gap-6",
  635:             signatures.length === 1
  636:               ? "justify-center"
  637:               : signatures.length >= 3
  638:                 ? "justify-between"
  639:                 : "",
  640:           ].join(" ")}
  641:         >
  642:           {signatures.map((signature: any) => (
  643:             <div
  644:               key={signature.key}
  645:               className={[
  646:                 "text-center",
  647:                 signatures.length >= 3
  648:                   ? "w-[31%]"
  649:                   : signatures.length === 2
  650:                     ? "min-w-0 w-full"
  651:                     : "w-[58mm]",
  652:               ].join(" ")}
  653:             >
  654:               <div
  655:                 className="report-design-signature-image-frame flex items-end justify-center rounded-md bg-white"
  656:                 style={{ height: "var(--report-signature-image-height, 10mm)" }}
  657:               >
  658:                 {signature.imageUrl ? (
  659:                   <img
  660:                     src={signature.imageUrl}
  661:                     alt={signature.label}
  662:                     className="report-design-signature-image max-w-[42mm] object-contain"
  663:                     style={{
  664:                       maxHeight: "var(--report-signature-image-height, 10mm)",
  665:                       background: "#ffffff",
  666:                       objectFit: "contain",
  667:                       mixBlendMode: "normal",
  668:                       filter: "none",
  669:                     }}
  670:                   />
  671:                 ) : (
  672:                   <div className="mb-1 w-full border-b border-dashed border-slate-400" />
  673:                 )}
  674:               </div>
  675: 
  676:               <div
  677:                 className="report-design-signature-name font-black text-slate-950"
  678:                 style={{
  679:                   marginTop: "var(--report-field-value-top-gap, 0.25rem)",
  680:                   fontSize: "12px",
  681:                   lineHeight: "1.25",
  682:                 }}
  683:               >
  684:                 {signature.signerName || "—"}
  685:               </div>
  686: 
  687:               {signature.signerTitle ? (
  688:                 <div
  689:                   className="report-design-signature-role font-bold text-slate-500"

  692:                     fontSize: "10px",
  693:                     lineHeight: "1.25",
  694:                   }}
  695:                 >
  696:                   {signature.signerTitle}
  697:                 </div>
  698:               ) : null}
  699:             </div>
  700:           ))}
  701:         </div>
  702:       </section>
  703:     );
  704:   }
  705:   if (block.kind === "evidence-gallery") {
  706:     return <EvidenceBlock block={block} previewCase={previewCase} implementation={implementation} textAlign={textAlign} />;
  707:   }
  708: 
  709:   if (block.kind === "closing-note") {
  710:     return (
  711:       <section className={getBlockShellClass(implementation, block.variant, textAlign)}>
  712:         {block.showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={block.title} fontSize={getBlockSetting(block, "titleFontSize")} /> : null}
  713:         <p
  714:           className={["text-slate-700", getReportFontSizeClass(getBlockSetting(block, "contentFontSize"), "text-base")].join(" ")}
  715:           style={{
  716:             margin: 0,
  717:             fontSize: `calc(1rem * var(--report-content-font-scale, 1) * ${getReportFontSizeMultiplier(getBlockSetting(block, "contentFontSize"))})`,
  718:             lineHeight: "var(--report-narrative-line-height, 2)",
  719:           }}
  720:         >
  721:           {rendered}
  722:         </p>
  723:       </section>
  724:     );
  725:   }
  726: 
  727:   return (
  728:     <section className={getBlockShellClass(implementation, block.variant, textAlign)}>
  729:       {block.showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={block.title} fontSize={getBlockSetting(block, "titleFontSize")} /> : null}
  730:       <p
  731:         className={["whitespace-pre-line text-slate-700", getReportFontSizeClass(getBlockSetting(block, "contentFontSize"), "text-lg")].join(" ")}
  732:         style={{
  733:           margin: 0,
  734:           fontSize: `calc(1.125rem * var(--report-content-font-scale, 1) * ${getReportFontSizeMultiplier(getBlockSetting(block, "contentFontSize"))})`,
  735:           lineHeight: "var(--report-narrative-line-height, 2)",
  736:         }}
  737:       >
  738:         {rendered}
  739:       </p>
  740:     </section>
  741:   );
  742: }

  744: function DesignFieldValueBlock({
  745:   title,
  746:   showTitle,
  747:   variant,
  748:   textAlign,
  749:   designId,
  750:   items,
  751: }: {
  752:   title: string;
  753:   showTitle?: boolean;
  754:   variant: string;
  755:   textAlign: string;
  756:   designId: ReportDesignId;
  757:   items: Array<{
  758:     key?: string;
  759:     label: string;
  760:     value?: string | null;
  761:   }>;
  762: }) {
  763:   const visibleItems = items.filter((item) => String(item.value || "").trim());
  764:   const implementation = getReportDesignImplementation(designId);
  765: 
  766:   return (
  767:     <section className={getBlockShellClass(implementation, variant, textAlign)}>
  768:       {showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={title} /> : null}
  769: 
  770:       {visibleItems.length ? (
  771:         <DesignValueGrid implementation={implementation} items={visibleItems} />
  772:       ) : (
  773:         <p className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-slate-500">
  774:           لا توجد قيم مرتبطة بهذا البلوك.
  775:         </p>
  776:       )}
  777:     </section>
  778:   );
  779: }

    9:   value,

   30:     : [];

   40:           getReportFontSizeClass(
   41:             labelFontSize,

   45:         ].join(" ")}

   49:             "var(--report-content-line-height, 1.55)",
   50:         }}
   51:       >
   52:         {label}
   53:       </p>

   69:               "var(--report-field-value-item-gap, 0.4rem)",
   70:             fontSize: `calc(0.75rem * var(--report-field-value-scale, 1) * ${getReportFontSizeMultiplier(valueFontSize)})`,
   71:             lineHeight:
   72:               "var(--report-content-line-height, 1.75)",
   73:           }}
   74:         >
   75:           {items.map(
   76:             (item, index) => (
   77:               <li
   78:                 key={`${label}-${item}-${index}`}
   79:                 className="report-smart-meta-list-item flex items-start"
   80:                 style={{
   81:                   gap:
   82:                     "var(--report-field-gap, 0.5rem)",
   83:                 }}
   84:               >
   85:                 <span className="report-smart-meta-bullet mt-[0.55em] h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-slate-400" />
   86:                 <span>{item}</span>
   87:               </li>
   88:             ),
   89:           )}
   90:         </ul>
   91:       ) : (
   92:         <p
   93:           className={[
   94:             "report-smart-meta-value",
   95:             getReportFontSizeClass(
   96:               valueFontSize,
   97:               "text-xs",
   98:             ),
   99:             "font-black text-slate-800",
  100:           ].join(" ")}
  101:           style={{

  103:             lineHeight:

  110:     </div>
  111:   );

  240:       }}
  241:     >
  242:       {title}
  243:     </h3>
  244:   );

    8: export function EvidenceBlock({
    9:   block,
   10:   previewCase,
   11:   implementation,
   12:   textAlign,
   13: }: {
   14:   block: any;
   15:   previewCase: PreviewCaseData | null;
   16:   implementation: ReportDesignImplementation;
   17:   textAlign: string;
   18: }) {
   19:   const hasSelectedCase = Boolean(previewCase?.caseId);
   20:   const realEvidences = getValidPreviewEvidences(previewCase);
   21:   const perPage = getEvidencePerPage(block);
   22:   const startIndex = block.evidenceStartIndex || 0;
   23:   const accent = implementation.palette;
   24: 
   25:   if (!realEvidences.length && hasSelectedCase) return null;
   26:   if (!realEvidences.length && block.evidenceEmptyBehavior === "hide") return null;
   27: 
   28:   const placeholderEvidences = createEvidencePlaceholders(perPage, startIndex);
   29:   const sourceEvidences = realEvidences.length ? realEvidences : placeholderEvidences;
   30:   const visibleEvidences = sourceEvidences.slice(startIndex, startIndex + perPage);
   31:   const isPlaceholderMode = !realEvidences.length;
   32:   const shellClassName = getBlockShellClass(implementation, block.variant, textAlign);
   33:   const EvidenceRenderer = implementation.EvidenceRenderer;
   34: 
   35:   if (EvidenceRenderer) {
   36:     return (
   37:       <EvidenceRenderer
   38:         block={block}
   39:         items={visibleEvidences as ReportEvidenceItem[]}
   40:         startIndex={startIndex}
   41:         placeholderMode={isPlaceholderMode}
   42:         textAlign={textAlign}
   43:         shellClassName={shellClassName}
   44:         gridClassName={getEvidenceGridClass(block)}
   45:         gridStyle={getEvidenceGridStyle(block)}
   46:         getImageUrl={getReportDesignEvidenceImageUrl}

   48:         getImageStyle={() => getEvidenceImageStyle(block)}
   49:         getImageClassName={() => getEvidenceImageClass(block)}
   50:         getImageHeightClassName={() => getEvidenceImageHeightClass(block)}
   51:         renderTitle={() => block.showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={block.title} fontSize={getBlockSetting(block, "titleFontSize")} /> : null}
   52:       />
   53:     );
   54:   }
   55: 
   56:   if (block.evidenceLayout === "ATTACHMENT_LIST") {
   57:     return (
   58:       <section className={shellClassName}>
   59:         {block.showTitle ? <BlockTitle title={block.title} fontSize={getBlockSetting(block, "titleFontSize")} /> : null}
   60:         <div
   61:           style={{
   62:             display: "grid",
   63:             gap: "var(--report-evidence-gap, 0.5rem)",
   64:           }}
   65:         >
   66:           {visibleEvidences.map((evidence, index) => (
   67:             <div
   68:               key={evidence.id || String(index)}
   69:               className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white text-sm font-bold text-slate-700"
   70:               style={{
   71:                 gap: "var(--report-field-gap, 0.75rem)",
   72:                 paddingInline: "calc(1rem * var(--report-field-spacing-scale, 1))",
   73:                 paddingBlock: "calc(0.75rem * var(--report-field-spacing-scale, 1))",
   74:                 fontSize: "calc(0.875rem * var(--report-content-font-scale, 1))",
   75:               }}
   76:             >
   77:               <span>{evidence.caption || evidence.title || `مرفق ${startIndex + index + 1}`}</span>
   78:               <span className={["rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-black", accent.badgeClass].join(" ")}>
   79:                 {isPlaceholderMode ? "معاينة" : "شاهد"}

   81:             </div>

   83:         </div>
   84:       </section>
   85:     );
   86:   }

   97:

  100:             gap: `calc(${baseGap} * var(--report-evidence-spacing-scale, 1))`,

  105:           const imageUrl = getReportDesignEvidenceImageUrl(evidence);
  106:

  108:             <figure
  109:               key={evidence.id || imageUrl || String(index)}

  112:             >

FILE: components\report-engine\design-renderers\shared\report-auto-evidence.tsx
============================================================
    1: import type { ReportDesignId } from "../report-design-types";
    2: import type { ReportPhysicalPageModel } from "../smart-layout/report-smart-physical-types";
    3: import { A4DesignPage } from "./report-blocks";
    4: import {
    5:   getEvidencePerPage,
    6:   getEvidenceStartIndex,
    7:   getValidPreviewEvidences,
    8: } from "./report-evidence-data";
    9: import type { PreviewCaseData } from "./report-types";
   10: 
   11: export type AutoEvidencePhysicalPage = {
   12:   page: ReportPhysicalPageModel;
   13:   pageLabel: string;
   14: };
   15: 
   16: export function buildAutoEvidencePhysicalPages({
   17:   activePage,
   18:   previewCase,
   19:   primaryEvidenceCount,
   20: }: {
   21:   activePage?: ReportPhysicalPageModel | null;
   22:   previewCase: PreviewCaseData | null;
   23:   primaryEvidenceCount?: number;
   24: }): AutoEvidencePhysicalPage[] {
   25:   const evidenceBlock = activePage?.blocks?.find(
   26:     (block) => block?.kind === "evidence-gallery",
   27:   );
   28: 
   29:   if (
   30:     !activePage ||
   31:     !evidenceBlock ||
   32:     evidenceBlock.evidenceAutoCreatePages === false
   33:   ) {
   34:     return [];
   35:   }
   36: 
   37:   const evidences = getValidPreviewEvidences(previewCase);
   38: 
   39:   if (!evidences.length) {
   40:     return [];
   41:   }
   42: 
   43:   const perPage = getEvidencePerPage(evidenceBlock);
   44:   const initialStartIndex = getEvidenceStartIndex(evidenceBlock);
   45:   const availableEvidenceCount = Math.max(
   46:     0,
   47:     evidences.length - initialStartIndex,
   48:   );
   49:   const legacyPrimaryCount = Math.min(availableEvidenceCount, perPage);
   50:   const consumedByPrimary = Number.isFinite(Number(primaryEvidenceCount))
   51:     ? Math.min(
   52:         availableEvidenceCount,
   53:         Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(primaryEvidenceCount))),
   54:       )
   55:     : legacyPrimaryCount;
   56:   const remainingCount = Math.max(
   57:     0,
   58:     availableEvidenceCount - consumedByPrimary,
   59:   );
   60: 
   61:   if (remainingCount <= 0) {
   62:     return [];
   63:   }
   64: 
   65:   const extraPagesCount = Math.ceil(remainingCount / perPage);
   66: 
   67:   return Array.from({ length: extraPagesCount }).map((_, index) => {
   68:     const pageNumber = index + 2;
   69:     const startIndex =
   70:       initialStartIndex + consumedByPrimary + index * perPage;
   71:     const pageEvidenceLimit = Math.min(
   72:       perPage,
   73:       evidences.length - startIndex,
   74:     );
   75:     const page: ReportPhysicalPageModel = {
   76:       ...activePage,
   77:       id: `${activePage.id}-evidence-${startIndex}`,
   78:       title: `${activePage.title} - صفحة ${pageNumber}`,
   79:       kind: "evidence",
   80:       corePhysicalPageId: activePage.corePhysicalPageId || activePage.id,
   81:       physicalPageRole: "evidence",
   82:       sourcePageIds: [...activePage.sourcePageIds],
   83:       blocks: [
   84:         {
   85:           ...evidenceBlock,
   86:           id: `${evidenceBlock.id}-auto-${startIndex}`,
   87:           title:
   88:             index === 0 && consumedByPrimary === 0
   89:               ? evidenceBlock.title
   90:               : `${evidenceBlock.title} - صفحة ${pageNumber}`,
   91:           evidenceStartIndex: startIndex,
   92:           evidenceLimit: pageEvidenceLimit,
   93:           evidenceAutoCreatePages: false,
   94:         },
   95:       ],
   96:     };
   97: 
   98:     return {
   99:       page,
  100:       pageLabel: `صفحة شواهد ${pageNumber}`,
  101:     };
  102:   });
  103: }
  104: 
  105: export function AutoEvidencePages({
  106:   designId,
  107:   activePage,
  108:   context,
  109:   previewCase,
  110:   primaryEvidenceCount,
  111:   preparedPages,
  112:   activePhysicalPageId,
  113:   renderMode = "stack",
  114: }: {
  115:   designId: ReportDesignId;
  116:   activePage?: ReportPhysicalPageModel;
  117:   context: Record<string, string>;
  118:   previewCase: PreviewCaseData | null;
  119:   primaryEvidenceCount?: number;
  120:   preparedPages?: AutoEvidencePhysicalPage[];
  121:   activePhysicalPageId?: string;
  122:   renderMode?: "single" | "stack";
  123: }) {
  124:   const pages = preparedPages || buildAutoEvidencePhysicalPages({
  125:     activePage,
  126:     previewCase,
  127:     primaryEvidenceCount,
  128:   });
  129:   const visiblePages = renderMode === "stack"
  130:     ? pages
  131:     : pages.filter(({ page }) => page.id === activePhysicalPageId);
  132: 
  133:   if (!visiblePages.length) {
  134:     return null;
  135:   }
  136: 
  137:   return (
  138:     <div
  139:       className={
  140:         renderMode === "stack"
  141:           ? "mt-6 space-y-6 print:mt-0 print:space-y-0"
  142:           : ""
  143:       }
  144:       data-report-auto-evidence-pages
  145:     >
  146:       {visiblePages.map(({ page, pageLabel }) => (
  147:         <A4DesignPage
  148:           key={page.id}
  149:           designId={designId}
  150:           page={page}
  151:           context={context}
  152:           previewCase={previewCase}
  153:           pageLabel={pageLabel}
  154:         />
  155:       ))}
  156:     </div>
  157:   );
  158: }

FILE: components\report-engine\design-renderers\shared\report-evidence-data.ts

    1: import { filterValidReportEvidenceItems } from "@/lib/report-engine/report-evidence-utils";
    2: import type { PreviewCaseData } from "./report-types";
    3: 
    4: const REPORT_DESIGN_IMAGE_EVIDENCE_EXTENSION_PATTERN =
    5:   /\.(png|jpe?g|webp|gif|avif)(?:[?#].*)?$/i;
    6: 
    7: function hasReportDesignImageEvidenceExtension(value: unknown) {
    8:   const text = String(value || "").trim().replaceAll("\\", "/");
    9: 
   10:   if (!text) return false;
   11: 
   12:   return REPORT_DESIGN_IMAGE_EVIDENCE_EXTENSION_PATTERN.test(text);
   13: }
   14: 
   15: function isReportDesignImageEvidence(
   16:   evidence: NonNullable<PreviewCaseData["evidences"]>[number] | undefined,
   17: ) {
   18:   if (!evidence) return false;
   19:   if (String(evidence.type || "").trim().toUpperCase() === "IMAGE") return true;
   20:   if (String(evidence.mimeType || "").toLowerCase().startsWith("image/")) return true;
   21: 
   22:   return [
   23:     evidence.imageUrl,
   24:     evidence.url,
   25:     evidence.fileUrl,
   26:     evidence.publicUrl,
   27:     evidence.storagePath,
   28:   ].some((value) => hasReportDesignImageEvidenceExtension(value));
   29: }
   30: 
   31: export function getReportDesignEvidenceImageUrl(
   32:   evidence: NonNullable<PreviewCaseData["evidences"]>[number] | undefined,
   33: ) {
   34:   if (!evidence || !isReportDesignImageEvidence(evidence)) return "";
   35: 
   36:   return String(
   37:     evidence.imageUrl ||
   38:       evidence.url ||
   39:       evidence.fileUrl ||
   40:       evidence.publicUrl ||
   41:       evidence.storagePath ||
   42:       "",
   43:   ).trim();
   44: }
   45: 
   46: export function getValidPreviewEvidences(previewCase: PreviewCaseData | null) {
   47:   const hasSelectedCase = Boolean(previewCase?.caseId);
   48: 
   49:   return filterValidReportEvidenceItems(previewCase?.evidences || [], {
   50:     allowSampleEvidence: !hasSelectedCase,
   51:   });
   52: }
   53: 
   54: export function getEvidenceStartIndex(block: any) {
   55:   const startIndex = Number(block.evidenceStartIndex || 0);
   56: 
   57:   if (!Number.isFinite(startIndex) || startIndex < 0) {
   58:     return 0;
   59:   }
   60: 
   61:   return Math.floor(startIndex);
   62: }
   63: 
   64: export function getEvidencePerPage(block: any) {
   65:   const explicitLimit = Number(block.evidenceLimit || 0);
   66: 
   67:   if (Number.isFinite(explicitLimit) && explicitLimit > 0) {
   68:     return Math.max(1, Math.floor(explicitLimit));
   69:   }
   70:   return getSmartEvidencePerPage(block);
   71: }
   72: 
   73: export function getSmartEvidencePerPage(block: any) {
   74:   const layout = String(block?.evidenceLayout || "TWO_PER_PAGE");
   75:   const ratio = String(block?.evidenceAspectRatio || "LANDSCAPE_4_3");
   76:   const fit = String(block?.evidenceFit || "contain");
   77: 
   78:   if (layout === "ATTACHMENT_LIST") return 10;
   79:   if (layout === "ONE_PER_PAGE") return 1;
   80:   if (layout === "TWO_PER_PAGE") return 2;
   81: 
   82:   /*
   83:     GRID_2X2 is a maximum capacity, not a forced capacity.
   84:     The system must keep every evidence card inside the A4 frame.
   85:     Portrait images are tall, so 4 cards can overflow the page.
   86:   */
   87:   if (layout === "GRID_2X2") {
   88:     if (ratio === "PORTRAIT_3_4") return 2;
   89:     if (ratio === "SQUARE_1_1" && fit === "cover") return 4;
   90:     if (ratio === "SQUARE_1_1") return 4;
   91:     if (ratio === "LANDSCAPE_16_9") return 4;
   92:     return 4;
   93:   }
   94: 
   95:   return 2;
   96: }
   97: 
   98: export function createEvidencePlaceholders(count: number, startIndex: number) {
   99:   return Array.from({ length: count }).map((_, index) => ({
  100:     id: `placeholder-evidence-${startIndex + index + 1}`,
  101:     title: `شاهد تجريبي ${startIndex + index + 1}`,
  102:     caption: "مكان الشاهد داخل التقارير",
  103:     fileUrl: "",
  104:     imageUrl: "",
  105:   }));
  106: }
  107: 
  108: export function getEvidenceGridClass(block: any) {
  109:   const perPage = getEvidencePerPage(block);
  110: 
  111:   if (block.evidenceLayout === "ATTACHMENT_LIST") {
  112:     return "grid gap-2";
  113:   }
  114: 
  115:   if (perPage <= 1) {
  116:     return "grid gap-3";
  117:   }
  118: 
  119:   return "grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2";
  120: }
  121: 
  122: export function getEvidenceImageHeightClass(block: any) {
  123:   const perPage = getEvidencePerPage(block);
  124:   const ratio = block.evidenceAspectRatio || "LANDSCAPE_4_3";
  125: 
  126:   /*
  127:     Fixed heights are intentional:
  128:     they keep evidence inside the printable A4 area.
  129:     Extra evidence must go to a new Evidence Page instead of stretching A4.
  130:   */
  131:   if (perPage <= 1) {
  132:     switch (ratio) {
  133:       case "PORTRAIT_3_4":
  134:         return "h-[185mm]";
  135:       case "SQUARE_1_1":
  136:         return "h-[160mm]";
  137:       case "LANDSCAPE_16_9":
  138:         return "h-[122mm]";
  139:       case "LANDSCAPE_4_3":
  140:       default:
  141:         return "h-[138mm]";
  142:     }
  143:   }
  144: 
  145:   if (perPage === 2) {
  146:     switch (ratio) {
  147:       case "PORTRAIT_3_4":
  148:         return "h-[92mm]";
  149:       case "SQUARE_1_1":
  150:         return "h-[82mm]";
  151:       case "LANDSCAPE_16_9":
  152:         return "h-[58mm]";
  153:       case "LANDSCAPE_4_3":
  154:       default:
  155:         return "h-[66mm]";
  156:     }
  157:   }
  158: 
  159:   switch (ratio) {
  160:     case "SQUARE_1_1":
  161:       return "h-[56mm]";
  162:     case "LANDSCAPE_16_9":
  163:       return "h-[42mm]";
  164:     case "PORTRAIT_3_4":
  165:       return "h-[82mm]";
  166:     case "LANDSCAPE_4_3":
  167:     default:
  168:       return "h-[48mm]";
  169:   }
  170: }
  171: 
  172: export function getEvidenceImageClass(block: any) {
  173:   const fit = block.evidenceFit === "cover" ? "object-cover" : "object-contain";
  174:   return `${getEvidenceImageHeightClass(block)} w-full ${fit}`;
  175: }
  176: 
  177: export function getEvidenceGridStyle(block: any) {
  178:   const perPage = getEvidencePerPage(block);
  179:   const gapMm = Number(block.evidenceGapMm || 0);
  180: 
  181:   const style: Record<string, string> = {};
  182: 
  183:   if (gapMm > 0) {
  184:     style.gap = `${Math.min(Math.max(gapMm, 2), 12)}mm`;
  185:   }
  186: 
  187:   if (block.evidenceLayout !== "ATTACHMENT_LIST") {
  188:     style.gridTemplateColumns =
  189:       perPage <= 1 ? "minmax(0, 1fr)" : "repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr))";
  190:   }
  191: 
  192:   return style;
  193: }
  194: 
  195: export function getEvidenceFigureStyle(block: any) {
  196:   const perPage = getEvidencePerPage(block);
  197:   const widthMm = Number(block.evidenceImageWidthMm || 0);
  198: 
  199:   if (!widthMm || perPage > 1) {
  200:     return {};
  201:   }
  202: 
  203:   return {
  204:     maxWidth: `${Math.min(Math.max(widthMm, 40), 180)}mm`,
  205:     marginInline: "auto",
  206:   };
  207: }
  208: 
  209: export function getEvidenceImageStyle(block: any) {
  210:   const heightMm = Number(block.evidenceImageHeightMm || 0);
  211:   const widthMm = Number(block.evidenceImageWidthMm || 0);
  212:   const perPage = getEvidencePerPage(block);
  213: 
  214:   const style: Record<string, string> = {
  215:     objectFit: block.evidenceFit === "cover" ? "cover" : "contain",
  216:   };
  217: 
  218:   if (heightMm > 0) {
  219:     style.height = `${Math.min(Math.max(heightMm, 35), 190)}mm`;
  220:   }
  221: 
  222:   if (widthMm > 0 && perPage <= 1) {
  223:     style.width = `${Math.min(Math.max(widthMm, 40), 180)}mm`;
  224:   }
  225: 
  226:   return style;
  227: }

FILE: components\report-engine\design-renderers\shared\report-signatures.ts

    1: export function getReportDesignSignatureStyleText() {
    2:   return `
    3:     .pdf-report-page,
    4:     .pdf-report-page * {
    5:       color-scheme: light !important;
    6:     }
    7: 
    8:     .report-design-signature-grid-block,
    9:     .report-design-signature-grid-block * {
   10:       color-scheme: light !important;
   11:     }
   12: 
   13:     .report-design-signature-image-frame {
   14:       background: #ffffff !important;
   15:     }
   16: 
   17:     .report-design-signature-image {
   18:       background: #ffffff !important;
   19:       object-fit: contain !important;
   20:       mix-blend-mode: normal !important;
   21:       filter: none !important;
   22:     }

    6: function findSavedEvidenceGalleryBlock(template: any) {
    7:   const pages = Array.isArray(template?.pages) ? template.pages : [];
    8: 
    9:   for (const page of pages) {
   10:     const blocks = Array.isArray(page?.blocks) ? page.blocks : [];
   11:     const block = blocks.find((item: any) => item?.kind === "evidence-gallery");
   12: 
   13:     if (block) {
   14:       return block;
   15:     }
   16:   }
   17: 
   18:   return null;
   19: }
   20: export function normalizeFinalReportTemplate(
   21:   template: any,
   22:   editorialBlocks: Record<string, string>,
   23:   previewCaseData: PreviewCaseData | null,
   24: ) {
   25:   const pages = Array.isArray(template?.pages) ? template.pages : [];
   26:   const savedEvidenceBlock = findSavedEvidenceGalleryBlock(template);
   27:   const hasEvidence = getValidPreviewEvidences(previewCaseData).length > 0;
   28: 
   29:   return {
   30:     ...template,
   31:     pages: pages.flatMap((page: any, pageIndex: number) => {
   32:       if (!hasEvidence && page?.kind === "evidence") {
   33:         return [];
   34:       }
   35: 
   36:       const pageBlocks = Array.isArray(page?.blocks) ? page.blocks : [];
   37: 
   38:       const normalizedBlocks = pageBlocks
   39:         .map((block: any, blockIndex: number) =>
   40:           normalizeFinalReportBlock(template, block, blockIndex, editorialBlocks),
   41:         )
   42:         .filter((block: any) => hasEvidence || block.kind !== "evidence-gallery");
   43: 
   44:       const shouldAddEvidenceBlock =
   45:         hasEvidence &&
   46:         page?.kind === "evidence" &&
   47:         !savedEvidenceBlock &&
   48:         !normalizedBlocks.some((block: any) => block.kind === "evidence-gallery");
   49: 
   50:       return [{
   51:         ...page,
   52:         id: page?.id || "final-page-" + (pageIndex + 1),
   53:         title: page?.title || "صفحة " + (pageIndex + 1),
   54:         kind: page?.kind || "content",
   55:         blocks: shouldAddEvidenceBlock
   56:           ? [
   57:               ...normalizedBlocks,
   58:               {
   59:                 id: "auto-evidence-gallery-" + (pageIndex + 1),
   60:                 kind: "evidence-gallery",
   61:                 title: "الشواهد والمرفقات",
   62:                 content: "",
   63:                 variant: "card",
   64:                 align: "right",
   65:                 showTitle: true,
   66:                 placement: "flow",
   67:                 evidenceLayout: "GRID_2X2",
   68:                 evidenceFit: "contain",
   69:                 evidenceAspectRatio: "LANDSCAPE_4_3",
   70:                 evidenceShowCaptions: true,
   71:                 evidenceAutoCreatePages: true,
   72:                 evidenceEmptyBehavior: "message",
   73:               },
   74:             ]
   75:           : normalizedBlocks,
   76:       }];
   77:     }),
   78:   };
   79: }

   81: function normalizeFinalReportBlock(
   82:   template: any,
   83:   block: any,
   84:   index: number,
   85:   editorialBlocks: Record<string, string>,
   86: ) {
   87:   const settings = block?.settings || {};
   88:   const textLibrary = settings.textLibrary || {};
   89: 
   90:   const rawKind =
   91:     settings.smartBlockKind ||
   92:     block?.smartBlockKind ||
   93:     block?.kind ||
   94:     "paragraph";
   95: 
   96:   const blockId = block?.id || "final-block-" + (index + 1);
   97: 
   98:   const editedContent = getFinalEditedBlockContent(
   99:     block,
  100:     blockId,
  101:     editorialBlocks,
  102:   );
  103: 
  104:   const normalizedKind = normalizeFinalBlockKind(rawKind);
  105: 
  106:   const resolvedContent =
  107:     editedContent ||
  108:     resolveFinalBlockContent({
  109:       template,
  110:       block,
  111:       settings,
  112:       textLibrary,
  113:       rawKind,
  114:     });
  115: 
  116:   return {
  117:     ...block,
  118:     id: blockId,
  119:     originalKind: rawKind,
  120:     kind: normalizedKind,
  121:     finalFieldGroup: settings.fieldGroup || rawKind,
  122:     title:
  123:       block?.customTitle ||
  124:       block?.title ||
  125:       settings.title ||
  126:       getDefaultFinalBlockTitle(rawKind),
  127:     content: resolvedContent,
  128:     variant: block?.variant || settings.style || "card",
  129:     align: block?.align || settings.align || "right",
  130:     showTitle:
  131:       typeof block?.showTitle === "boolean"
  132:         ? block.showTitle
  133:         : settings.showTitle !== false,
  134:     showMeta:
  135:       typeof block?.showMeta === "boolean"
  136:         ? block.showMeta
  137:         : settings.showMeta !== false,
  138:     placement: block?.placement || settings.placement || "flow",
  139:     snippetId:
  140:       block?.snippetId ||
  141:       settings.snippetId ||
  142:       textLibrary.snippetId ||
  143:       null,
  144: 
  145:     fieldKeys:
  146:       block?.fieldKeys ||
  147:       settings.fieldKeys ||
  148:       settings.selectedFieldKeys ||
  149:       settings.workflowFieldKeys ||
  150:       [],
  151: 
  152:     evidenceLayout:
  153:       block?.evidenceLayout || settings.evidenceLayout || "GRID_2X2",
  154:     evidenceFit: block?.evidenceFit || settings.evidenceFit || "contain",
  155:     evidenceAspectRatio:
  156:       block?.evidenceAspectRatio ||
  157:       settings.evidenceAspectRatio ||
  158:       "LANDSCAPE_4_3",
  159:     evidenceShowCaptions:
  160:       typeof block?.evidenceShowCaptions === "boolean"
  161:         ? block.evidenceShowCaptions
  162:         : settings.evidenceShowCaptions !== false,
  163:     evidenceAutoCreatePages:
  164:       typeof block?.evidenceAutoCreatePages === "boolean"
  165:         ? block.evidenceAutoCreatePages
  166:         : settings.evidenceAutoCreatePages !== false,
  167:     evidenceEmptyBehavior:
  168:       block?.evidenceEmptyBehavior ||
  169:       settings.evidenceEmptyBehavior ||
  170:       "message",
  171:   };
  172: }

  337: function normalizeFinalBlockKind(kind: string) {
  338:   if (kind === "cover-title") return "hero-title";
  339:   if (kind === "paragraph") return "multi-paragraph";
  340:   if (kind === "custom-paragraph") return "multi-paragraph";
  341:   if (kind === "text-library") return "multi-paragraph";
  342:   if (kind === "case-meta") return "field-list";
  343:   if (kind === "student-summary") return "field-list";
  344:   if (kind === "service-summary") return "field-list";
  345:   if (kind === "field-list") return "field-list";
  346:   if (kind === "approval-signature") return "closing-note";
  347:   if (kind === "identity-header") return "section-text";
  348: 
  349:   return kind;
  350: }
  351: 
  352: function getDefaultFinalBlockTitle(kind: string) {
  353:   if (kind === "cover-title") return "عنوان التقارير";
  354:   if (kind === "case-meta") return "بيانات الحالة";
  355:   if (kind === "student-summary") return "بيانات الطالب/الطالبة";
  356:   if (kind === "service-summary") return "بيانات الخدمة";
  357:   if (kind === "field-list") return "حقول من الحالة";
  358:   if (kind === "text-library") return "نص من مكتبة النصوص";
  359:   if (kind === "custom-paragraph") return "فقرة مخصصة";
  360:   if (kind === "evidence-gallery") return "الشواهد والمرفقات";
  361:   if (kind === "approval-signature") return "الاعتماد";
  362: 
  363:   return "بلوك التقارير";
  364: }
