  366: 
  367: function getFinalEditedBlockContent(
  368:   block: any,
  369:   blockId: string,
  370:   editorialBlocks: Record<string, string>,
  371: ) {
  372:   const candidateKeys = [
  373:     blockId,
  374:     block?.id ? String(block.id) : "",
  375:   ].filter(Boolean);
  376: 
  377:   for (const key of candidateKeys) {
  378:     const value = editorialBlocks[key];
  379: 
  380:     if (!value || !String(value).trim()) {
  381:       continue;
  382:     }
  383: 
  384:     if (isFinalLegacyRenderedReportDump(value)) {
  385:       continue;
  386:     }
  387: 
  388:     return value;
  389:   }
  390: 
  391:   return "";
  392: }
  393: 
  394: function isFinalLegacyRenderedReportDump(value: string) {
  395:   const text = String(value || "");
  396: 
  397:   if (!text.trim()) {
  398:     return false;
  399:   }
  400: 
  401:   const hasReplacementChars = /�/.test(text);
  402:   const hasMojibakeMarks = /[ØÙÃ]/.test(text);
  403:   const hasReportDumpLabels =
  404:     /program_name\s*:|semester\s*:|gregorian_date\s*:|beneficiaries\s*:|execution_action\s*:|execution_mechanism\s*:|performance_indicator\s*:|selectedStudent\s*:/.test(
  405:       text,
  406:     );
  407: 
  408:   const hasOldArabicDump =
  409:     text.includes("ملخص التقارير") ||
  410:     text.includes("بيانات الحالة") ||
  411:     text.includes("القيم المسجلة") ||
  412:     text.includes("الشواهد:") ||
  413:     text.includes("تقرير:");
  414: 
  415:   const tooLongForBlock = text.length > 1800;
  416: 
  417:   return (
  418:     hasReplacementChars ||
  419:     hasMojibakeMarks ||
  420:     hasReportDumpLabels ||
  421:     (hasOldArabicDump && tooLongForBlock)
  422:   );
  423: }

  425: 
  426: function resolveFinalBlockContent({
  427:   template,
  428:   block,
  429:   settings,
  430:   textLibrary,
  431:   rawKind,
  432: }: {
  433:   template: any;
  434:   block: any;
  435:   settings: any;
  436:   textLibrary: any;
  437:   rawKind: string;
  438: }) {
  439:   if (rawKind === "cover-title") {
  440:     return block?.content || settings?.content || "{{case.title}}";
  441:   }
  442: 
  443:   if (rawKind === "custom-paragraph") {
  444:     return block?.customContent || block?.content || settings?.content || "";
  445:   }
  446: 
  447:   if (rawKind === "paragraph") {
  448:     return block?.content || settings?.content || "";
  449:   }
  450: 
  451:   if (rawKind === "text-library") {
  452:     return resolveFinalTextLibraryContent(template, block, settings, textLibrary);
  453:   }
  454: 
  455:   const candidateContent =
  456:     block?.content ||
  457:     settings?.content ||
  458:     block?.customContent ||
  459:     block?.defaultContent ||
  460:     "";
  461: 
  462:   return isFinalLegacyRenderedReportDump(candidateContent)
  463:     ? ""
  464:     : candidateContent;
  465: }
  466: 
  467: function resolveFinalTextLibraryContent(
  468:   template: any,
  469:   block: any,
  470:   settings: any,
  471:   textLibrary: any,
  472: ) {
  473:   const snippets = collectFinalTextSnippets(template);
  474:   const snippetId =
  475:     textLibrary?.snippetId ||
  476:     settings?.snippetId ||
  477:     block?.snippetId ||
  478:     "";
  479: 
  480:   if (snippetId) {
  481:     const selected = snippets.find((snippet: any) => snippet.id === snippetId);
  482: 
  483:     if (selected) {
  484:       return selected.content || selected.text || selected.body || "";
  485:     }
  486:   }
  487: 
  488:   const category =
  489:     textLibrary?.category ||
  490:     settings?.category ||
  491:     block?.category ||
  492:     "مقدمة";
  493: 
  494:   const serviceSlug =
  495:     textLibrary?.serviceSlug ||
  496:     settings?.serviceSlug ||
  497:     template?.serviceSlug ||
  498:     "";
  499: 
  500:   const matching = snippets.find((snippet: any) => {
  501:     const matchesCategory =
  502:       !category ||
  503:       category === "all" ||
  504:       snippet.category === category;
  505: 
  506:     const matchesService =
  507:       !serviceSlug ||
  508:       !snippet.serviceSlug ||
  509:       snippet.serviceSlug === serviceSlug;
  510: 
  511:     return matchesCategory && matchesService;
  512:   });
  513: 
  514:   if (matching) {
  515:     return matching.content || matching.text || matching.body || "";
  516:   }
  517: 
  518:   return getFinalTextLibraryFallback(category);
  519: }
  520: 
  521: function collectFinalTextSnippets(template: any) {
  522:   const candidates = [
  523:     template?.snippets,
  524:     template?.textSnippets,
  525:     template?.textLibrary,
  526:     template?.library?.snippets,
  527:     template?.settings?.snippets,
  528:   ];
  529: 
  530:   for (const candidate of candidates) {
  531:     if (Array.isArray(candidate)) {
  532:       return candidate;
  533:     }
  534:   }
  535: 
  536:   return [];
  537: }
  538: 
  539: function getFinalTextLibraryFallback(category: string) {
  540:   if (category === "هدف") {
  541:     return "يهدف هذا التقارير إلى توثيق {{service.name}} للحالة {{case.title}}، وبيان أبرز البيانات والإجراءات المرتبطة بها.";
  542:   }
  543: 
  544:   if (category === "إجراء") {
  545:     return "تم تنفيذ الإجراءات المرتبطة بالحالة وفق البيانات المدخلة في النظام، مع توثيق ما يلزم من شواهد ومرفقات.";
  546:   }
  547: 
  548:   if (category === "نتيجة") {
  549:     return "تشير البيانات المدخلة إلى أن الحالة تم التعامل معها ضمن خدمة {{service.name}}، مع حفظ النتائج والتوصيات داخل التقارير.";
  550:   }
  551: 
  552:   if (category === "توصية") {
  553:     return "يوصى بمتابعة الحالة حسب الحاجة، وتحديث السجل عند وجود مستجدات، وربط الشواهد الداعمة بالتقارير.";
  554:   }
  555: 
  556:   if (category === "خاتمة") {
  557:     return "تم إعداد هذا التقارير من منصة التوجيه الطلابي اعتمادًا على بيانات الحالة والشواهد المرتبطة بها.";
  558:   }
  559: 
  560:   return "تم إعداد هذا التقارير لخدمة {{service.name}} بناءً على بيانات الحالة {{case.title}} والشواهد المرتبطة بها.";
  561: }
  562: 
  563: export function collectFinalValues(data: any): FinalReportValueItem[] {
  564:   if (Array.isArray(data?.values)) {
  565:     return data.values.map((item: any) => ({
  566:       fieldKey: item.fieldKey || item.key || "",
  567:       fieldLabel: item.fieldLabel || item.label || item.fieldKey || "",
  568:       value:
  569:         item.value === undefined || item.value === null
  570:           ? ""
  571:           : String(item.value),
  572:       valueItems: Array.isArray(item.valueItems)
  573:         ? item.valueItems.map((valueItem: unknown) => String(valueItem || "").trim()).filter(Boolean)
  574:         : undefined,
  575:     }));
  576:   }
  577: 
  578:   if (data?.values && typeof data.values === "object") {
  579:     return Object.entries(data.values).map(([key, value]) => ({
  580:       fieldKey: key,
  581:       fieldLabel: key,
  582:       value:
  583:         value === undefined || value === null
  584:           ? ""
  585:           : String(value),
  586:     }));
  587:   }
  588: 
  589:   return [];
  590: }
  591: 
  592: export function resolveContextVariable(key: string, context: Record<string, string>) {
  593:   const aliases: Record<string, string[]> = {
  594:     reportTitle: ["reportTitle", "case.title", "caseTitle"],
  595:     caseTitle: ["case.title", "caseTitle", "reportTitle"],
  596:     serviceName: ["service.name", "serviceName"],
  597:     studentName: ["student.name", "studentName"],
  598:     studentGrade: ["student.grade", "studentGrade"],
  599:     studentClassroom: ["student.classroom", "studentClassroom"],
  600:     guardianName: ["student.guardianName", "guardianName"],
  601:     guardianPhone: ["student.guardianPhone", "guardianPhone"],
  602:     schoolName: ["identity.schoolName", "schoolName"],
  603:     counselorName: ["identity.counselorName", "counselorName"],
  604:     principalName: ["identity.principalName", "principalName"],
  605:   };
  606: 
  607:   const lookupKeys = [
  608:     key,
  609:     ...(aliases[key] || []),
  610:     key.startsWith("field.") ? key : "field." + key,
  611:   ];
  612: 
  613:   for (const lookupKey of lookupKeys) {
  614:     const value = context[lookupKey];
  615: 
  616:     if (value !== undefined && value !== null && String(value).trim() !== "") {
  617:       return String(value);
  618:     }
  619:   }
  620: 
  621:   return "";
  622: }
  623: 
  624: export function getFinalFieldListItems(block: any, previewCaseData: PreviewCaseData | null) {
  625:   const data = (previewCaseData || {}) as any;
  626:   const values = collectFinalValues(data);
  627:   const group = block.finalFieldGroup || block.originalKind || "";
  628:   const selectedKeys = Array.isArray(block.fieldKeys) ? block.fieldKeys : [];
  629: 
  630:   if (group === "case-meta") {
  631:     return [
  632:       {
  633:         key: "service",
  634:         label: "الخدمة",
  635:         value: data.serviceName || data.service?.name || data.caseEntry?.service?.name || "",
  636:       },
  637:       {
  638:         key: "case-title",
  639:         label: "الحالة",
  640:         value: data.title || data.caseEntry?.title || "",
  641:       },
  642:       {
  643:         key: "status",
  644:         label: "الحالة الحالية",
  645:         value: data.status || data.caseEntry?.status || "",
  646:       },
  647:       {
  648:         key: "created-at",
  649:         label: "تاريخ الإنشاء",
  650:         value: formatFinalDate(data.createdAt || data.caseEntry?.createdAt),
  651:       },
  652:     ];
  653:   }
  654: 
  655:   if (group === "student-summary") {
  656:     const student = data.student || data.caseEntry?.student || {};
  657: 
  658:     return [
  659:       {
  660:         key: "student-name",
  661:         label: "الطالب/الطالبة",
  662:         value: student.name || student.fullName || "",
  663:       },
  664:       {
  665:         key: "grade",
  666:         label: "الصف",
  667:         value: student.grade || "",
  668:       },
  669:       {
  670:         key: "classroom",
  671:         label: "الفصل",
  672:         value: student.classroom || "",
  673:       },
  674:       {
  675:         key: "guardian",
  676:         label: "ولي الأمر",
  677:         value: student.guardianName || "",
  678:       },
  679:     ];
  680:   }
  681: 
  682:   if (selectedKeys.length) {
  683:     return values
  684:       .filter((item: FinalReportValueItem) =>
  685:         selectedKeys.some(
  686:           (key: string) =>
  687:             key === item.fieldKey ||
  688:             key === item.fieldLabel ||
  689:             "field." + key === item.fieldKey,
  690:         ),
  691:       )
  692:       .map((item: FinalReportValueItem) => ({
  693:         key: item.fieldKey,
  694:         label: item.fieldLabel || item.fieldKey,
  695:         value: item.value,
  696:         ...(Array.isArray(item.valueItems) && item.valueItems.length > 1
  697:           ? { valueItems: item.valueItems }
  698:           : {}),
  699:       }));
  700:   }
  701: 
  702:   return values.slice(0, 10).map((item: FinalReportValueItem) => ({
  703:     key: item.fieldKey,
  704:     label: item.fieldLabel || item.fieldKey,
  705:     value: item.value,
  706:     ...(Array.isArray(item.valueItems) && item.valueItems.length > 1
  707:       ? { valueItems: item.valueItems }
  708:       : {}),
  709:   }));
  710: }
  711: 
  712: function formatFinalEvidenceCount(count: number) {
  713:   if (count <= 0) return "0 شاهد";
  714:   if (count === 1) return "شاهد واحد";
  715:   if (count === 2) return "شاهدان";
  716:   if (count >= 3 && count <= 10) return count + " شواهد";
  717: 
  718:   return count + " شاهد";
  719: }
  720: 
  721: 
  722: function formatFinalDate(value?: string | null) {
  723:   if (!value) {
  724:     return new Date().toLocaleDateString("ar-SA");
  725:   }
  726: 
  727:   try {
  728:     return new Date(value).toLocaleDateString("ar-SA");
  729:   } catch {
  730:     return String(value);
  731:   }
  732: }
  733: 
  734: 
  735: 
  736: 
  737:

  763:   }
  764: `;
  765: 
  766: function applyProfile(
  767:   element: HTMLElement,
  768:   profile: ReportSmartA4Profile,
  769: ) {
  770:   const variables =
  771:     getReportSmartA4StyleVariables(
  772:       profile,
  773:     );
  774: 
  775:   Object.entries(
  776:     variables,
  777:   ).forEach(
  778:     ([property, value]) => {
  779:       element.style.setProperty(
  780:         property,
  781:         String(value),
  782:       );
  783:     },
  784:   );
  785: }
  786: 
  787: function applyCandidate(
  788:   viewport: HTMLElement,
  789:   profile: ReportSmartA4Profile,
  790:   fieldLayout:
  791:     ReportSmartA4FieldLayout,
  792: ) {
  793:   applyProfile(
  794:     viewport,
  795:     profile,
  796:   );
  797: 
  798:   viewport.dataset.reportDensity =
  799:     profile.mode;
  800: 
  801:   viewport.dataset.reportFieldLayout =
  802:     fieldLayout;
  803: }
  804: 
  805: function nextAnimationFrame() {
  806:   return new Promise<void>(
  807:     (resolve) => {
  808:       window.requestAnimationFrame(
  809:         () => resolve(),
  810:       );
  811:     },
  812:   );
  813: }
  814: 
  815: function sameResult(
  816:   current: ReportSmartA4LayoutResult,
  817:   next: ReportSmartA4LayoutResult,
  818: ) {
  819:   return (
  820:     current.mode === next.mode &&
  821:     current.fieldLayout ===
  822:       next.fieldLayout &&
  823:     current.fits === next.fits &&
  824:     current.needsOverflowPage ===
  825:       next.needsOverflowPage &&
  826:     current.overflowPx ===
  827:       next.overflowPx &&
  828:     current.signatureHeightPx ===
  829:       next.signatureHeightPx &&
  830:     current.signatureReservedPx ===
  831:       next.signatureReservedPx &&
  832:     current.boundaryBottomPx === next.boundaryBottomPx &&
  833:     current.footerBoundaryUsed === next.footerBoundaryUsed &&
  834:     current.dominantRole ===
  835:       next.dominantRole &&
  836:     current.severity ===
  837:       next.severity &&
  838:     current.candidateId ===
  839:       next.candidateId &&
  840:     current.score === next.score &&
  841:     current.preferEvidenceMove ===
  842:       next.preferEvidenceMove
  843:   );
  844: }
  845: 
  846: export function ReportSmartA4ContentRegion({
  847:   children,
  848:   heightMm,
  849:   footerSafeAreaMm = 0,
  850:   className = "",
  851:   contentClassName = "",
  852:   layoutKey,
  853:   priorityMode = "signature",
  854:   onLayoutResult,
  855: }: ReportSmartA4ContentRegionProps) {
  856:   const inheritedSemanticFingerprint =
  857:     useReportSmartSemanticFingerprint();
  858: 
  859:   const semanticInputFingerprint =
  860:     createSemanticInputFingerprint({
  861:       inheritedSemanticFingerprint,
  862:       heightMm,
  863:       footerSafeAreaMm,
  864:       layoutKey: layoutKey ?? null,
  865:       priorityMode,
  866:     });
  867: 
  868:   const viewportRef =
  869:     useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(
  870:       null,
  871:     );
  872: 
  873:   const contentRef =
  874:     useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(
  875:       null,
  876:     );
  877: 
  878:   const scheduledFrameRef =
  879:     useRef<number | null>(null);
  880: 
  881:   const scheduleMeasurementRef =
  882:     useRef<() => void>(() => undefined);
  883: 
  884:   const runIdRef =
  885:     useRef(0);
  886: 
  887:   const measuringRef =
  888:     useRef(false);
  889: 
  890:   const frozenRef =
  891:     useRef(false);
  892: 
  893:   const rerunRequestedRef =
  894:     useRef(false);
  895: 
  896:   const fontsSettledRef =
  897:     useRef(false);
  898: 
  899:   const settledAssetSourcesRef =
  900:     useRef(new Set<string>());
  901: 
  902:   const resizeFingerprintRef =
  903:     useRef("");
  904: 
  905:   const stabilizationRef = useRef({
  906:     fingerprint: "",
  907:     stablePasses: 0,
  908:     attempts: 0,
  909:   });
  910: 
  911:   const resultRef =
  912:     useRef(INITIAL_RESULT);
  913: 
  914:   const onLayoutResultRef =
  915:     useRef(onLayoutResult);
  916: 
  917:   const [result, setResult] =
  918:     useState(INITIAL_RESULT);
  919: 
  920:   const [layoutPhase, setLayoutPhase] =
  921:     useState<ReportSmartA4LifecyclePhase>("MEASURING");
  922: 
  923:   useEffect(() => {
  924:     onLayoutResultRef.current =
  925:       onLayoutResult;
  926:   }, [onLayoutResult]);
  927: 
  928:   const runMeasurement =
  929:     useCallback(async () => {
  930:       const viewport =
  931:         viewportRef.current;
  932: 
  933:       const content =
  934:         contentRef.current;
  935: 
  936:       if (
  937:         !viewport ||
  938:         !content ||
  939:         measuringRef.current ||
  940:         frozenRef.current ||
  941:         !fontsSettledRef.current
  942:       ) {
  943:         return;
  944:       }
  945: 
  946:       measuringRef.current = true;
  947:       rerunRequestedRef.current = false;
  948:       setLayoutPhase("MEASURING");
  949: 
  950:       const runId =
  951:         ++runIdRef.current;
  952: 
  953:       try {
  954:         const evaluated:
  955:           ReportSmartA4EvaluatedCandidate[] =
  956:           [];
  957: 
  958:         /*
  959:          * -----------------------------------------------------
  960:          * PLAN EXPLORATION
  961:          * -----------------------------------------------------
  962:          *
  963:          * كل Candidate يطبق فعلًا على DOM ثم يقاس.
  964:          * لا نعتمد على تقدير عدد الأحرف فقط.
  965:          */
  966:         for (
  967:           const candidate of
  968:           REPORT_SMART_A4_CANDIDATES
  969:         ) {
  970:           const profile =
  971:             getReportSmartA4Profile(
  972:               candidate.mode,
  973:             );
  974: 
  975:           applyCandidate(
  976:             viewport,
  977:             profile,
  978:             candidate.fieldLayout,
  979:           );
  980: 
  981:           await nextAnimationFrame();
  982:           await nextAnimationFrame();
  983: 
  984:           if (
  985:             runId !== runIdRef.current
  986:           ) {
  987:             return;
  988:           }
  989: 
  990:           const measurement =
  991:             measureReportSmartA4Layout(
  992:               viewport,
  993:               content,
  994:               priorityMode,
  995:               profile,
  996:             );
  997: 
  998:           const score =
  999:             scoreReportSmartA4Candidate(
 1000:               candidate,
 1001:               measurement,
 1002:             );
 1003: 
 1004:           evaluated.push({
 1005:             candidate,
 1006:             profile,
 1007:             measurement,
 1008:             score,
 1009:           });
 1010:         }
 1011: 
 1012:         if (
 1013:           runId !== runIdRef.current ||
 1014:           !evaluated.length
 1015:         ) {
 1016:           return;
 1017:         }
 1018: 
 1019:         const plan =
 1020:           chooseReportSmartA4Plan(
 1021:             evaluated,
 1022:           );
 1023: 
 1024:         const selected =
 1025:           evaluated.find(
 1026:             (item) =>
 1027:               item.candidate.id ===
 1028:               plan.candidateId,
 1029:           ) || evaluated[
 1030:             evaluated.length - 1
 1031:           ];
 1032: 
 1033:         /*
 1034:          * تثبيت الخطة الفائزة فقط بعد انتهاء جميع القياسات.
 1035:          */
 1036:         applyCandidate(
 1037:           viewport,
 1038:           selected.profile,
 1039:           selected.candidate
 1040:             .fieldLayout,
 1041:         );
 1042: 
 1043:         await nextAnimationFrame();
 1044: 
 1045:         if (
 1046:           runId !== runIdRef.current
 1047:         ) {
 1048:           return;
 1049:         }
 1050: 
 1051:         /*
 1052:          * قياس نهائي بعد تثبيت الخطة الفائزة.
 1053:          * هذا يمنع اختلاف القياس بسبب frame وسيط.
 1054:          */
 1055:         const finalMeasurement =
 1056:           measureReportSmartA4Layout(
 1057:             viewport,
 1058:             content,
 1059:             priorityMode,
 1060:             selected.profile,
 1061:           );
 1062: 
 1063:         const finalScore =
 1064:           scoreReportSmartA4Candidate(
 1065:             selected.candidate,
 1066:             finalMeasurement,
 1067:           );
 1068: 
 1069:         const finalPlan =
 1070:           chooseReportSmartA4Plan([
 1071:             {
 1072:               candidate:
 1073:                 selected.candidate,
 1074:               profile:
 1075:                 selected.profile,
 1076:               measurement:
 1077:                 finalMeasurement,
 1078:               score:
 1079:                 finalScore,
 1080:             },
 1081:           ]);
 1082: 
 1083:         const nextResult:
 1084:           ReportSmartA4LayoutResult = {
 1085:           mode:
 1086:             selected.candidate.mode,
 1087: 
 1088:           fieldLayout:
 1089:             selected.candidate
 1090:               .fieldLayout,
 1091: 
 1092:           fits:
 1093:             finalMeasurement.fits,
 1094: 
 1095:           needsOverflowPage:
 1096:             !finalMeasurement.fits,
 1097: 
 1098:           overflowPx:
 1099:             finalMeasurement
 1100:               .overflowPx,
 1101: 
 1102:           signatureHeightPx:
 1103:             finalMeasurement
 1104:               .signatureHeightPx,
 1105: 
 1106:           signatureReservedPx:
 1107:             finalMeasurement
 1108:               .signatureReservedPx,
 1109: 
 1110:           boundaryBottomPx:
 1111:             finalMeasurement.boundaryBottomPx,
 1112: 
 1113:           footerBoundaryUsed:
 1114:             finalMeasurement.footerBoundaryUsed,
 1115: 
 1116:           dominantRole:
 1117:             finalMeasurement
 1118:               .dominantRole,
 1119: 
 1120:           severity:
 1121:             finalPlan.severity,
 1122: 
 1123:           candidateId:
 1124:             selected.candidate.id,
 1125: 
 1126:           score:
 1127:             Math.round(
 1128:               finalScore * 10,
 1129:             ) / 10,
 1130: 
 1131:           /*
 1132:            * Important:
 1133:            * preserve decision from ALL candidate evaluation.
 1134:            * If the best overall fit required aggressive density,
 1135:            * Composer may move evidence and let us re-plan.
 1136:            */
 1137:           preferEvidenceMove:
 1138:             plan.preferEvidenceMove,
 1139:         };
 1140: 
 1141:         const unsettledImages = Array.from(
 1142:           content.querySelectorAll<HTMLImageElement>("img"),
 1143:         ).filter((image) => !image.complete);
 1144: 
 1145:         if (unsettledImages.length > 0) {
 1146:           setLayoutPhase("MEASURING");
 1147:           return;
 1148:         }
 1149: 
 1150:         content
 1151:           .querySelectorAll<HTMLImageElement>("img")
 1152:           .forEach((image) => {
 1153:             const source = image.currentSrc || image.src;
 1154:             if (source) {
 1155:               settledAssetSourcesRef.current.add(source);
 1156:             }
 1157:           });
 1158: 
 1159:         const layoutResultFingerprint =
 1160:           createSemanticInputFingerprint({
 1161:             candidateId: nextResult.candidateId,
 1162:             dominantRole: nextResult.dominantRole,
 1163:             fieldLayout: nextResult.fieldLayout,
 1164:             fits: nextResult.fits,
 1165:             mode: nextResult.mode,
 1166:             overflowPx: roundLayoutMetric(nextResult.overflowPx),
 1167:             preferEvidenceMove: nextResult.preferEvidenceMove,
 1168:             signatureHeightPx: roundLayoutMetric(
 1169:               nextResult.signatureHeightPx,
 1170:             ),
 1171:             signatureReservedPx: roundLayoutMetric(
 1172:               nextResult.signatureReservedPx,
 1173:             ),
 1174:             boundaryBottomPx: roundLayoutMetric(
 1175:               nextResult.boundaryBottomPx,
 1176:             ),
 1177:             footerBoundaryUsed: nextResult.footerBoundaryUsed,
 1178:           });
 1179: 
 1180:         const previousStability = stabilizationRef.current;
 1181:         const stablePasses =
 1182:           previousStability.fingerprint === layoutResultFingerprint
 1183:             ? previousStability.stablePasses + 1
 1184:             : 1;
 1185:         const attempts = previousStability.attempts + 1;
 1186: 
 1187:         stabilizationRef.current = {
 1188:           fingerprint: layoutResultFingerprint,
 1189:           stablePasses,
 1190:           attempts,
 1191:         };
 1192: 
 1193:         if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "development") {
 1194:           viewport.dataset.smartA4LayoutFingerprint =
 1195:             getFingerprintPrefix(layoutResultFingerprint);
 1196:           viewport.dataset.smartA4StablePasses = String(stablePasses);
 1197:           viewport.dataset.smartA4Attempts = String(attempts);
 1198:         }
 1199: 
 1200:         viewport.dataset
 1201:           .smartA4DominantRole =
 1202:           nextResult.dominantRole;
 1203: 
 1204:         viewport.dataset
 1205:           .smartA4Severity =
 1206:           nextResult.severity;
 1207: 
 1208:         viewport.dataset
 1209:           .smartA4Candidate =
 1210:           nextResult.candidateId;
 1211: 
 1212:         viewport.dataset
 1213:           .smartA4Score =
 1214:           String(nextResult.score);
 1215: 
 1216:         viewport.dataset
 1217:           .smartA4PreferEvidenceMove =
 1218:           String(
 1219:             nextResult
 1220:               .preferEvidenceMove,
 1221:           );
 1222: 
 1223:         viewport.dataset
 1224:           .smartA4SignatureProtected =
 1225:           String(
 1226:             nextResult
 1227:               .signatureHeightPx > 0,
 1228:           );
 1229: 
 1230:         if (
 1231:           !sameResult(
 1232:             resultRef.current,
 1233:             nextResult,
 1234:           )
 1235:         ) {
 1236:           resultRef.current =
 1237:             nextResult;
 1238: 
 1239:           setResult(nextResult);
 1240: 
 1241:           onLayoutResultRef.current?.(
 1242:             nextResult,
 1243:           );
 1244:         }
 1245: 
 1246:         if (
 1247:           stablePasses >= 2 ||
 1248:           attempts >= MAX_SMART_A4_MEASUREMENT_ATTEMPTS
 1249:         ) {
 1250:           frozenRef.current = true;
 1251:           rerunRequestedRef.current = false;
 1252:           setLayoutPhase("FROZEN");
 1253:         } else {
 1254:           rerunRequestedRef.current = true;
 1255:           setLayoutPhase("STABILIZING");
 1256:         }
 1257:       } finally {
 1258:         measuringRef.current =
 1259:           false;
 1260:       }
 1261:     }, [priorityMode]);
 1262: 
 1263:   const scheduleMeasurement =
 1264:     useCallback(() => {
 1265:       if (
 1266:         frozenRef.current ||
 1267:         measuringRef.current ||
 1268:         scheduledFrameRef.current !==
 1269:           null
 1270:       ) {
 1271:         return;
 1272:       }
 1273: 
 1274:       scheduledFrameRef.current =
 1275:         window.requestAnimationFrame(
 1276:           () => {
 1277:             scheduledFrameRef.current =
 1278:               null;
 1279: 
 1280:             void runMeasurement().finally(() => {
 1281:               if (
 1282:                 rerunRequestedRef.current &&
 1283:                 !frozenRef.current
 1284:               ) {
 1285:                 rerunRequestedRef.current = false;
 1286:                 scheduleMeasurementRef.current();
 1287:               }
 1288:             });
 1289:           },
 1290:         );
 1291:     }, [runMeasurement]);
 1292: 
 1293:   useLayoutEffect(() => {
 1294:     scheduleMeasurementRef.current = scheduleMeasurement;
 1295: 
 1296:     return () => {
 1297:       scheduleMeasurementRef.current = () => undefined;
 1298:     };
 1299:   }, [scheduleMeasurement]);
 1300: 
 1301:   useLayoutEffect(() => {
 1302:     runIdRef.current += 1;
 1303:     frozenRef.current = false;
 1304:     rerunRequestedRef.current = false;
 1305:     resizeFingerprintRef.current = "";
 1306:     settledAssetSourcesRef.current.clear();
 1307:     stabilizationRef.current = {
 1308:       fingerprint: "",
 1309:       stablePasses: 0,
 1310:       attempts: 0,
 1311:     };
 1312:     resultRef.current = INITIAL_RESULT;
 1313:     setResult(INITIAL_RESULT);
 1314:     setLayoutPhase("MEASURING");
 1315: 
 1316:     scheduleMeasurement();
 1317: 
 1318:     return () => {
 1319:       runIdRef.current += 1;
 1320: 
 1321:       if (scheduledFrameRef.current !== null) {
 1322:         window.cancelAnimationFrame(scheduledFrameRef.current);
 1323:         scheduledFrameRef.current = null;
 1324:       }
 1325:     };
 1326:   }, [
 1327:     semanticInputFingerprint,
 1328:     scheduleMeasurement,
 1329:   ]);
 1330: 
 1331:   useEffect(() => {
 1332:     const viewport =
 1333:       viewportRef.current;
 1334: 
 1335:     const content =
 1336:       contentRef.current;
 1337: 
 1338:     if (
 1339:       !viewport ||
 1340:       !content
 1341:     ) {
 1342:       return;
 1343:     }
 1344: 
 1345:     const handleResize = () => {
 1346:       if (frozenRef.current) {
 1347:         return;
 1348:       }
 1349: 
 1350:       const viewportRect = viewport.getBoundingClientRect();
 1351:       const contentRect = content.getBoundingClientRect();
 1352:       const nextResizeFingerprint = [
 1353:         roundLayoutMetric(viewportRect.width),
 1354:         roundLayoutMetric(viewportRect.height),
 1355:         roundLayoutMetric(contentRect.width),
 1356:         roundLayoutMetric(contentRect.height),
 1357:         roundLayoutMetric(content.scrollHeight),
 1358:       ].join(":");
 1359: 
 1360:       if (resizeFingerprintRef.current === nextResizeFingerprint) {
 1361:         return;
 1362:       }
 1363: 
 1364:       resizeFingerprintRef.current = nextResizeFingerprint;
 1365:       scheduleMeasurement();
 1366:     };
 1367: 
 1368:     const resizeObserver =
 1369:       new ResizeObserver(handleResize);
 1370: 
 1371:     const mutationObserver =
 1372:       new MutationObserver(() => {
 1373:         if (frozenRef.current) {
 1374:           return;
 1375:         }
 1376: 
 1377:         scheduleMeasurement();
 1378:       });
 1379: 
 1380:     const handleLoadedAsset =
 1381:       (event: Event) => {
 1382:         if (frozenRef.current) {
 1383:           return;
 1384:         }
 1385: 
 1386:         const image =
 1387:           event.target instanceof HTMLImageElement
 1388:             ? event.target
 1389:             : null;
 1390:         const source = image?.currentSrc || image?.src || "";
 1391: 
 1392:         if (
 1393:           source &&
 1394:           settledAssetSourcesRef.current.has(source)
 1395:         ) {
 1396:           return;
 1397:         }
 1398: 
 1399:         if (source) {
 1400:           settledAssetSourcesRef.current.add(source);
 1401:         }
 1402: 
 1403:         scheduleMeasurement();
 1404:       };
 1405: 
 1406:     resizeObserver.observe(
 1407:       viewport,
 1408:     );
 1409: 
 1410:     resizeObserver.observe(
 1411:       content,
 1412:     );
 1413: 
 1414:     mutationObserver.observe(
 1415:       content,
 1416:       {
 1417:         childList: true,
 1418:         characterData: true,
 1419:         subtree: true,
 1420:       },
 1421:     );
 1422: 
 1423:     content.addEventListener(
 1424:       "load",
 1425:       handleLoadedAsset,
 1426:       true,
 1427:     );
 1428: 
 1429:     content.addEventListener(
 1430:       "error",
 1431:       handleLoadedAsset,
 1432:       true,
 1433:     );
 1434: 
 1435:     window.addEventListener(
 1436:       "resize",
 1437:       handleResize,
 1438:     );
 1439: 
 1440:     if (!document.fonts) {
 1441:       fontsSettledRef.current = true;
 1442:       scheduleMeasurement();
 1443:     } else if (fontsSettledRef.current) {
 1444:       scheduleMeasurement();
 1445:     } else {
 1446:       void document.fonts.ready
 1447:         .then(() => {
 1448:           if (!fontsSettledRef.current) {
 1449:             fontsSettledRef.current = true;
 1450:             scheduleMeasurement();
 1451:           }
 1452:         })
 1453:         .catch(() => {
 1454:           fontsSettledRef.current = true;
 1455:           scheduleMeasurement();
 1456:         });
 1457:     }
 1458: 
 1459:     return () => {
 1460:       runIdRef.current += 1;
 1461: 
 1462:       resizeObserver.disconnect();
 1463:       mutationObserver.disconnect();
 1464: 
 1465:       content.removeEventListener(
 1466:         "load",
 1467:         handleLoadedAsset,
 1468:         true,
 1469:       );
 1470: 
 1471:       content.removeEventListener(
 1472:         "error",
 1473:         handleLoadedAsset,
 1474:         true,
 1475:       );
 1476: 
 1477:       window.removeEventListener(
 1478:         "resize",
 1479:         handleResize,
 1480:       );
 1481: 
 1482:       if (
 1483:         scheduledFrameRef.current !==
 1484:         null
 1485:       ) {
 1486:         window.cancelAnimationFrame(
 1487:           scheduledFrameRef.current,
 1488:         );
 1489: 
 1490:         scheduledFrameRef.current =
 1491:           null;
 1492:       }
 1493:     };
 1494:   }, [scheduleMeasurement, semanticInputFingerprint]);
 1495: 
 1496:   const selectedProfile =
 1497:     getReportSmartA4Profile(
 1498:       result.mode,
 1499:     );
 1500: 
 1501:   const selectedVariables =
 1502:     getReportSmartA4StyleVariables(
 1503:       selectedProfile,
 1504:     );
 1505: 
 1506:   const viewportStyle = {
 1507:     ...selectedVariables,
 1508: 
 1509:     height: `${heightMm}mm`,
 1510:     minHeight: `${heightMm}mm`,
 1511:     maxHeight: `${heightMm}mm`,
 1512: 
 1513:     overflow:
 1514:       result.needsOverflowPage
 1515:         ? "visible"
 1516:         : "hidden",
 1517:   } as CSSProperties;
 1518: 
 1519:   const developmentDiagnostics =
 1520:     process.env.NODE_ENV ===
 1521:     "development"
 1522:       ? {
 1523:           "data-smart-a4-semantic-fingerprint":
 1524:             getFingerprintPrefix(semanticInputFingerprint),
 1525: 
 1526:           "data-smart-a4-mode":
 1527:             result.mode,
 1528: 
 1529:           "data-smart-a4-field-layout":
 1530:             result.fieldLayout,
 1531: 
 1532:           "data-smart-a4-fits":
 1533:             String(result.fits),
 1534: 
 1535:           "data-smart-a4-overflow-px":
 1536:             String(
 1537:               result.overflowPx,
 1538:             ),
 1539: 
 1540:           "data-smart-a4-needs-overflow-page":
 1541:             String(
 1542:               result
 1543:                 .needsOverflowPage,
 1544:             ),
 1545: 
 1546:           "data-smart-a4-signature-height-px":
 1547:             String(
 1548:               result
 1549:                 .signatureHeightPx,
 1550:             ),
 1551: 
 1552:           "data-smart-a4-signature-reserved-px":
 1553:             String(
 1554:               result
 1555:                 .signatureReservedPx,
 1556:             ),
 1557: 
 1558:           "data-smart-a4-boundary-bottom-px":
 1559:             String(result.boundaryBottomPx),
 1560: 
 1561:           "data-smart-a4-footer-boundary-used":
 1562:             String(result.footerBoundaryUsed),
 1563: 
 1564:           "data-smart-a4-dominant-role":
 1565:             result.dominantRole,
 1566: 
 1567:           "data-smart-a4-severity":
 1568:             result.severity,
 1569: 
 1570:           "data-smart-a4-candidate":
 1571:             result.candidateId,
 1572: 
 1573:           "data-smart-a4-score":
 1574:             String(
 1575:               result.score,
 1576:             ),
 1577:         }
 1578:       : {};
 1579: 
 1580:   return (
 1581:     <div
 1582:       ref={viewportRef}
 1583:       className={[
 1584:         "report-smart-a4-content relative",
 1585:         className,
 1586:       ]
 1587:         .filter(Boolean)
 1588:         .join(" ")}
 1589:       style={viewportStyle}
 1590:       data-report-density={
 1591:         result.mode
 1592:       }
 1593:       data-report-field-layout={
 1594:         result.fieldLayout
 1595:       }
 1596:       data-report-overflow={
 1597:         result.needsOverflowPage
 1598:           ? "minimum-overflow"
 1599:           : "fit"
 1600:       }
 1601:       data-smart-a4-overflow-px={
 1602:         result.overflowPx
 1603:       }
 1604:       data-smart-a4-dominant-role={
 1605:         result.dominantRole
 1606:       }
 1607:       data-smart-a4-severity={
 1608:         result.severity
 1609:       }
 1610:       data-smart-a4-candidate={
 1611:         result.candidateId
 1612:       }
 1613:       data-smart-a4-score={
 1614:         result.score
 1615:       }
 1616:       data-smart-a4-prefer-evidence-move={
 1617:         String(
 1618:           result
 1619:             .preferEvidenceMove,
 1620:         )
 1621:       }
 1622:       data-smart-a4-signature-reserved-px={
 1623:         result.signatureReservedPx
 1624:       }
 1625:       data-smart-a4-boundary-bottom-px={result.boundaryBottomPx}
 1626:       data-smart-a4-footer-boundary-used={String(result.footerBoundaryUsed)}
 1627:       data-report-footer-safe-area-mm={footerSafeAreaMm}
 1628:       data-smart-a4-phase={layoutPhase}
 1629:       {...developmentDiagnostics}
 1630:     >
 1631:       <style>
 1632:         {SMART_A4_ENGINE_CSS}
 1633:       </style>
 1634: 
 1635:       <div
 1636:         ref={contentRef}
 1637:         className={[
 1638:           "min-h-full",
 1639:           contentClassName,
 1640:         ]
 1641:           .filter(Boolean)
 1642:           .join(" ")}
 1643:         data-report-smart-content
 1644:       >
 1645:         {children}
 1646:       </div>
 1647:     </div>
 1648:   );
 1649: }

FILE: components\report-engine\design-renderers\smart-layout\report-smart-a4-measure.ts

    1: import {
    2:   REPORT_SMART_A4_FOOTER_BOUNDARY_GAP_MM,
    3:   REPORT_SMART_A4_TOLERANCE_PX,
    4:   type ReportSmartA4PriorityMode,
    5:   type ReportSmartA4Profile,
    6: } from "./report-smart-a4-config";
    7: 
    8: export type ReportSmartA4BlockRole =
    9:   | "fields"
   10:   | "evidence"
   11:   | "narrative"
   12:   | "table"
   13:   | "signature"
   14:   | "title"
   15:   | "general";
   16: 
   17: export type ReportSmartA4BlockMeasurement = {
   18:   role: ReportSmartA4BlockRole;
   19:   kind: string;
   20:   heightPx: number;
   21:   topPx: number;
   22:   bottomPx: number;
   23:   overflowPx: number;
   24: };
   25: 
   26: export type ReportSmartA4Measurement = {
   27:   fits: boolean;
   28: 
   29:   viewportHeightPx: number;
   30:   contentHeightPx: number;
   31: 
   32:   overflowPx: number;
   33: 
   34:   signatureHeightPx: number;
   35:   signatureReservedPx: number;
   36: 
   37:   mainContentBottomPx: number;
   38:   mainContentBudgetPx: number;
   39:   mainContentOverflowPx: number;
   40: 
   41:   boundaryBottomPx: number;
   42:   footerBoundaryUsed: boolean;
   43: 
   44:   fieldHeightPx: number;
   45:   evidenceHeightPx: number;
   46:   narrativeHeightPx: number;
   47:   tableHeightPx: number;
   48: 
   49:   dominantRole: ReportSmartA4BlockRole;
   50: 
   51:   blocks: ReportSmartA4BlockMeasurement[];
   52: };
   53: 
   54: function round(value: number) {
   55:   return Math.round(value * 10) / 10;
   56: }
   57: 
   58: function getBlockRole(
   59:   element: HTMLElement,
   60: ): ReportSmartA4BlockRole {
   61:   if (
   62:     element.dataset.reportPriorityBlock === "signature" ||
   63:     element.hasAttribute("data-report-design-signature-block")
   64:   ) {
   65:     return "signature";
   66:   }
   67: 
   68:   const kind = String(
   69:     element.dataset.reportSmartBlock || "",
   70:   ).trim();
   71: 
   72:   switch (kind) {
   73:     case "dynamic-fields":
   74:     case "field-list":
   75:     case "case-meta":
   76:     case "student-summary":
   77:     case "service-summary":
   78:       return "fields";
   79: 
   80:     case "evidence-gallery":
   81:       return "evidence";
   82: 
   83:     case "multi-paragraph":
   84:     case "closing-note":
   85:       return "narrative";
   86: 
   87:     case "report-one-table":
   88:     case "structured-table":
   89:       return "table";
   90: 
   91:     case "hero-title":
   92:     case "meta-strip":
   93:       return "title";
   94: 
   95:     default:
   96:       return "general";
   97:   }
   98: }
   99: 
  100: function sumRoleHeight(
  101:   blocks: ReportSmartA4BlockMeasurement[],
  102:   role: ReportSmartA4BlockRole,
  103: ) {
  104:   return blocks
  105:     .filter((block) => block.role === role)
  106:     .reduce((sum, block) => sum + block.heightPx, 0);
  107: }
  108: 
  109: export function measureReportSmartA4Layout(
  110:   viewport: HTMLElement,
  111:   content: HTMLElement,
  112:   priorityMode: ReportSmartA4PriorityMode,
  113:   profile: ReportSmartA4Profile,
  114: ): ReportSmartA4Measurement {
  115:   const viewportRect = viewport.getBoundingClientRect();
  116:   const contentRect = content.getBoundingClientRect();
  117: 
  118:   const localScale =
  119:     viewport.clientHeight > 0
  120:       ? viewportRect.height / viewport.clientHeight
  121:       : 1;
  122: 
  123:   const safeScale =
  124:     Number.isFinite(localScale) && localScale > 0
  125:       ? localScale
  126:       : 1;
  127: 
  128:   const viewportHeightPx = viewport.clientHeight;
  129:   const footerSafeAreaMm = Math.max(
  130:     0,
  131:     Number(viewport.dataset.reportFooterSafeAreaMm || "0") || 0,
  132:   );
  133:   const footerSafeAreaPx = footerSafeAreaMm * (96 / 25.4);
  134:   const footerBoundaryGapPx =
  135:     REPORT_SMART_A4_FOOTER_BOUNDARY_GAP_MM * (96 / 25.4);
  136:   const physicalPage = viewport.closest<HTMLElement>(".pdf-report-page");
  137:   const pageFooter =
  138:     physicalPage?.querySelector<HTMLElement>("[data-report-page-footer]") ||
  139:     null;
  140:   const fallbackBoundaryBottom =
  141:     viewportRect.bottom - footerSafeAreaPx * safeScale;
  142:   const realFooterBoundaryBottom = pageFooter
  143:     ? pageFooter.getBoundingClientRect().top - footerBoundaryGapPx * safeScale
  144:     : Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  145:   const boundaryBottom = Math.min(
  146:     fallbackBoundaryBottom,
  147:     realFooterBoundaryBottom,
  148:   );
  149:   const footerBoundaryUsed = Boolean(pageFooter);
  150:   const usableViewportHeightPx = Math.max(
  151:     0,
  152:     Math.min(
  153:       viewportHeightPx,
  154:       (boundaryBottom - viewportRect.top) / safeScale,
  155:     ),
  156:   );
  157: 
  158:   const elements = Array.from(
  159:     content.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
  160:       '[data-report-smart-block], [data-report-priority-block="signature"], [data-report-design-signature-block]',
  161:     ),
  162:   );
  163: 
  164:   const blocks = elements.map(
  165:     (element): ReportSmartA4BlockMeasurement => {
  166:       const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
  167: 
  168:       const topPx =
  169:         (rect.top - contentRect.top) / safeScale;
  170: 
  171:       const bottomPx =
  172:         (rect.bottom - contentRect.top) / safeScale;
  173: 
  174:       return {
  175:         role: getBlockRole(element),
  176:         kind: String(
  177:           element.dataset.reportSmartBlock || "",
  178:         ),
  179:         heightPx: round(rect.height / safeScale),
  180:         topPx: round(topPx),
  181:         bottomPx: round(bottomPx),
  182:         overflowPx: round(
  183:           Math.max(
  184:             0,
  185:             (rect.bottom - boundaryBottom) / safeScale,
  186:           ),
  187:         ),
  188:       };
  189:     },
  190:   );
  191:   const crossesHardBoundary = elements.some(
  192:     (element) => element.getBoundingClientRect().bottom > boundaryBottom,
  193:   );
  194: 
  195:   const signatureBlocks = blocks.filter(
  196:     (block) => block.role === "signature",
  197:   );
  198: 
  199:   const nonSignatureBlocks = blocks.filter(
  200:     (block) => block.role !== "signature",
  201:   );
  202: 
  203:   const signatureHeightPx = signatureBlocks.reduce(
  204:     (sum, block) => sum + block.heightPx,
  205:     0,
  206:   );
  207: 
  208:   const signatureReservedPx =
  209:     priorityMode === "signature" && signatureBlocks.length
  210:       ? signatureHeightPx + profile.signatureSafetyGapPx
  211:       : 0;
  212: 
  213:   const mainContentBudgetPx = Math.max(
  214:     0,
  215:     usableViewportHeightPx - signatureReservedPx,
  216:   );
  217: 
  218:   const mainContentBottomPx = nonSignatureBlocks.reduce(
  219:     (largest, block) =>
  220:       Math.max(largest, block.bottomPx),
  221:     0,
  222:   );
  223: 
  224:   const mainContentOverflowPx =
  225:     priorityMode === "signature"
  226:       ? Math.max(
  227:           0,
  228:           mainContentBottomPx - mainContentBudgetPx,
  229:         )
  230:       : 0;
  231: 
  232:   const blockOverflowPx = blocks.reduce(
  233:     (largest, block) =>
  234:       Math.max(largest, block.overflowPx),
  235:     0,
  236:   );
  237: 
  238:   const scrollOverflowPx = Math.max(
  239:     0,
  240:     content.scrollHeight - viewportHeightPx,
  241:   );
  242: 
  243:   const boundingOverflowPx = Math.max(
  244:     0,
  245:     (contentRect.bottom - viewportRect.bottom) / safeScale,
  246:   );
  247: 
  248:   const overflowPx = Math.max(
  249:     0,
  250:     blockOverflowPx,
  251:     scrollOverflowPx,
  252:     boundingOverflowPx,
  253:     mainContentOverflowPx,
  254:   );
  255: 
  256:   const roleHeights = {
  257:     fields: sumRoleHeight(blocks, "fields"),
  258:     evidence: sumRoleHeight(blocks, "evidence"),
  259:     narrative: sumRoleHeight(blocks, "narrative"),
  260:     table: sumRoleHeight(blocks, "table"),
  261:     signature: signatureHeightPx,
  262:     title: sumRoleHeight(blocks, "title"),
  263:     general: sumRoleHeight(blocks, "general"),
  264:   };
  265: 
  266:   const dominantRole = (
  267:     Object.entries(roleHeights) as Array<
  268:       [ReportSmartA4BlockRole, number]
  269:     >
  270:   ).reduce(
  271:     (best, current) =>
  272:       current[1] > best[1] ? current : best,
  273:     ["general", 0] as [
  274:       ReportSmartA4BlockRole,
  275:       number,
  276:     ],
  277:   )[0];
  278: 
  279:   return {
  280:     fits:
  281:       !crossesHardBoundary &&
  282:       blockOverflowPx <= 0 &&
  283:       overflowPx <= REPORT_SMART_A4_TOLERANCE_PX,
  284: 
  285:     viewportHeightPx: round(usableViewportHeightPx),
  286:     contentHeightPx: round(content.scrollHeight),
  287: 
  288:     overflowPx: round(overflowPx),
  289: 
  290:     signatureHeightPx: round(signatureHeightPx),
  291:     signatureReservedPx: round(signatureReservedPx),
  292: 
  293:     mainContentBottomPx: round(mainContentBottomPx),
  294:     mainContentBudgetPx: round(mainContentBudgetPx),
  295:     mainContentOverflowPx: round(mainContentOverflowPx),
  296: 
  297:     boundaryBottomPx: round(
  298:       (boundaryBottom - viewportRect.top) / safeScale,
  299:     ),
  300:     footerBoundaryUsed,
  301: 
  302:     fieldHeightPx: round(roleHeights.fields),
  303:     evidenceHeightPx: round(roleHeights.evidence),
  304:     narrativeHeightPx: round(roleHeights.narrative),
  305:     tableHeightPx: round(roleHeights.table),
  306: 
  307:     dominantRole,
  308: 
  309:     blocks,
  310:   };
  311: }

FILE: components\report-engine\design-renderers\smart-layout\report-smart-a4-planner.ts

    1: import type {
    2:   ReportSmartA4Mode,
    3:   ReportSmartA4Profile,
    4: } from "./report-smart-a4-config";
    5: 
    6: import type {
    7:   ReportSmartA4BlockRole,
    8:   ReportSmartA4Measurement,
    9: } from "./report-smart-a4-measure";
   10: 
   11: export type ReportSmartA4FieldLayout =
   12:   | "comfortable"
   13:   | "packed"
   14:   | "inline";
   15: 
   16: export type ReportSmartA4OverflowSeverity =
   17:   | "none"
   18:   | "tiny"
   19:   | "small"
   20:   | "medium"
   21:   | "large";
   22: 
   23: export type ReportSmartA4Candidate = {
   24:   id: string;
   25:   mode: ReportSmartA4Mode;
   26:   fieldLayout: ReportSmartA4FieldLayout;
   27: };
   28: 
   29: export type ReportSmartA4EvaluatedCandidate = {
   30:   candidate: ReportSmartA4Candidate;
   31:   profile: ReportSmartA4Profile;
   32:   measurement: ReportSmartA4Measurement;
   33:   score: number;
   34: };
   35: 
   36: export type ReportSmartA4Plan = {
   37:   candidateId: string;
   38: 
   39:   mode: ReportSmartA4Mode;
   40:   fieldLayout: ReportSmartA4FieldLayout;
   41: 
   42:   fits: boolean;
   43:   score: number;
   44: 
   45:   severity: ReportSmartA4OverflowSeverity;
   46:   dominantRole: ReportSmartA4BlockRole;
   47: 
   48:   overflowPx: number;
   49: 
   50:   preferEvidenceMove: boolean;
   51: };
   52: 
   53: /*
   54:  * ترتيب التجربة مقصود:
   55:  *
   56:  * 1. نحافظ على typography الطبيعية.
   57:  * 2. نعيد توزيع الحقول أولًا.
   58:  * 3. نقلل فراغات الحقول أكثر.
   59:  * 4. بعدها فقط نبدأ بتقليل كثافة النص العامة.
   60:  *
   61:  * لا نحتاج 12 خطة لأن هذا سيجعل ResizeObserver مكلفًا جدًا.
   62:  */
   63: export const REPORT_SMART_A4_CANDIDATES:
   64:   readonly ReportSmartA4Candidate[] = [
   65:   {
   66:     id: "normal-comfortable",
   67:     mode: "normal",
   68:     fieldLayout: "comfortable",
   69:   },
   70:   {
   71:     id: "normal-packed",
   72:     mode: "normal",
   73:     fieldLayout: "packed",
   74:   },
   75:   {
   76:     id: "compact-packed",
   77:     mode: "compact",
   78:     fieldLayout: "packed",
   79:   },
   80:   {
   81:     id: "normal-inline",
   82:     mode: "normal",
   83:     fieldLayout: "inline",
   84:   },
   85:   {
   86:     id: "compact-inline",
   87:     mode: "compact",
   88:     fieldLayout: "inline",
   89:   },
   90:   {
   91:     id: "dense-packed",
   92:     mode: "dense",
   93:     fieldLayout: "packed",
   94:   },
   95:   {
   96:     id: "dense-inline",
   97:     mode: "dense",
   98:     fieldLayout: "inline",
   99:   },
  100:   {
  101:     id: "minimum-safe-packed",
  102:     mode: "minimum-safe",
  103:     fieldLayout: "packed",
  104:   },
  105:   {
  106:     id: "minimum-safe-inline",
  107:     mode: "minimum-safe",
  108:     fieldLayout: "inline",
  109:   },
  110: ] as const;
  111: 
  112: export function getReportSmartA4OverflowSeverity(
  113:   overflowPx: number,
  114: ): ReportSmartA4OverflowSeverity {
  115:   if (overflowPx <= 2) return "none";
  116:   if (overflowPx <= 12) return "tiny";
  117:   if (overflowPx <= 48) return "small";
  118:   if (overflowPx <= 120) return "medium";
  119:   return "large";
  120: }
  121: 
  122: function getModeReadabilityScore(
  123:   mode: ReportSmartA4Mode,
  124: ) {
  125:   switch (mode) {
  126:     case "normal":
  127:       return 100;
  128: 
  129:     case "compact":
  130:       return 88;
  131: 
  132:     case "dense":
  133:       return 69;
  134: 
  135:     case "minimum-safe":
  136:       return 50;
  137: 
  138:     default:
  139:       return 50;
  140:   }
  141: }
  142: 
  143: function getFieldLayoutScore(
  144:   layout: ReportSmartA4FieldLayout,
  145: ) {
  146:   switch (layout) {
  147:     /*
  148:      * Preferred production layout:
  149:      * compact, formal and easy to scan.
  150:      */
  151:     case "packed":
  152:       return 8;
  153: 
  154:     /*
  155:      * Comfortable remains valid for very short reports.
  156:      */
  157:     case "comfortable":
  158:       return 10;
  159: 
  160:     /*
  161:      * Inline is a last-resort space saver.
  162:      * Do not select it merely because it saves a few pixels.
  163:      */
  164:     case "inline":
  165:       return -8;
  166: 
  167:     default:
  168:       return -20;
  169:   }
  170: }
  171: 
  172: export function scoreReportSmartA4Candidate(
  173:   candidate: ReportSmartA4Candidate,
  174:   measurement: ReportSmartA4Measurement,
  175: ) {
  176:   const fitScore =
  177:     measurement.fits ? 1000 : 0;
  178: 
  179:   const readability =
  180:     getModeReadabilityScore(
  181:       candidate.mode,
  182:     );
  183: 
  184:   const fieldLayoutScore =
  185:     getFieldLayoutScore(
  186:       candidate.fieldLayout,
  187:     );
  188: 
  189:   /*
  190:    * إذا الخطة لا تدخل الصفحة نعاقب مقدار overflow.
  191:    * هذا يسمح باختيار أقرب خطة آمنة في حالة عدم نجاح أي خطة.
  192:    */
  193:   const overflowPenalty =
  194:     Math.min(
  195:       700,
  196:       measurement.overflowPx * 2.2,
  197:     );
  198: 
  199:   /*
  200:    * إذا الحقول تستهلك جزءًا كبيرًا من الصفحة،
  201:    * packed/inline يصبحان أكثر منطقية.
  202:    */
  203:   const fieldRatio =
  204:     measurement.fieldHeightPx /
  205:     Math.max(
  206:       1,
  207:       measurement.viewportHeightPx,
  208:     );
  209: 
  210:   const fieldEfficiencyBonus =
  211:     fieldRatio >= 0.28 &&
  212:     candidate.fieldLayout !==
  213:       "comfortable"
  214:       ? 5
  215:       : 0;
  216: 
  217:   return (
  218:     fitScore +
  219:     readability +
  220:     fieldLayoutScore +
  221:     fieldEfficiencyBonus -
  222:     overflowPenalty
  223:   );
  224: }
  225: 
  226: export function chooseReportSmartA4Plan(
  227:   evaluated:
  228:     ReportSmartA4EvaluatedCandidate[],
  229: ): ReportSmartA4Plan {
  230:   if (!evaluated.length) {
  231:     return {
  232:       candidateId:
  233:         "minimum-safe-inline",
  234: 
  235:       mode: "minimum-safe",
  236:       fieldLayout: "inline",
  237: 
  238:       fits: false,
  239:       score: 0,
  240: 
  241:       severity: "large",
  242:       dominantRole: "general",
  243: 
  244:       overflowPx: 0,
  245: 
  246:       preferEvidenceMove: false,
  247:     };
  248:   }
  249: 
  250:   const fitting =
  251:     evaluated.filter(
  252:       (item) =>
  253:         item.measurement.fits,
  254:     );
  255: 
  256:   const pool =
  257:     fitting.length
  258:       ? fitting
  259:       : evaluated;
  260: 
  261:   const selected = [...pool].sort(
  262:     (a, b) => b.score - a.score,
  263:   )[0];
  264: 
  265:   const measurement =
  266:     selected.measurement;
  267: 
  268:   const severity =
  269:     getReportSmartA4OverflowSeverity(
  270:       measurement.overflowPx,
  271:     );
  272: 
  273:   /*
  274:    * إذا التقرير لا يدخل إلا بعد dense/minimum-safe
  275:    * بينما يوجد شاهد في الصفحة، نفضل نقل الشاهد أولًا.
  276:    *
  277:    * هذا هو التغيير المهم:
  278:    * جودة النص والتوقيع أعلى أولوية من إبقاء كل الصور
  279:    * في الصفحة الرئيسية.
  280:    */
  281:   const aggressiveDensity =
  282:     selected.candidate.mode ===
  283:       "dense" ||
  284:     selected.candidate.mode ===
  285:       "minimum-safe";
  286: 
  287:   const hasEvidence =
  288:     measurement.evidenceHeightPx > 0;
  289: 
  290:   const preferEvidenceMove =
  291:     hasEvidence &&
  292:     (
  293:       !measurement.fits ||
  294:       aggressiveDensity
  295:     );
  296: 
  297:   return {
  298:     candidateId:
  299:       selected.candidate.id,
  300: 
  301:     mode:
  302:       selected.candidate.mode,
  303: 
  304:     fieldLayout:
  305:       selected.candidate
  306:         .fieldLayout,
  307: 
  308:     fits:
  309:       measurement.fits,
  310: 
  311:     score:
  312:       Math.round(
  313:         selected.score * 10,
  314:       ) / 10,
  315: 
  316:     severity,
  317: 
  318:     dominantRole:
  319:       measurement.dominantRole,
  320: 
  321:     overflowPx:
  322:       measurement.overflowPx,
  323: 
  324:     preferEvidenceMove,
  325:   };
  326: }

FILE: components\report-engine\design-renderers\smart-layout\report-smart-field-layout.ts
============================================================
    1: import type { ReportValueItem } from "../designs/report-design-component-types";
    2: 
    3: export type ReportSmartFieldKind =
    4:   | "short"
    5:   | "medium"
    6:   | "long"
    7:   | "list";
    8: 
    9: export type ReportSmartFieldProfile =
   10:   | "short-heavy"
   11:   | "balanced"
   12:   | "long-heavy";
   13: 
   14: function normalizeText(value: unknown) {
   15:   return String(value || "")
   16:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
   17:     .trim();
   18: }
   19: 
   20: function getValueItems(item: ReportValueItem) {
   21:   if (
   22:     Array.isArray(item.valueItems) &&
   23:     item.valueItems.length
   24:   ) {
   25:     return item.valueItems
   26:       .map(normalizeText)
   27:       .filter(Boolean);
   28:   }
   29: 
   30:   if (Array.isArray(item.value)) {
   31:     return item.value
   32:       .map(normalizeText)
   33:       .filter(Boolean);
   34:   }
   35: 
   36:   const value = normalizeText(item.value);
   37: 
   38:   return value ? [value] : [];
   39: }
   40: 
   41: export function getReportSmartFieldMetrics(
   42:   item: ReportValueItem,
   43: ) {
   44:   const label = normalizeText(item.label);
   45:   const values = getValueItems(item);
   46: 
   47:   const valueCharacters = values.reduce(
   48:     (sum, value) => sum + value.length,
   49:     0,
   50:   );
   51: 
   52:   const longestValue = values.reduce(
   53:     (longest, value) =>
   54:       Math.max(longest, value.length),
   55:     0,
   56:   );
   57: 
   58:   return {
   59:     labelCharacters: label.length,
   60:     valueCharacters,
   61:     longestValue,
   62:     totalCharacters:
   63:       label.length + valueCharacters,
   64:     valuesCount: values.length,
   65:   };
   66: }
   67: 
   68: export function classifyReportSmartField(
   69:   item: ReportValueItem,
   70: ): ReportSmartFieldKind {
   71:   const m =
   72:     getReportSmartFieldMetrics(item);
   73: 
   74:   /*
   75:    * SHORT
   76:    * مناسب لربع صف تقريبًا.
   77:    */
   78:   if (
   79:     m.valuesCount <= 1 &&
   80:     m.longestValue <= 36 &&
   81:     m.labelCharacters <= 36 &&
   82:     m.totalCharacters <= 68
   83:   ) {
   84:     return "short";
   85:   }
   86: 
   87:   /*
   88:    * LIST
   89:    * القائمة الصغيرة ليست Full Width تلقائيًا.
   90:    * نسمح لها بنصف صف إذا كانت قصيرة.
   91:    */
   92:   if (
   93:     m.valuesCount > 1 &&
   94:     m.valuesCount <= 3 &&
   95:     m.longestValue <= 52 &&
   96:     m.valueCharacters <= 115
   97:   ) {
   98:     return "medium";
   99:   }
  100: 
  101:   /*
  102:    * MEDIUM
  103:    * نصف صف، حتى لو احتاج سطرين أو ثلاثة.
  104:    */
  105:   if (
  106:     m.valuesCount <= 1 &&
  107:     m.longestValue <= 125 &&
  108:     m.totalCharacters <= 165
  109:   ) {
  110:     return "medium";
  111:   }
  112: 
  113:   /*
  114:    * القائمة الأكبر فقط تعتبر list كاملة.
  115:    */
  116:   if (m.valuesCount > 1) {
  117:     return "list";
  118:   }
  119: 
  120:   return "long";
  121: }
  122: 
  123: export function getReportSmartFieldProfile(
  124:   items: ReportValueItem[],
  125: ): ReportSmartFieldProfile {
  126:   if (!items.length) {
  127:     return "balanced";
  128:   }
  129: 
  130:   const counts = {
  131:     short: 0,
  132:     medium: 0,
  133:     long: 0,
  134:     list: 0,
  135:   };
  136: 
  137:   for (const item of items) {
  138:     counts[
  139:       classifyReportSmartField(item)
  140:     ] += 1;
  141:   }
  142: 
  143:   const wideCount =
  144:     counts.long + counts.list;
  145: 
  146:   if (
  147:     wideCount >=
  148:     Math.ceil(items.length * 0.55)
  149:   ) {
  150:     return "long-heavy";
  151:   }
  152: 
  153:   if (
  154:     counts.short + counts.medium >=
  155:     Math.ceil(items.length * 0.7)
  156:   ) {
  157:     return "short-heavy";
  158:   }
  159: 
  160:   return "balanced";
  161: }

   57: function isSignatureBlock(block: any) {
   58:   const kind =
   59:     String(block?.kind || "").trim();
   60: 
   61:   const smartKind =
   62:     String(
   63:       block?.settings?.smartBlockKind || "",
   64:     ).trim();
   65: 
   66:   const title =
   67:     String(block?.title || "").trim();
   68: 
   69:   return (
   70:     kind === "signature-grid" ||
   71:     kind === "signatures" ||
   72:     kind === "approval-signatures" ||
   73:     smartKind === "signature-grid" ||
   74:     smartKind === "signatures" ||
   75:     title.includes("توقيع") ||
   76:     title.includes("اعتماد") ||
   77:     Array.isArray(block?.signatures)
   78:   );
   79: }

   81: function getSignatureBlocks(page: any) {
   82:   const blocks =
   83:     Array.isArray(page?.blocks)
   84:       ? page.blocks
   85:       : [];
   86: 
   87:   return blocks.filter(
   88:     isSignatureBlock,
   89:   );

   92: function getEvidenceBlock(page: any) {

   96:       : [];

   99:     blocks.find(
  100:       (block: any) =>
  101:         block?.kind ===
  102:         "evidence-gallery",
  103:     ) || null
  104:   );
  105: }

  107: function getCompositionResetKey(

  127: function makePrimaryPage(
  128:   page: ReportPhysicalPageModel | undefined,
  129:   evidenceBlock: any,
  130:   primaryEvidenceCount: number,
  131:   keepSignatureOnPrimary: boolean,
  132: ) {
  133:   if (!page) {
  134:     return page;
  135:   }
  136: 
  137:   const originalBlocks =
  138:     Array.isArray(page.blocks)
  139:       ? page.blocks
  140:       : [];
  141: 
  142:   let blocks =
  143:     keepSignatureOnPrimary
  144:       ? [...originalBlocks]
  145:       : originalBlocks.filter(
  146:           (block: any) =>
  147:             !isSignatureBlock(block),
  148:         );
  149: 
  150:   if (!evidenceBlock) {
  151:     return {
  152:       ...page,
  153:       blocks,
  154:     };
  155:   }
  156: 
  157:   /*
  158:    * Zero evidence means:
  159:    * remove evidence-gallery from the primary page.
  160:    *
  161:    * AutoEvidencePages still receives the ORIGINAL page,
  162:    * so no evidence is lost.
  163:    */
  164:   if (primaryEvidenceCount <= 0) {
  165:     blocks = blocks.filter(
  166:       (block: any) =>
  167:         block?.id !==
  168:         evidenceBlock.id,
  169:     );
  170: 
  171:     return {
  172:       ...page,
  173:       blocks,
  174:     };
  175:   }
  176: 
  177:   blocks = blocks.map(
  178:     (block: any) =>
  179:       block?.id === evidenceBlock.id
  180:         ? {
  181:             ...block,
  182:             evidenceStartIndex: getEvidenceStartIndex(evidenceBlock),
  183:             evidenceLimit:
  184:               primaryEvidenceCount,
  185:           }
  186:         : block,
  187:   );
  188: 
  189:   return {
  190:     ...page,
  191:     blocks,
  192:   };
  193: }
  194:

  202:     id:

  206:       "اعتماد التقرير",
  207: 
  208:     kind:
  209:       "signature",
  210: 
  211:     corePhysicalPageId:
  212:       page.corePhysicalPageId || page.id,
  213: 
  214:     physicalPageRole:
  215:       "signature" as const,
  216: 
  217:     sourcePageIds:
  218:       [...page.sourcePageIds],
  219: 
  220:     blocks:

  222:         (block, index) => ({
  223:           ...block,
  224: 
  225:           id:

  227:

  229:            * This page is already an explicit overflow page.
  230:            */
  231:           placement:
  232:             "flow",
  233:         }),
  234:       ),
  235:   };
