   91: 
   92: function normalizePlacement(block: LogicalReportBlock) {
   93:   return String(block?.placement || "flow").trim() || "flow";
   94: }
   95: 
   96: function isVisibleBlock(block: LogicalReportBlock) {
   97:   return Boolean(block) && block.visible !== false;
   98: }
   99: 
  100: function isEvidenceBlock(block: LogicalReportBlock) {
  101:   return String(block?.kind || "").trim() === "evidence-gallery";
  102: }
  103: 
  104: function isSignatureBlock(block: LogicalReportBlock) {
  105:   const kind = String(block?.kind || "").trim();
  106:   const smartKind = String(
  107:     block?.settings?.smartBlockKind || "",
  108:   ).trim();
  109:   const title = String(block?.title || "").trim();
  110: 
  111:   return (
  112:     kind === "signature-grid" ||
  113:     kind === "signatures" ||
  114:     kind === "approval-signatures" ||
  115:     smartKind === "signature-grid" ||
  116:     smartKind === "signatures" ||
  117:     title.includes("توقيع") ||
  118:     title.includes("اعتماد") ||
  119:     Array.isArray(block?.signatures)
  120:   );
  121: }
  122: 
  123: function uniqueStrings(values: string[]) {
  124:   return Array.from(
  125:     new Set(
  126:       values
  127:         .map((value) => String(value || "").trim())
  128:         .filter(Boolean),
  129:     ),
  130:   );
  131: }
  132: 
  133: /** Normalize deterministic physical identities without discarding content. */
  134: function canonicalizePhysicalPages(pages: readonly PhysicalPage[]) {
  135:   return pages.map((page, index) => {
  136:     const id = `smart-physical-${index + 1}`;
  137: 
  138:     return {
  139:       ...page,
  140:       id,
  141:       corePhysicalPageId: id,
  142:       physicalPageRole: "primary" as const,
  143:       blocks: [...page.blocks],
  144:       sourcePageIds: uniqueStrings(page.sourcePageIds),
  145:     };
  146:   });
  147: }
  148: 
  149: function buildLogicalPageModels(pages: LogicalPage[]): LogicalPageModel[] {
  150:   const sourcePages = pages.length > 0
  151:     ? pages
  152:     : [{
  153:         id: "logical-page-1",
  154:         title: "التقرير",
  155:         kind: "content",
  156:         blocks: [],
  157:       }];
  158: 
  159:   return sourcePages.map((page, pageIndex) => {
  160:     const regularItems: FlowItem[] = [];
  161:     const signatureItems: FlowItem[] = [];
  162:     const evidenceItems: FlowItem[] = [];
  163:     const sourcePageId =
  164:       String(page?.id || `logical-page-${pageIndex + 1}`);
  165: 
  166:     const sourcePageTitle =
  167:       String(page?.title || `صفحة ${pageIndex + 1}`);
  168: 
  169:     const blocks = Array.isArray(page?.blocks)
  170:       ? page.blocks.filter(isVisibleBlock)
  171:       : [];
  172: 
  173:     const fixedBlocks = blocks.filter(
  174:       (block) =>
  175:         normalizePlacement(block) !== "flow" &&
  176:         !isSignatureBlock(block),
  177:     );
  178: 
  179:     blocks
  180:       .filter(
  181:         (block) =>
  182:           normalizePlacement(block) === "flow" ||
  183:           isSignatureBlock(block),
  184:       )
  185:       .flatMap((block) => paginateStructuredTableBlock(block))
  186:       .forEach((block) => {
  187:         const item: FlowItem = {
  188:           block,
  189:           sourcePageId,
  190:           sourcePageTitle,
  191:         };
  192: 
  193:         if (isSignatureBlock(block)) {
  194:           signatureItems.push(item);
  195:           return;
  196:         }
  197: 
  198:         if (isEvidenceBlock(block)) {
  199:           evidenceItems.push(item);
  200:           return;
  201:         }
  202: 
  203:         regularItems.push(item);
  204:       });
  205:     return {
  206:       sourcePageId,
  207:       sourcePageTitle,
  208:       kind: String(page?.kind || "content"),
  209:       regularItems,
  210:       signatureItems,
  211:       evidenceItems,
  212:       fixedBlocks,
  213:     };
  214:   });
  215: }
  216: 
  217: function buildSemanticInputFingerprint(
  218:   designId: ReportDesignId,
  219:   pages: LogicalPage[],
  220:   context: Record<string, string>,
  221:   previewCase: PreviewCaseData | null,
  222:   fallbackPageLabel: string,
  223:   suppressAutoEvidencePages: boolean,
  224: ) {
  225:   return createSemanticInputFingerprint({
  226:     designId,
  227:     fallbackPageLabel,
  228:     suppressAutoEvidencePages,
  229:     context,
  230:     pages,
  231:     previewCase,
  232:   });
  233: }
  234: 
  235: function makePhysicalPage(
  236:   index: number,
  237:   logicalPage: LogicalPageModel,
  238:   blocks: LogicalReportBlock[],
  239: ): PhysicalPage {
  240:   return {
  241:     id: `smart-physical-${index + 1}`,
  242:     title: logicalPage.sourcePageTitle,
  243:     kind: logicalPage.kind,
  244:     blocks: mergeAdjacentCommitteeItemsTableBlocks(blocks),
  245:     sourcePageIds: [logicalPage.sourcePageId],
  246:     corePhysicalPageId: `smart-physical-${index + 1}`,
  247:     physicalPageRole: "primary",
  248:   };
  249: }
  250: 
  251: function addLogicalPageFixedBlocks(
  252:   physicalPage: PhysicalPage,
  253:   logicalPage: LogicalPageModel,
  254:   includeFixedBlocks: boolean,
  255: ) {
  256:   if (!includeFixedBlocks || !logicalPage.fixedBlocks.length) {
  257:     return physicalPage;
  258:   }
  259: 
  260:   return {
  261:     ...physicalPage,
  262:     blocks: [
  263:       ...physicalPage.blocks,
  264:       ...logicalPage.fixedBlocks,
  265:     ],
  266:   };
  267: }
  268: 
  269: function appendLogicalPageSpecialBlocks(
  270:   pages: PhysicalPage[],
  271:   logicalPage: LogicalPageModel,
  272: ) {
  273:   const result =
  274:     pages.length > 0
  275:       ? pages.map((page) => ({
  276:           ...page,
  277:           blocks: [...page.blocks],
  278:           sourcePageIds: [...page.sourcePageIds],
  279:         }))
  280:       : [
  281:           {
  282:             id: "smart-physical-1",
  283:             title: logicalPage.sourcePageTitle,
  284:             kind: logicalPage.kind,
  285:             blocks: [...logicalPage.fixedBlocks],
  286:             sourcePageIds: [logicalPage.sourcePageId],
  287:             corePhysicalPageId: "smart-physical-1",
  288:             physicalPageRole: "primary",
  289:           } satisfies PhysicalPage,
  290:         ];
  291: 
  292:   const lastIndex = result.length - 1;
  293:   const last = result[lastIndex];
  294: 
  295:   /*
  296:    * Evidence is intentionally placed BEFORE signature.
  297:    *
  298:    * SmartReportPageComposer then decides:
  299:    * 2 evidence / 1 evidence / 0 evidence on this page,
  300:    * while the remaining evidence goes to AutoEvidencePages.
  301:    */
  302:   const primaryEvidenceItem = logicalPage.evidenceItems.find(
  303:     (item) => Number(item.block?.evidenceStartIndex || 0) <= 0,
  304:   ) || logicalPage.evidenceItems[0];
  305:   const evidenceBlocks = primaryEvidenceItem
  306:     ? [primaryEvidenceItem.block]
  307:     : [];
  308:   const evidenceContinuationItems = logicalPage.evidenceItems.filter(
  309:     (item) => item !== primaryEvidenceItem,
  310:   );
  311: 
  312:   const signatureBlocks =
  313:     logicalPage.signatureItems.map((item) => item.block);
  314: 
  315:   result[lastIndex] = {
  316:     ...last,
  317:     sourcePageIds: [logicalPage.sourcePageId],
  318:     blocks: [
  319:       ...last.blocks,
  320:       ...evidenceBlocks,
  321:       ...signatureBlocks,
  322:     ],
  323:   };
  324: 
  325:   evidenceContinuationItems.forEach((item) => {
  326:     result.push(
  327:       makePhysicalPage(
  328:         result.length,
  329:         logicalPage,
  330:         [item.block],
  331:       ),
  332:     );
  333:   });
  334: 
  335:   return result;
  336: }
  337: 
  338: function buildComposedPhysicalPages(
  339:   corePages: readonly PhysicalPage[],
  340:   compositions: Readonly<Record<string, ReportPhysicalPageComposition>>,
  341:   logicalPages: readonly LogicalPageModel[],
  342: ) {
  343:   const logicalTitles = new Map(
  344:     logicalPages.map((page) => [page.sourcePageId, page.sourcePageTitle]),
  345:   );
  346:   const logicalPageCounters = new Map<string, number>();
  347:   const seenPhysicalIds = new Set<string>();
  348: 
  349:   return corePages.flatMap((corePage) => {
  350:     const composedPages =
  351:       compositions[corePage.id]?.pages?.length
  352:         ? compositions[corePage.id].pages
  353:         : [corePage];
  354: 
  355:     return composedPages.flatMap((page) => {
  356:       const id = String(page.id || "").trim();
  357: 
  358:       if (!id || seenPhysicalIds.has(id)) {
  359:         return [];
  360:       }
  361: 
  362:       seenPhysicalIds.add(id);
  363:       const sourcePageIds = uniqueStrings(
  364:         page.sourcePageIds.length
  365:           ? page.sourcePageIds
  366:           : corePage.sourcePageIds,
  367:       );
  368:       const sourceLogicalPageId = sourcePageIds[0] || "logical-page-1";
  369:       const physicalIndexWithinLogicalPage =
  370:         (logicalPageCounters.get(sourceLogicalPageId) || 0) + 1;
  371:       logicalPageCounters.set(
  372:         sourceLogicalPageId,
  373:         physicalIndexWithinLogicalPage,
  374:       );
  375:       const sourceTitle =
  376:         logicalTitles.get(sourceLogicalPageId) || corePage.title || "التقرير";
  377:       const title =
  378:         physicalIndexWithinLogicalPage === 1
  379:           ? sourceTitle
  380:           : `${sourceTitle} - صفحة ${physicalIndexWithinLogicalPage}`;
  381: 
  382:       return [{
  383:         ...page,
  384:         title,
  385:         sourcePageIds,
  386:         corePhysicalPageId: corePage.id,
  387:         physicalPageIndex: seenPhysicalIds.size - 1,
  388:         physicalIndexWithinLogicalPage,
  389:       }];
  390:     });
  391:   });
  392: }
  393: 
  394: function buildPhysicalNavigationItems(
  395:   pages: readonly PhysicalPage[],
  396: ): ReportTwoPhysicalNavigationItem[] {
  397:   return pages.map((page, index) => ({
  398:     physicalPageId: page.id,
  399:     corePhysicalPageId: page.corePhysicalPageId,
  400:     sourceLogicalPageId: page.sourcePageIds[0] || "logical-page-1",
  401:     sourcePageIds: [...page.sourcePageIds],
  402:     label: page.title,
  403:     physicalPageIndex: index,
  404:     physicalIndexWithinLogicalPage:
  405:       page.physicalIndexWithinLogicalPage || 1,
  406:     role: page.physicalPageRole,
  407:   }));
  408: }
  409: 
  410: export function SmartPhysicalReportComposer({
  411:   designId,
  412:   pages,
  413:   activePageId,
  414:   activePhysicalPageId,
  415:   context,
  416:   previewCase,
  417:   fallbackPageLabel = "التقرير",
  418:   renderMode = "stack",
  419:   suppressAutoEvidencePages = false,
  420:   onPhysicalPagesChange,
  421: }: SmartPhysicalReportComposerProps) {
  422:   const logicalPageModels = useMemo(
  423:     () => buildLogicalPageModels(pages || []),
  424:     [pages],
  425:   );
  426: 
  427:   const semanticInputFingerprint = useMemo(
  428:     () =>
  429:       buildSemanticInputFingerprint(
  430:         designId,
  431:         pages || [],
  432:         context,
  433:         previewCase,
  434:         fallbackPageLabel,
  435:         suppressAutoEvidencePages,
  436:       ),
  437:     [
  438:       context,
  439:       designId,
  440:       fallbackPageLabel,
  441:       pages,
  442:       previewCase,
  443:       suppressAutoEvidencePages,
  444:     ],
  445:   );
  446: 
  447:   const semanticFingerprintParts = useMemo(
  448:     () => ({
  449:       content: semanticInputFingerprint,
  450:       design: createSemanticInputFingerprint(designId),
  451:       evidence: createSemanticInputFingerprint({
  452:         blocks: pages.flatMap((page) =>
  453:           (page.blocks || []).filter(isEvidenceBlock),
  454:         ),
  455:         evidences: previewCase?.evidences || [],
  456:       }),
  457:       header: createSemanticInputFingerprint({
  458:         fallbackPageLabel,
  459:         settings: Object.fromEntries(
  460:           Object.entries(context).filter(([key]) =>
  461:             key.startsWith("identity.") ||
  462:             key.startsWith("report."),
  463:           ),
  464:         ),
  465:       }),
  466:       pageStructure: createSemanticInputFingerprint(
  467:         pages.map((page) => ({
  468:           blocks: (page.blocks || []).map((block) => ({
  469:             id: block?.id,
  470:             kind: block?.kind,
  471:             placement: block?.placement,
  472:           })),
  473:           id: page.id,
  474:           kind: page.kind,
  475:         })),
  476:       ),
  477:       signatures: createSemanticInputFingerprint({
  478:         blocks: pages.flatMap((page) =>
  479:           (page.blocks || []).filter(isSignatureBlock),
  480:         ),
  481:         context: Object.fromEntries(
  482:           Object.entries(context).filter(([key]) =>
  483:             key.toLowerCase().includes("signature"),
  484:           ),
  485:         ),
  486:       }),
  487:     }),
  488:     [
  489:       context,
  490:       designId,
  491:       fallbackPageLabel,
  492:       pages,
  493:       previewCase?.evidences,
  494:       semanticInputFingerprint,
  495:     ],
  496:   );
  497: 
  498:   const semanticFingerprintPartsFingerprint = useMemo(
  499:     () => createSemanticInputFingerprint(semanticFingerprintParts),
  500:     [semanticFingerprintParts],
  501:   );
  502: 
  503:   const measurementHostRef =
  504:     useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  505: 
  506:   const outputHostRef =
  507:     useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  508: 
  509:   const [physicalPages, setPhysicalPages] =
  510:     useState<PhysicalPage[]>([]);
  511: 
  512:   const [finalPhysicalPages, setFinalPhysicalPages] =
  513:     useState<PhysicalPage[]>([]);
  514: 
  515:   const [pageCompositions, setPageCompositions] =
  516:     useState<Record<string, ReportPhysicalPageComposition>>({});
  517: 
  518:   const [acceptedItems, setAcceptedItems] =
  519:     useState<FlowItem[]>([]);
  520: 
  521:   const [logicalPageIndex, setLogicalPageIndex] =
  522:     useState(0);
  523: 
  524:   const [currentLogicalPhysicalPages, setCurrentLogicalPhysicalPages] =
  525:     useState<PhysicalPage[]>([]);
  526: 
  527:   const [cursor, setCursor] =
  528:     useState(0);
  529: 
  530:   const [candidateVersion, setCandidateVersion] =
  531:     useState(0);
  532: 
  533:   const [done, setDone] =
  534:     useState(false);
  535: 
  536:   const [planningPhase, setPlanningPhase] =
  537:     useState<PhysicalPlanningPhase>("DIRTY");
  538: 
  539:   const [plannedResetKey, setPlannedResetKey] =
  540:     useState(semanticInputFingerprint);
  541: 
  542:   const planningPhaseRef =
  543:     useRef<PhysicalPlanningPhase>("DIRTY");
  544: 
  545:   const outputStabilityRef = useRef({
  546:     signature: "",
  547:     count: 0,
  548:     attempts: 0,
  549:   });
  550: 
  551:   const finalizedPlanRef = useRef<{
  552:     semanticInputFingerprint: string;
  553:     layoutResultFingerprint: string;
  554:     pages: readonly PhysicalPage[];
  555:   } | null>(null);
  556: 
  557:   const previousSemanticPartsRef =
  558:     useRef<typeof semanticFingerprintParts | null>(null);
  559: 
  560:   const [invalidationReason, setInvalidationReason] =
  561:     useState<PhysicalInvalidationReason>("initial");
  562: 
  563:   const fontsSettledRef = useRef(false);
  564:   const settledAssetSourcesRef = useRef(new Set<string>());
  565:   const outputResizeFingerprintRef = useRef("");
  566: 
  567:   const decisionLockRef =
  568:     useRef(false);
  569: 
  570:   /*
  571:    * New report/template data => rebuild physical pagination.
  572:    */
  573:   useEffect(() => {
  574:     const currentParts = JSON.parse(
  575:       semanticFingerprintPartsFingerprint,
  576:     ) as typeof semanticFingerprintParts;
  577:     const previousParts = previousSemanticPartsRef.current;
  578: 
  579:     const nextInvalidationReason: PhysicalInvalidationReason = !previousParts
  580:       ? "initial"
  581:       : previousParts.design !== currentParts.design
  582:         ? "design-change"
  583:         : previousParts.pageStructure !==
  584:             currentParts.pageStructure
  585:           ? "page-structure-change"
  586:           : previousParts.evidence !== currentParts.evidence
  587:             ? "evidence-change"
  588:             : previousParts.signatures !==
  589:                 currentParts.signatures
  590:               ? "signature-change"
  591:               : previousParts.header !== currentParts.header
  592:                 ? "header-settings-change"
  593:                 : "content-change";
  594: 
  595:     setInvalidationReason(nextInvalidationReason);
  596: 
  597:     previousSemanticPartsRef.current = currentParts;
  598:     finalizedPlanRef.current = null;
  599:     decisionLockRef.current = false;
  600: 
  601:     planningPhaseRef.current = "DIRTY";
  602:     setPlanningPhase("DIRTY");
  603:     setPhysicalPages([]);
  604:     setFinalPhysicalPages([]);
  605:     setPageCompositions({});
  606:     setAcceptedItems([]);
  607:     setLogicalPageIndex(0);
  608:     setCurrentLogicalPhysicalPages([]);
  609:     setCursor(0);
  610:     setCandidateVersion(
  611:       (current) => current + 1,
  612:     );
  613:     setDone(false);
  614:     planningPhaseRef.current = "PLANNING";
  615:     setPlanningPhase("PLANNING");
  616:     outputStabilityRef.current = {
  617:       signature: "",
  618:       count: 0,
  619:       attempts: 0,
  620:     };
  621:     settledAssetSourcesRef.current.clear();
  622:     outputResizeFingerprintRef.current = "";
  623:     setPlannedResetKey(semanticInputFingerprint);
  624:   }, [
  625:     semanticInputFingerprint,
  626:     semanticFingerprintPartsFingerprint,
  627:   ]);
  628: 
  629:   const activeLogicalPage =
  630:     logicalPageModels[logicalPageIndex] ?? logicalPageModels[0]!;
  631: 
  632:   const completeCurrentLogicalPage = useCallback(
  633:     (corePages: PhysicalPage[]) => {
  634:       if (!activeLogicalPage) {
  635:         setDone(true);
  636:         return;
  637:       }
  638: 
  639:       const logicalPhysicalPages = corePages.length > 0
  640:         ? corePages
  641:         : [
  642:             makePhysicalPage(
  643:               physicalPages.length,
  644:               activeLogicalPage,
  645:               [...activeLogicalPage.fixedBlocks],
  646:             ),
  647:           ];
  648: 
  649:       const completedLogicalPages =
  650:         appendLogicalPageSpecialBlocks(
  651:           logicalPhysicalPages,
  652:           activeLogicalPage,
  653:         );
  654: 
  655:       setPhysicalPages(
  656:         canonicalizePhysicalPages([
  657:           ...physicalPages,
  658:           ...completedLogicalPages,
  659:         ]),
  660:       );
  661:       setCurrentLogicalPhysicalPages([]);
  662:       setAcceptedItems([]);
  663:       setCursor(0);
  664: 
  665:       if (logicalPageIndex >= logicalPageModels.length - 1) {
  666:         setDone(true);
  667:       } else {
  668:         setLogicalPageIndex((current) => current + 1);
  669:         setCandidateVersion((current) => current + 1);
  670:       }
  671:     },
  672:     [
  673:       activeLogicalPage,
  674:       logicalPageIndex,
  675:       logicalPageModels.length,
  676:       physicalPages,
  677:     ],
  678:   );
  679: 
  680:   const hasRegularItems =
  681:     activeLogicalPage.regularItems.length > 0;
  682: 
  683:   const nextItem =
  684:     activeLogicalPage.regularItems[cursor];
  685: 
  686:   const candidateItems = useMemo(() => {
  687:     if (done) {
  688:       return [];
  689:     }
  690: 
  691:     if (!hasRegularItems) {
  692:       return [];
  693:     }
  694: 
  695:     if (!nextItem) {
  696:       return acceptedItems;
  697:     }
  698: 
  699:     return [
  700:       ...acceptedItems,
  701:       nextItem,
  702:     ];
  703:   }, [
  704:     acceptedItems,
  705:     done,
  706:     hasRegularItems,
  707:     nextItem,
  708:   ]);
  709: 
  710:   const candidateBlocks = useMemo(() => {
  711:     if (!hasRegularItems) {
  712:       /*
  713:        * Signature-only report:
  714:        * signature is the core content because there are no
  715:        * regular blocks to pack.
  716:        */
  717:       const signatureBlocks = activeLogicalPage.signatureItems.map(
  718:         (item) => item.block,
  719:       );
  720: 
  721:       return [
  722:         ...signatureBlocks,
  723:         ...(currentLogicalPhysicalPages.length === 0
  724:           ? activeLogicalPage.fixedBlocks
  725:           : []),
  726:       ];
  727:     }
  728: 
  729:     /*
  730:      * IMPORTANT:
  731:      *
  732:      * Physical pagination packs REGULAR CONTENT ONLY.
  733:      *
  734:      * Do not inject signature into the final regular candidate.
  735:      * Doing that caused this failure:
  736:      *
  737:      * fields                         -> fits
  738:      * fields + narrative + signature -> fails
  739:      * engine closes page at fields   -> WRONG
  740:      *
  741:      * Now:
  742:      *
  743:      * fields + narrative + other regular blocks
  744:      * are packed using the real Smart A4 measurement first.
  745:      *
  746:      * Evidence and signature are appended only after the core
  747:      * physical pages have been built, and SmartReportPageComposer
  748:      * negotiates their final placement.
  749:      */
  750:     return [
  751:       ...mergeAdjacentCommitteeItemsTableBlocks(
  752:         candidateItems.map((item) => item.block),
  753:       ),
  754:       ...(currentLogicalPhysicalPages.length === 0
  755:         ? activeLogicalPage.fixedBlocks
  756:         : []),
  757:     ];
  758:   }, [
  759:     activeLogicalPage,
  760:     candidateItems,
  761:     currentLogicalPhysicalPages.length,
  762:     hasRegularItems,
  763:   ]);
  764: 
  765:   const candidatePage = useMemo(
  766:     () => ({
  767:       id:
  768:         `smart-measure-${candidateVersion}-${logicalPageIndex}-${cursor}-${acceptedItems.length}`,
  769: 
  770:       title: activeLogicalPage.sourcePageTitle || fallbackPageLabel,
  771: 
  772:       kind: "content",
  773: 
  774:       blocks: candidateBlocks,
  775:     }),
  776:     [
  777:       candidateVersion,
  778:       logicalPageIndex,
  779:       cursor,
  780:       acceptedItems.length,
  781:       fallbackPageLabel,
  782:       candidateBlocks,
  783:       activeLogicalPage.sourcePageTitle,
  784:     ],
  785:   );
  786: 
  787:   /*
  788:    * Special case:
  789:    * no regular blocks at all.
  790:    */
  791:   useEffect(() => {
  792:     if (
  793:       done ||
  794:       hasRegularItems
  795:     ) {
  796:       return;
  797:     }
  798: 
  799:     /*
  800:      * We still render the hidden measurement candidate first
  801:      * if signatures exist.
  802:      */
  803:     if (activeLogicalPage.signatureItems.length > 0) {
  804:       return;
  805:     }
  806: 
  807:     completeCurrentLogicalPage([]);
  808:   }, [
  809:     activeLogicalPage.signatureItems.length,
  810:     completeCurrentLogicalPage,
  811:     done,
  812:     hasRegularItems,
  813:   ]);
  814: 
  815:   /*
  816:    * DOM-driven physical page packing.
  817:    *
  818:    * Greedy but measured:
  819:    *
  820:    * current accepted blocks + next block
  821:    *              ↓
  822:    *     real A4 Smart engine
  823:    *              ↓
  824:    * fits? yes => accept block
  825:    * fits? no  => finalize previous physical page
  826:    */
  827:   useEffect(() => {
  828:     const host =
  829:       measurementHostRef.current;
  830: 
  831:     if (
  832:       !host ||
  833:       done
  834:     ) {
  835:       return;
  836:     }
  837: 
  838:     let frame: number | null = null;
  839: 
  840:     const inspect = () => {
  841:       if (frame !== null) {
  842:         window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  843:       }
  844: 
  845:       frame =
  846:         window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
  847:           frame = null;
  848: 
  849:           if (decisionLockRef.current) {
  850:             return;
  851:           }
  852: 
  853:           const viewport =
  854:             host.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
  855:               ".report-smart-a4-content",
  856:             );
  857: 
  858:           if (!viewport) {
  859:             return;
  860:           }
  861: 
  862:           /*
  863:            * score=0 is the initial React state.
  864:            * Wait until the internal Smart A4 multi-plan engine
  865:            * has completed a real measurement.
  866:            */
  867:           const scoreValue =
  868:             Number(
  869:               viewport.getAttribute(
  870:                 "data-smart-a4-score",
  871:               ) || "0",
  872:             );
  873: 
  874:           const candidateId =
  875:             viewport.getAttribute(
  876:               "data-smart-a4-candidate",
  877:             );
  878: 
  879:           const smartA4Phase =
  880:             viewport.getAttribute(
  881:               "data-smart-a4-phase",
  882:             );
  883: 
  884:           if (
  885:             smartA4Phase !== "FROZEN" ||
  886:             !candidateId ||
  887:             !Number.isFinite(scoreValue) ||
  888:             scoreValue === 0
  889:           ) {
  890:             return;
  891:           }
  892: 
  893:           const minimumSafeSelected =
  894:             viewport.dataset.reportDensity === "minimum-safe";
  895:           const fits =
  896:             viewport.dataset.reportOverflow === "fit" &&
  897:             !(minimumSafeSelected && acceptedItems.length > 0);
  898: 
  899:           decisionLockRef.current = true;
  900: 
  901:           /*
  902:            * ---------------------------------------------------
  903:            * NO REGULAR CONTENT, SIGNATURE-ONLY CORE PAGE
  904:            * ---------------------------------------------------
  905:            */
  906:           if (!hasRegularItems) {
  907:             completeCurrentLogicalPage([]);
  908: 
  909:             decisionLockRef.current = false;
  910:             return;
  911:           }
  912: 
  913:           const isLastItem =
  914:             cursor >=
  915:             activeLogicalPage.regularItems.length - 1;
  916: 
  917:           /*
  918:            * Candidate fits:
  919:            * accept next block.
  920:            */
  921:           if (fits) {
  922:             const nextAccepted =
  923:               nextItem
  924:                 ? [
  925:                     ...acceptedItems,
  926:                     nextItem,
  927:                   ]
  928:                 : acceptedItems;
  929: 
  930:             if (isLastItem) {
  931:               /*
  932:                * Entire last REGULAR physical page fits.
  933:                *
  934:                * Evidence and signature placement happens only
  935:                * after the regular packing phase completes.
  936:                */
  937:               const basePage =
  938:                 makePhysicalPage(
  939:                   physicalPages.length + currentLogicalPhysicalPages.length,
  940:                   activeLogicalPage,
  941:                   nextAccepted.map(
  942:                     (item) => item.block,
  943:                   ),
  944:                 );
  945: 
  946:               const withFixed =
  947:                 addLogicalPageFixedBlocks(
  948:                   basePage,
  949:                   activeLogicalPage,
  950:                   currentLogicalPhysicalPages.length === 0,
  951:                 );
  952: 
  953:               completeCurrentLogicalPage([
  954:                 ...currentLogicalPhysicalPages,
  955:                 withFixed,
  956:               ]);
  957: 
  958:               decisionLockRef.current = false;
  959:               return;
  960:             }
  961: 
  962:             setAcceptedItems(
  963:               nextAccepted,
  964:             );
  965: 
  966:             setCursor(
  967:               (current) =>
  968:                 current + 1,
  969:             );
  970: 
  971:             setCandidateVersion(
  972:               (current) =>
  973:                 current + 1,
  974:             );
  975: 
  976:             decisionLockRef.current = false;
  977:             return;
  978:           }
  979: 
  980:           /*
  981:            * Candidate does NOT fit.
  982:            *
  983:            * If we already had accepted content,
  984:            * finalize it and retry the same next item on a new page.
  985:            */
  986:           if (
  987:             acceptedItems.length > 0
  988:           ) {
  989:             const basePage =
  990:               makePhysicalPage(
  991:                 physicalPages.length + currentLogicalPhysicalPages.length,
  992:                 activeLogicalPage,
  993:                 acceptedItems.map(
  994:                   (item) => item.block,
  995:                 ),
  996:               );
  997: 
  998:             const finalized =
  999:               addLogicalPageFixedBlocks(
 1000:                 basePage,
 1001:                 activeLogicalPage,
 1002:                 currentLogicalPhysicalPages.length === 0,
 1003:               );
 1004: 
 1005:             setCurrentLogicalPhysicalPages((current) =>
 1006:               [...current, finalized],
 1007:             );
 1008: 
 1009:             setAcceptedItems([]);
 1010: 
 1011:             /*
 1012:              * Do NOT advance cursor.
 1013:              * Retry the overflowing block as the first block
 1014:              * of the next physical page.
 1015:              */
 1016:             setCandidateVersion(
 1017:               (current) =>
 1018:                 current + 1,
 1019:             );
 1020: 
 1021:             decisionLockRef.current = false;
 1022:             return;
 1023:           }
 1024: 
 1025:           /*
 1026:            * A single block itself does not fit.
 1027:            *
 1028:            * We cannot safely split arbitrary blocks here.
 1029:            * Preserve it intact on its own physical page and let
 1030:            * Smart A4 minimum-safe handle it without data loss.
 1031:            */
 1032:           if (nextItem) {
 1033:             const oversizedPage =
 1034:               makePhysicalPage(
 1035:                 physicalPages.length + currentLogicalPhysicalPages.length,
 1036:                 activeLogicalPage,
 1037:                 [nextItem.block],
 1038:               );
 1039: 
 1040:             const withFixed =
 1041:               addLogicalPageFixedBlocks(
 1042:                 oversizedPage,
 1043:                 activeLogicalPage,
 1044:                 currentLogicalPhysicalPages.length === 0,
 1045:               );
 1046: 
 1047:             const newPages = [
 1048:               ...currentLogicalPhysicalPages,
 1049:               withFixed,
 1050:             ];
 1051: 
 1052:             if (isLastItem) {
 1053:               completeCurrentLogicalPage(newPages);
 1054:             } else {
 1055:               setCurrentLogicalPhysicalPages(newPages);
 1056: 
 1057:               setCursor(
 1058:                 (current) =>
 1059:                   current + 1,
 1060:               );
 1061: 
 1062:               setCandidateVersion(
 1063:                 (current) =>
 1064:                   current + 1,
 1065:               );
 1066:             }
 1067: 
 1068:             decisionLockRef.current = false;
 1069:             return;
 1070:           }
 1071: 
 1072:           decisionLockRef.current = false;
 1073:         });
 1074:     };
 1075: 
 1076:     const observer =
 1077:       new MutationObserver(inspect);
 1078: 
 1079:     observer.observe(host, {
 1080:       subtree: true,
 1081:       childList: true,
 1082:       attributes: true,
 1083:       attributeFilter: [
 1084:         "data-smart-a4-phase",
 1085:       ],
 1086:     });
 1087: 
 1088:     inspect();
 1089: 
 1090:     return () => {
 1091:       observer.disconnect();
 1092: 
 1093:       if (frame !== null) {
 1094:         window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
 1095:       }
 1096:     };
 1097:   }, [
 1098:     activeLogicalPage,
 1099:     acceptedItems,
 1100:     candidateVersion,
 1101:     completeCurrentLogicalPage,
 1102:     currentLogicalPhysicalPages,
 1103:     cursor,
 1104:     done,
 1105:     hasRegularItems,
 1106:     nextItem,
 1107:     physicalPages,
 1108:   ]);
 1109: 
 1110:   /*
 1111:    * During calculation keep old/new physical pages hidden
 1112:    * until a stable plan exists.
 1113:    */
 1114:   const isPlanCurrent =
 1115:     plannedResetKey === semanticInputFingerprint;
 1116: 
 1117:   const handlePageCompositionReady = useCallback(
 1118:     (composition: ReportPhysicalPageComposition) => {
 1119:       setPageCompositions((current) => {
 1120:         const previous = current[composition.corePhysicalPageId];
 1121:         const previousFingerprint = previous
 1122:           ? createSemanticInputFingerprint(previous.pages)
 1123:           : "";
 1124:         const nextFingerprint = createSemanticInputFingerprint(
 1125:           composition.pages,
 1126:         );
 1127: 
 1128:         if (previousFingerprint === nextFingerprint) {
 1129:           return current;
 1130:         }
 1131: 
 1132:         return {
 1133:           ...current,
 1134:           [composition.corePhysicalPageId]: composition,
 1135:         };
 1136:       });
 1137:     },
 1138:     [],
 1139:   );
 1140: 
 1141:   const composedPhysicalPages = useMemo(
 1142:     () =>
 1143:       buildComposedPhysicalPages(
 1144:         physicalPages,
 1145:         pageCompositions,
 1146:         logicalPageModels,
 1147:       ),
 1148:     [logicalPageModels, pageCompositions, physicalPages],
 1149:   );
 1150: 
 1151:   const allCoreCompositionsReady =
 1152:     physicalPages.length > 0 &&
 1153:     physicalPages.every((page) => Boolean(pageCompositions[page.id]));
 1154: 
 1155:   const renderablePhysicalPages = useMemo(
 1156:     () =>
 1157:       finalPhysicalPages.length > 0
 1158:         ? finalPhysicalPages
 1159:         : composedPhysicalPages,
 1160:     [composedPhysicalPages, finalPhysicalPages],
 1161:   );
 1162: 
 1163:   const visiblePages =
 1164:     done && isPlanCurrent && renderablePhysicalPages.length
 1165:       ? renderablePhysicalPages
 1166:       : [];
 1167: 
 1168:   const selectedPhysicalPages =
 1169:     renderMode === "stack"
 1170:       ? visiblePages
 1171:       : (() => {
 1172:           if (!visiblePages.length) {
 1173:             return [];
 1174:           }
 1175: 
 1176:           if (activePhysicalPageId) {
 1177:             const matchingPhysicalPage = visiblePages.find(
 1178:               (page) => page.id === activePhysicalPageId,
 1179:             );
 1180: 
 1181:             if (matchingPhysicalPage) {
 1182:               return [matchingPhysicalPage];
 1183:             }
 1184:           }
 1185: 
 1186:           if (activePageId) {
 1187:             const matching =
 1188:               visiblePages.find(
 1189:                 (page) =>
 1190:                   page.sourcePageIds.includes(
 1191:                     activePageId,
 1192:                   ),
 1193:               );
 1194: 
 1195:             if (matching) {
 1196:               return [matching];
 1197:             }
 1198:           }
 1199: 
 1200:           return [visiblePages[0]];
 1201:         })();
 1202: 
 1203:   useEffect(() => {
 1204:     if (!done) {
 1205:       return;
 1206:     }
 1207: 
 1208:     if (
 1209:       finalizedPlanRef.current?.semanticInputFingerprint ===
 1210:         semanticInputFingerprint &&
 1211:       (planningPhaseRef.current === "READY" ||
 1212:         planningPhaseRef.current === "FROZEN")
 1213:     ) {
 1214:       return;
 1215:     }
 1216: 
 1217:     const host = outputHostRef.current;
 1218: 
 1219:     if (!host) {
 1220:       return;
 1221:     }
 1222: 
 1223:     let frame: number | null = null;
 1224:     let disposed = false;
 1225:     let observer: MutationObserver | null = null;
 1226:     let resizeObserver: ResizeObserver | null = null;
 1227: 
 1228:     const setPhase = (next: PhysicalPlanningPhase) => {
 1229:       planningPhaseRef.current = next;
 1230:       setPlanningPhase((current) =>
 1231:         current === next ? current : next,
 1232:       );
 1233:     };
 1234: 
 1235:     const isTerminalPhase = () =>
 1236:       planningPhaseRef.current === "READY" ||
 1237:       planningPhaseRef.current === "FROZEN";
 1238: 
 1239:     const resetStability = () => {
 1240:       outputStabilityRef.current = {
 1241:         signature: "",
 1242:         count: 0,
 1243:         attempts: outputStabilityRef.current.attempts,
 1244:       };
 1245:     };
 1246: 
 1247:     const freezeFinalPlan = (
 1248:       layoutResultFingerprint: string,
 1249:     ) => {
 1250:       if (isTerminalPhase()) {
 1251:         return;
 1252:       }
 1253: 
 1254:       const frozenPages = composedPhysicalPages.map((page) => ({
 1255:         ...page,
 1256:         blocks: [...page.blocks],
 1257:         sourcePageIds: [...page.sourcePageIds],
 1258:       }));
 1259: 
 1260:       finalizedPlanRef.current = {
 1261:         semanticInputFingerprint,
 1262:         layoutResultFingerprint,
 1263:         pages: frozenPages,
 1264:       };
 1265: 
 1266:       setFinalPhysicalPages(frozenPages);
 1267: 
 1268:       if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "development") {
 1269:         host.dataset.reportPhysicalLayoutFingerprint =
 1270:           getFingerprintPrefix(layoutResultFingerprint);
 1271:         host.dataset.reportPhysicalStablePasses = String(
 1272:           outputStabilityRef.current.count,
 1273:         );
 1274:         host.dataset.reportPhysicalAttempts = String(
 1275:           outputStabilityRef.current.attempts,
 1276:         );
 1277:       }
 1278: 
 1279:       observer?.disconnect();
 1280:       resizeObserver?.disconnect();
 1281:       setPhase("READY");
 1282: 
 1283:       queueMicrotask(() => {
 1284:         if (
 1285:           !disposed &&
 1286:           planningPhaseRef.current === "READY" &&
 1287:           finalizedPlanRef.current?.semanticInputFingerprint ===
 1288:             semanticInputFingerprint
 1289:         ) {
 1290:           setPhase("FROZEN");
 1291:         }
 1292:       });
 1293:     };
 1294: 
 1295:     const inspect = () => {
 1296:       if (
 1297:         disposed ||
 1298:         isTerminalPhase() ||
 1299:         frame !== null
 1300:       ) {
 1301:         return;
 1302:       }
 1303: 
 1304:       frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
 1305:         frame = null;
 1306: 
 1307:         if (
 1308:           disposed ||
 1309:           isTerminalPhase()
 1310:         ) {
 1311:           return;
 1312:         }
 1313: 
 1314:         outputStabilityRef.current.attempts += 1;
 1315: 
 1316:         const renderedPages = host.querySelectorAll(
 1317:           ".pdf-report-page",
 1318:         );
 1319: 
 1320:         if (!renderedPages.length) {
 1321:           resetStability();
 1322:           setPhase("MEASURING");
 1323:           if (
 1324:             outputStabilityRef.current.attempts <
 1325:             MAX_PHYSICAL_STABILIZATION_ATTEMPTS
 1326:           ) {
 1327:             inspect();
 1328:           } else {
 1329:             freezeFinalPlan(
 1330:               createSemanticInputFingerprint({
 1331:                 fallback: "missing-rendered-page-dom",
 1332:                 physicalPageCount: physicalPages.length,
 1333:                 blocks: physicalPages.map((page) =>
 1334:                   page.blocks.map((block) => block?.id || block?.kind),
 1335:                 ),
 1336:               }),
 1337:             );
 1338:           }
 1339:           return;
 1340:         }
 1341: 
 1342:         const smartViewports = Array.from(
 1343:           host.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
 1344:             ".report-smart-a4-content",
 1345:           ),
 1346:         );
 1347: 
 1348:         const pageComposers = Array.from(
 1349:           host.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
 1350:             "[data-report-smart-page-composer]",
 1351:           ),
 1352:         );
 1353: 
 1354:         const smartLayoutsFinal = smartViewports.every(
 1355:           (viewport) =>
 1356:             viewport.dataset.smartA4Phase === "FROZEN",
 1357:         );
 1358:         const smartLayoutsFit = smartViewports.every(
 1359:           (viewport) => viewport.dataset.reportOverflow === "fit",
 1360:         );
 1361: 
 1362:         const pageComposersReady = pageComposers.every(
 1363:           (composer) =>
 1364:             composer.dataset.reportSmartPagePhase === "FROZEN" ||
 1365:             composer.dataset.reportSmartPagePhase === "READY",
 1366:         );
 1367:         const hasExpectedComposerCount =
 1368:           pageComposers.length === physicalPages.length;
 1369:         const renderedPageCountMatchesComposition =
 1370:           renderedPages.length === composedPhysicalPages.length;
 1371: 
 1372:         const images = Array.from(
 1373:           host.querySelectorAll<HTMLImageElement>("img"),
 1374:         );
 1375:         const assetsSettled = images.every((image) => image.complete);
 1376:         const reachedAttemptLimit =
 1377:           outputStabilityRef.current.attempts >=
 1378:           MAX_PHYSICAL_STABILIZATION_ATTEMPTS;
 1379: 
 1380:         if (
 1381:           !smartLayoutsFinal ||
 1382:           !smartLayoutsFit ||
 1383:           !pageComposersReady ||
 1384:           !hasExpectedComposerCount ||
 1385:           !allCoreCompositionsReady ||
 1386:           !renderedPageCountMatchesComposition ||
 1387:           !assetsSettled ||
 1388:           !fontsSettledRef.current
 1389:         ) {
 1390:           resetStability();
 1391:           setPhase("MEASURING");
 1392: 
 1393:           if (reachedAttemptLimit) {
 1394:             freezeFinalPlan(
 1395:               createSemanticInputFingerprint({
 1396:                 fallback: true,
 1397:                 physicalPages: composedPhysicalPages.map((page) => ({
 1398:                   blocks: page.blocks.map((block) => block?.id || block?.kind),
 1399:                   sourcePageIds: page.sourcePageIds,
 1400:                 })),
 1401:                 renderedPageCount: renderedPages.length,
 1402:               }),
 1403:             );
 1404:           } else {
 1405:             inspect();
 1406:           }
 1407:           return;
 1408:         }
 1409: 
 1410:         const smartSignature = smartViewports.map((viewport) =>
 1411:           [
 1412:             viewport.dataset.smartA4Candidate || "legacy",
 1413:             viewport.dataset.reportDensity || "normal",
 1414:             viewport.dataset.reportFieldLayout || "comfortable",
 1415:             viewport.dataset.reportOverflow || "fit",
 1416:             roundLayoutMetric(
 1417:               Number(viewport.dataset.smartA4OverflowPx || "0"),
 1418:             ),
 1419:             roundLayoutMetric(
 1420:               Number(
 1421:                 viewport.dataset.smartA4SignatureReservedPx || "0",
 1422:               ),
 1423:             ),
 1424:             viewport.dataset.smartA4FooterBoundaryUsed || "false",
 1425:             roundLayoutMetric(
 1426:               Number(viewport.dataset.smartA4BoundaryBottomPx || "0"),
 1427:             ),
 1428:           ].join(":"),
 1429:         );
 1430: 
 1431:         const composerSignature = pageComposers.map((composer) =>
 1432:           [
 1433:             composer.dataset.reportPrimaryEvidenceCount || "0",
 1434:             composer.dataset.reportTotalEvidenceCount || "0",
 1435:             composer.dataset.reportSignatureOnPrimary || "true",
 1436:             composer.dataset.reportSmartPagePhase || "",
 1437:           ].join(":"),
 1438:         );
 1439: 
 1440:         const signature = createSemanticInputFingerprint({
 1441:           physicalPages: composedPhysicalPages.map((page) => ({
 1442:             blocks: page.blocks.map((block) => block?.id || block?.kind),
 1443:             sourcePageIds: page.sourcePageIds,
 1444:           })),
 1445:           renderedPageCount: renderedPages.length,
 1446:           smartSignature,
 1447:           composerSignature,
 1448:         });
 1449: 
 1450:         const previous = outputStabilityRef.current;
 1451: 
 1452:         outputStabilityRef.current =
 1453:           previous.signature === signature
 1454:             ? {
 1455:               signature,
 1456:               count: previous.count + 1,
 1457:               attempts: previous.attempts,
 1458:             }
 1459:             : {
 1460:                 signature,
 1461:                 count: 1,
 1462:                 attempts: previous.attempts,
 1463:               };
 1464: 
 1465:         if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "development") {
 1466:           host.dataset.reportPhysicalLayoutFingerprint =
 1467:             getFingerprintPrefix(signature);
 1468:           host.dataset.reportPhysicalStablePasses = String(
 1469:             outputStabilityRef.current.count,
 1470:           );
 1471:           host.dataset.reportPhysicalAttempts = String(
 1472:             outputStabilityRef.current.attempts,
 1473:           );
 1474:         }
 1475: 
 1476:         if (
 1477:           outputStabilityRef.current.count >= 2 ||
 1478:           reachedAttemptLimit
 1479:         ) {
 1480:           freezeFinalPlan(signature);
 1481:           return;
 1482:         }
 1483: 
 1484:         setPhase("STABILIZING");
 1485:         inspect();
 1486:       });
 1487:     };
 1488: 
 1489:     resetStability();
 1490:     setPhase("MEASURING");
 1491: 
 1492:     observer = new MutationObserver(inspect);
 1493: 
 1494:     observer.observe(host, {
 1495:       subtree: true,
 1496:       childList: true,
 1497:       attributes: true,
 1498:       attributeFilter: [
 1499:         "data-smart-a4-phase",
 1500:         "data-report-smart-page-phase",
 1501:       ],
 1502:     });
 1503: 
 1504:     resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => {
 1505:       if (isTerminalPhase()) {
 1506:         return;
 1507:       }
 1508: 
 1509:       const rect = host.getBoundingClientRect();
 1510:       const nextResizeFingerprint = [
 1511:         roundLayoutMetric(rect.width),
 1512:         roundLayoutMetric(rect.height),
 1513:         roundLayoutMetric(host.scrollWidth),
 1514:         roundLayoutMetric(host.scrollHeight),
 1515:       ].join(":");
 1516: 
 1517:       if (
 1518:         outputResizeFingerprintRef.current === nextResizeFingerprint
 1519:       ) {
 1520:         return;
 1521:       }
 1522: 
 1523:       outputResizeFingerprintRef.current = nextResizeFingerprint;
 1524:       inspect();
 1525:     });
 1526:     resizeObserver.observe(host);
 1527: 
 1528:     const handleAssetLoad = (event: Event) => {
 1529:       if (isTerminalPhase()) {
 1530:         return;
 1531:       }
 1532: 
 1533:       const image =
 1534:         event.target instanceof HTMLImageElement
 1535:           ? event.target
 1536:           : null;
 1537:       const source = image?.currentSrc || image?.src || "";
 1538: 
 1539:       if (
 1540:         source &&
 1541:         settledAssetSourcesRef.current.has(source)
 1542:       ) {
 1543:         return;
 1544:       }
 1545: 
 1546:       if (source) {
 1547:         settledAssetSourcesRef.current.add(source);
 1548:       }
 1549: 
 1550:       resetStability();
 1551:       setPhase("MEASURING");
 1552:       inspect();
 1553:     };
 1554: 
 1555:     host.addEventListener("load", handleAssetLoad, true);
 1556:     host.addEventListener("error", handleAssetLoad, true);
 1557:     inspect();
 1558: 
 1559:     if (!document.fonts) {
 1560:       fontsSettledRef.current = true;
 1561:       inspect();
 1562:     } else if (fontsSettledRef.current) {
 1563:       inspect();
 1564:     } else {
 1565:       void document.fonts.ready
 1566:         .then(() => {
 1567:           if (!disposed && !fontsSettledRef.current) {
 1568:             fontsSettledRef.current = true;
 1569:             inspect();
 1570:           }
 1571:         })
 1572:         .catch(() => {
 1573:           if (!disposed) {
 1574:             fontsSettledRef.current = true;
 1575:             inspect();
 1576:           }
 1577:         });
 1578:     }
 1579: 
 1580:     return () => {
 1581:       disposed = true;
 1582:       observer?.disconnect();
 1583:       resizeObserver?.disconnect();
 1584:       host.removeEventListener("load", handleAssetLoad, true);
 1585:       host.removeEventListener("error", handleAssetLoad, true);
 1586: 
 1587:       if (frame !== null) {
 1588:         window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
 1589:       }
 1590:     };
 1591:   }, [
 1592:     allCoreCompositionsReady,
 1593:     composedPhysicalPages,
 1594:     done,
 1595:     semanticInputFingerprint,
 1596:     physicalPages,
 1597:   ]);
 1598: 
 1599:   const isReady =
 1600:     done &&
 1601:     isPlanCurrent &&
 1602:     finalPhysicalPages.length > 0 &&
 1603:     (planningPhase === "READY" || planningPhase === "FROZEN");
 1604: 
 1605:   const corePhysicalPagesToRender =
 1606:     !isReady || renderMode === "stack"
 1607:       ? physicalPages
 1608:       : (() => {
 1609:           const selectedPage = selectedPhysicalPages[0];
 1610:           const selectedCorePage = selectedPage
 1611:             ? physicalPages.find(
 1612:                 (page) => page.id === selectedPage.corePhysicalPageId,
 1613:               )
 1614:             : null;
 1615: 
 1616:           return selectedCorePage
 1617:             ? [selectedCorePage]
 1618:             : physicalPages.slice(0, 1);
 1619:         })();
 1620: 
 1621:   useEffect(() => {
 1622:     if (!isReady || !onPhysicalPagesChange) {
 1623:       return;
 1624:     }
 1625: 
 1626:     onPhysicalPagesChange(
 1627:       buildPhysicalNavigationItems(finalPhysicalPages),
 1628:     );
 1629:   }, [finalPhysicalPages, isReady, onPhysicalPagesChange]);
 1630: 
 1631:   const developmentDiagnostics =
 1632:     process.env.NODE_ENV === "development"
 1633:       ? {
 1634:           "data-report-physical-semantic-fingerprint":
 1635:             getFingerprintPrefix(semanticInputFingerprint),
 1636:           "data-report-physical-invalidation-reason":
 1637:             invalidationReason,
 1638:         }
 1639:       : {};
 1640: 
 1641:   return (
 1642:     <>
 1643:       <style>{`
 1644:         @media print {
 1645:           [data-report-physical-measurement-stage],
 1646:           [data-report-physical-output-planning="true"] {
 1647:             display: none !important;
 1648:           }
 1649:         }
 1650:       `}</style>
 1651:       {/*
 1652:        * ======================================================
 1653:        * OFFSCREEN MEASUREMENT STAGE
 1654:        * ======================================================
 1655:        *
 1656:        * This uses the REAL design and the REAL Smart A4 engine.
 1657:        * It is not an estimated character-count paginator.
 1658:        */}
 1659:       {!done ? (
 1660:         <div
 1661:           ref={measurementHostRef}
 1662:           aria-hidden="true"
 1663:           className="pointer-events-none fixed left-[-100000px] top-0 -z-50 w-[210mm]"
 1664:           style={{
 1665:             visibility: "hidden",
 1666:             contain: "layout style paint",
 1667:           }}
 1668:           data-report-physical-measurement-stage
 1669:           data-report-measurement-only="true"
 1670:         >
 1671:           <ReportSmartSemanticFingerprintProvider
 1672:             fingerprint={createSemanticInputFingerprint({
 1673:               blocks: candidatePage.blocks,
 1674:               kind: candidatePage.kind,
 1675:               semanticInputFingerprint,
 1676:             })}
 1677:           >
 1678:             <A4DesignPage
 1679:               key={candidatePage.id}
 1680:               designId={designId}
 1681:               page={candidatePage}
 1682:               context={context}
 1683:               previewCase={previewCase}
 1684:               pageLabel={fallbackPageLabel}
 1685:             />
 1686:           </ReportSmartSemanticFingerprintProvider>
 1687:         </div>
 1688:       ) : null}
 1689: 
 1690:       {/*
 1691:        * ======================================================
 1692:        * PHYSICAL A4 OUTPUT
 1693:        * ======================================================
 1694:        */}
 1695:       {done ? (
 1696:         <div
 1697:           ref={outputHostRef}
 1698:           className={
 1699:             renderMode === "stack"
 1700:               ? "space-y-6 print:space-y-0"
 1701:               : ""
 1702:           }
 1703:           data-report-smart-physical-pages
 1704:           data-report-physical-planning-phase={planningPhase}
 1705:           data-report-physical-output-planning={String(!isReady)}
 1706:           data-report-physical-page-count={
 1707:             visiblePages.length
 1708:           }
 1709:           {...developmentDiagnostics}
 1710:           aria-hidden={!isReady ? "true" : undefined}
 1711:           style={
 1712:             !isReady
 1713:               ? {
 1714:                   position: "fixed",
 1715:                   left: "-100000px",
 1716:                   top: 0,
 1717:                   width: "210mm",
 1718:                   visibility: "hidden",
 1719:                   pointerEvents: "none",
 1720:                   contain: "layout style paint",
 1721:                   zIndex: -50,
 1722:                 }
 1723:               : undefined
 1724:           }
 1725:         >
 1726:           {corePhysicalPagesToRender.map(
 1727:             (page, index) => (
 1728:               <div
 1729:                 key={`${designId}:${getFingerprintPrefix(semanticInputFingerprint)}:${page.id}`}
 1730:                 className={
 1731:                   renderMode === "stack" &&
 1732:                   index < corePhysicalPagesToRender.length - 1
 1733:                     ? "print:break-after-page"
 1734:                     : ""
 1735:                 }
 1736:                 data-report-physical-page-index={
 1737:                   index
 1738:                 }
 1739:                 data-report-source-pages={
 1740:                   page.sourcePageIds.join(",")
 1741:                 }
 1742:               >
 1743:                 <ReportSmartSemanticFingerprintProvider
 1744:                   fingerprint={createSemanticInputFingerprint({
 1745:                     page: {
 1746:                       blocks: page.blocks,
 1747:                       kind: page.kind,
 1748:                       title: page.title,
 1749:                     },
 1750:                     semanticInputFingerprint,
 1751:                   })}
 1752:                 >
 1753:                   <SmartReportPageComposer
 1754:                     designId={designId}
 1755:                     page={page}
 1756:                     context={context}
 1757:                     previewCase={previewCase}
 1758:                     pageLabel={
 1759:                       page.title ||
 1760:                       fallbackPageLabel
 1761:                     }
 1762:                     suppressAutoEvidencePages={suppressAutoEvidencePages}
 1763:                     renderMode={isReady ? renderMode : "stack"}
 1764:                     activePhysicalPageId={
 1765:                       isReady ? selectedPhysicalPages[0]?.id : undefined
 1766:                     }
 1767:                     onCompositionReady={handlePageCompositionReady}
 1768:                   />
 1769:                 </ReportSmartSemanticFingerprintProvider>
 1770:               </div>
 1771:             ),
 1772:           )}
 1773:         </div>
 1774:       ) : null}
 1775: 
 1776:       {!isReady ? (
 1777:         <div
 1778:           className="flex min-h-[320px] flex-col items-center justify-center gap-4 text-center font-bold text-slate-500 print:hidden"
 1779:           data-report-physical-planning
 1780:           data-report-physical-planning-phase={planningPhase}
 1781:           {...developmentDiagnostics}
 1782:         >
 1783:           <span
 1784:             className="h-11 w-11 animate-spin rounded-full border-4 border-slate-200 border-t-emerald-600"
 1785:             aria-hidden="true"
 1786:           />
 1787:           <span className="text-base sm:text-lg">
 1788:             جارٍ تنظيم صفحات التقرير...
 1789:           </span>
 1790:           <span className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-400">
 1791:             يتم ضبط توزيع المحتوى تلقائيًا
 1792:           </span>
 1793:         </div>
 1794:       ) : null}
 1795:     </>
 1796:   );
 1797: }

    1: "use client";

   12: export function ReportTwoActivePreviewRenderer({ template, context, previewCase, sourcePayload, variantId }: {
   13:   template: any;
   14:   context: Record<string, string>;
   15:   previewCase: any;
   16:   sourcePayload?: unknown;
   17:   variantId?: string | null;
   18: }) {
   19:   const displayTemplate = useMemo(
   20:     () => applyStructuredTableDisplayMetadataToTemplate(template, sourcePayload),
   21:     [template, sourcePayload],
   22:   ) as any;
   23:   const pages = Array.isArray(displayTemplate?.pages) ? displayTemplate.pages : [];
   24:   const activePage = pages[0] || null;
   25:   const designId = isReportDesignId(displayTemplate?.designTemplateId)
   26:     ? displayTemplate.designTemplateId
   27:     : null;
   28:

   30:     return (
   31:       <section className="p-8 text-center font-bold text-red-700" dir="rtl">

   33:       </section>
   34:     );
   35:   }
   36: 
   37:   return (
   38:     <section
   39:       className={[
   40:         "report-two-a4-host report-two-persisted-active-root",
   41:         variantId === OFFICIAL_ACTIVITY_CARD_VARIANT_ID
   42:           ? "report-two-official-activity-card"
   43:           : "",
   44:       ].join(" ")}
   45:     >
   46:       <ReportTwoOfficialActivitySignatureStyle
   47:         enabled={variantId === OFFICIAL_ACTIVITY_CARD_VARIANT_ID}
   48:       />
   49:       <ReportDesignRenderer
   50:         renderMode="stack"
   51:         chromeLayout="split"
   52:         designId={designId}
   53:         template={displayTemplate}
   54:         activePage={activePage}
   55:         activePageId={activePage?.id || ""}
   56:         context={context || {}}
   57:         previewCase={previewCase || null}
   58:         onActivePageChange={() => undefined}
   59:         onAddPage={() => undefined}
   60:         onMovePage={() => undefined}
   61:         onDeletePage={() => undefined}
   62:         canMovePage={() => false}
   63:         canDeletePage={() => false}
   64:       />
   65:     </section>
   66:   );
   67: }

   15: 
   16: type PrintDocumentSnapshot = {
   17:   template: any;
   18:   context: Record<string, string>;
   19:   previewCase: any;
   20:   sourcePayload?: unknown;
   21:   designTemplateId?: ReportDesignId;
   22:   variantId?: string | null;
   23: };
   24: 
   25: export function ReportTwoPrintDocument({
   26:   snapshot,
   27:   autoPrint = false,
   28: }: {
   29:   snapshot: PrintDocumentSnapshot;
   30:   autoPrint?: boolean;
   31: }) {
   32:   const template = applyStructuredTableDisplayMetadataToTemplate(
   33:     snapshot.template || { pages: [] },
   34:     snapshot.sourcePayload,
   35:   ) as any;
   36:   const pages = Array.isArray(template.pages) ? template.pages : [];
   37:   const context = snapshot.context || {};
   38:   const previewCase = snapshot.previewCase || null;
   39:   const requestedDesignId =
   40:     snapshot.designTemplateId || template.designTemplateId;
   41:   const designId = isReportDesignId(requestedDesignId)
   42:     ? requestedDesignId
   43:     : null;
   44: 
   45:   const logoSrc = getDesignLogoSrc(context);
   46: 
   47:   if (!designId) {
   48:     return (
   49:       <main className="p-8 text-center font-bold text-red-700" dir="rtl">
   50:         تعذر تحديد تصميم التقرير المحفوظ للطباعة.
   51:       </main>
   52:     );
   53:   }
   54: 
   55:   return (
   56:     <main
   57:       className={[
   58:         "report-two-print-document",
   59:         snapshot.variantId === OFFICIAL_ACTIVITY_CARD_VARIANT_ID
   60:           ? "report-two-official-activity-card"
   61:           : "",
   62:       ].join(" ")}
   63:       dir="rtl"
   64:     >
   65:       {autoPrint ? <ReportTwoSnapshotPrintController /> : null}
   66:       <ReportTwoOfficialActivitySignatureStyle
   67:         enabled={snapshot.variantId === OFFICIAL_ACTIVITY_CARD_VARIANT_ID}
   68:       />
   69:       <style>{`
   70:         @page {
   71:           size: A4;
   72:           margin: 0;
   73:         }
   74: 
   75:         :root,
   76:         html,
   77:         body {
   78:           color-scheme: light !important;
   79:         }
   80: 
   81:         * {
   82:           box-sizing: border-box;
   83:           -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
   84:           print-color-adjust: exact;
   85:         }
   86: 
   87:         html,
   88:         body {
   89:           margin: 0 !important;
   90:           padding: 0 !important;
   91:           background: #ffffff !important;
   92:           color: #0f172a;
   93:           direction: rtl;
   94:         }
   95: 
   96:         body {
   97:           overflow: visible !important;
   98:         }
   99: 
  100:         .report-two-print-document {
  101:           background: #ffffff;
  102:         }
  103: 
  104:         .report-two-print-document .pdf-report-page {
  105:           width: 210mm !important;
  106:           min-width: 210mm !important;
  107:           max-width: 210mm !important;
  108:           height: 297mm !important;
  109:           min-height: 297mm !important;
  110:           max-height: 297mm !important;
  111:           overflow: hidden !important;
  112:           break-after: page !important;
  113:           page-break-after: always !important;
  114:           page-break-inside: avoid !important;
  115:         }
  116: 
  117:         .report-two-print-document .pdf-report-page:last-child {
  118:           break-after: auto !important;
  119:           page-break-after: auto !important;
  120:         }
  121: 
  122:         .report-two-print-document img {
  123:           max-width: 100%;
  124:         }
  125:       `}</style>
  126:       {logoSrc && <link rel="preload" as="image" href={logoSrc} />}
  127: 
  128:       <ReportDesignRenderer
  129:         designId={designId}
  130:         template={template}
  131:         activePage={pages[0] || null}
  132:         activePageId={pages[0]?.id || ""}
  133:         context={context}
  134:         previewCase={previewCase}
  135:         renderMode="stack"
  136:         chromeLayout="none"
  137:         onActivePageChange={() => undefined}
  138:         onAddPage={() => undefined}
  139:         onMovePage={() => undefined}
  140:         onDeletePage={() => undefined}
  141:         canMovePage={() => false}
  142:         canDeletePage={() => false}
  143:       />
  144:     </main>
  145:   );
  146: }

    1: "use client";

   43: function ReportTwoCollapsibleCard({
   44:   id,
   45:   title,
   46:   children,
   47: }: ReportTwoCollapsibleCardProps) {
   48:   const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
   49: 
   50:   return (
   51:     <section
   52:       data-report-two-panel-id={id}
   53:       className="rounded-[2rem] border border-emerald-100 bg-white p-3 shadow-sm dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-950 dark:shadow-black/30"
   54:     >
   55:       <button
   56:         type="button"
   57:         onClick={() => setOpen((current) => !current)}
   58:         className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-[1.4rem] bg-slate-50 px-4 py-3 text-right transition hover:bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
   59:       >
   60:         <span className="text-sm font-black text-slate-950 dark:text-white">
   61:           {title}
   62:         </span>
   63: 
   64:         <span
   65:           className={[
   66:             "rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-black transition",
   67:             open
   68:               ? "bg-emerald-700 text-white"
   69:               : "bg-white text-emerald-700 ring-1 ring-emerald-100",
   70:           ].join(" ")}
   71:         >
   72:           {open ? "إغلاق" : "فتح"}
   73:         </span>
   74:       </button>
   75: 
   76:       {open ? (
   77:         <div className="mt-3 rounded-[1.5rem] bg-white p-2 dark:bg-slate-950">
   78:           {children}
   79:         </div>
   80:       ) : null}
   81:     </section>
   82: 
   83:             );
   84: }

  145: function getReportTwoDynamicFieldBaseId(
  146:   field: Partial<Pick<ReportTwoDynamicField, "id" | "key" | "label">>,
  147:   index: number,
  148: ) {
  149:   return (
  150:     cleanText(field.id) ||
  151:     cleanText(field.key) ||
  152:     cleanText(field.label) ||
  153:     `dynamic-field-${index + 1}`
  154:   );

  157: function ensureUniqueReportTwoDynamicFieldIds(fields: ReportTwoDynamicField[]) {
  158:   const seen = new Map<string, number>();
  159: 
  160:   return fields.map((field, index) => {
  161:     const baseId = getReportTwoDynamicFieldBaseId(field, index);
  162:     const count = seen.get(baseId) || 0;
  163:     seen.set(baseId, count + 1);
  164: 
  165:     return {
  166:       ...field,
  167:       id: count === 0 ? baseId : `${baseId}__${count + 1}`,
  168:     };
  169:   });
  170: }

  315: function getReportTwoSavedTemplatesStorageKey(serviceSlug: string) {
  316:   return `report-2:saved-runtime-templates:${serviceSlug || "general"}`;
  317: }
  318: 
  319: function readReportTwoSavedTemplates(
  320:   serviceSlug: string,
  321: ): ReportTwoSavedRuntimeTemplate[] {
  322:   if (typeof window === "undefined") return [];
  323: 
  324:   try {
  325:     const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(
  326:       getReportTwoSavedTemplatesStorageKey(serviceSlug),
  327:     );
  328: 
  329:     if (!raw) return [];
  330: 
  331:     const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
  332: 
  333:     return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [];
  334:   } catch {
  335:     return [];
  336:   }
  337: }
  338: 
  339: function getReportTwoSavedTemplatesStoragePrefix() {
  340:   return "report-2:saved-runtime-templates:";
  341: }
  342: 
  343: function readAllReportTwoSavedTemplates() {
  344:   if (typeof window === "undefined") return [];
  345: 
  346:   const prefix = getReportTwoSavedTemplatesStoragePrefix();
  347:   const items: ReportTwoSavedRuntimeTemplate[] = [];
  348: 
  349:   for (let index = 0; index < window.localStorage.length; index += 1) {
  350:     const key = window.localStorage.key(index);
  351: 
  352:     if (!key || !key.startsWith(prefix)) continue;
  353: 
  354:     const serviceSlug = key.slice(prefix.length);
  355:     const templates = readReportTwoSavedTemplates(serviceSlug);
  356: 
  357:     templates.forEach((template) => {
  358:       items.push({
  359:         ...template,
  360:         serviceSlug: (template as any).serviceSlug || serviceSlug,
  361:       } as ReportTwoSavedRuntimeTemplate);
  362:     });
  363:   }
  364: 
  365:   return items.sort((a, b) => {
  366:     return String((b as any).updatedAt || (b as any).createdAt || "").localeCompare(
  367:       String((a as any).updatedAt || (a as any).createdAt || ""),
  368:     );
  369:   });
  370: }
  371: 
  372: function deleteReportTwoSavedTemplateFromAllServices(templateId: string) {
  373:   if (typeof window === "undefined") return [];
  374: 
  375:   const prefix = getReportTwoSavedTemplatesStoragePrefix();
  376: 
  377:   for (let index = window.localStorage.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
  378:     const key = window.localStorage.key(index);
  379: 
  380:     if (!key || !key.startsWith(prefix)) continue;
  381: 
  382:     const serviceSlug = key.slice(prefix.length);
  383:     const templates = readReportTwoSavedTemplates(serviceSlug);
  384:     const nextTemplates = templates.filter((template) => template.id !== templateId);
  385: 
  386:     if (nextTemplates.length !== templates.length) {
  387:       writeReportTwoSavedTemplates(serviceSlug, nextTemplates);
  388:     }
  389:   }
  390: 
  391:   return readAllReportTwoSavedTemplates();
  392: }
  393: function writeReportTwoSavedTemplates(
  394:   serviceSlug: string,
  395:   items: ReportTwoSavedRuntimeTemplate[],
  396: ) {
  397:   if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  398: 
  399:   window.localStorage.setItem(
  400:     getReportTwoSavedTemplatesStorageKey(serviceSlug),
  401:     JSON.stringify(items),
  402:   );
  403: }

  413: function formatReportTwoSavedAt(value: string) {
  414:   if (!value) return "لم يتم الحفظ بعد";
  415: 
  416:   try {
  417:     return new Date(value).toLocaleString("ar-SA", {
  418:       hour: "2-digit",
  419:       minute: "2-digit",
  420:       second: "2-digit",
  421:     });
  422:   } catch {
  423:     return "تم الحفظ";
  424:   }
  425: }
  426: 
  427: function parseReportTwoDraftSnapshot(value: string | null) {
  428:   if (!value) return null;
  429: 
  430:   try {
  431:     const parsed = JSON.parse(value) as ReportTwoDraftSnapshot;
  432: 
  433:     if (!parsed || parsed.version !== 1 || !parsed.template?.pages) {
  434:       return null;
  435:     }
  436: 
  437:     return parsed;
  438:   } catch {
  439:     return null;
  440:   }
  441: }
  442: function makeId(prefix: string) {
  443:   return `${prefix}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`;
  444: }
  445: 
  446: function cleanText(value: unknown) {
  447:   return String(value ?? "").trim();
  448: }
  449: 
  450: function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, any> {
  451:   if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) {
  452:     return {};
  453:   }
  454: 
  455:   return value as Record<string, any>;
  456: }
  457: 
  458: function asArray(value: unknown): any[] {
  459:   return Array.isArray(value) ? value : [];
  460: }
  461: 
  462: function normalizeDesignId(value: unknown): ReportDesignId {
  463:   const designId = cleanText(value);
  464: 
  465:   if (reportDesignTemplates.some((item) => item.id === designId)) {
  466:     return designId as ReportDesignId;
  467:   }
  468: 
  469:   return "ministry-form";
  470: }

  472: function createBlock(kind: StudioBlockKind): StudioBlock {
  473:   const base = {
  474:     id: makeId(kind),
  475:     kind,
  476:     title: "بلوك جديد",
  477:     content: "",
  478:     variant: "card",
  479:     source: "manual",
  480:     showTitle: true,
  481:     showMeta: false,
  482:     align: "right" as const,
  483:     placement: "flow",
  484:   };
  485: 
  486:   if (kind === "hero-title") {
  487:     return {
  488:       ...base,
  489:       title: "عنوان التقرير",
  490:       content: "{{case.title}}",
  491:       variant: "hero",
  492:       showTitle: false,
  493:       align: "center",
  494:     };
  495:   }
  496: 
  497:   if (kind === "dynamic-fields") {
  498:     return {
  499:       ...base,
  500:       title: "حقول الحالة",
  501:       content: "",
  502:       variant: "soft",
  503:     };
  504:   }
  505: 
  506:   if (kind === "signature-grid") {
  507:     return {
  508:       ...base,
  509:       title: "تواقيع الاعتماد",
  510:       content: "",
  511:       variant: "soft",
  512:       align: "center",
  513:       placement: "bottom",
  514:       showTitle: false,
  515:     };
  516:   }
  517: 
  518:   if (kind === "evidence-gallery") {
  519:     return {
  520:       ...base,
  521:       title: "الشواهد والمرفقات",
  522:       evidenceLayout: "TWO_PER_PAGE",
  523:       evidenceFit: "contain",
  524:       evidenceAspectRatio: "SQUARE_1_1",
  525:       evidenceShowCaptions: false,
  526:       evidenceAutoCreatePages: false,
  527:       evidenceEmptyBehavior: "message",
  528:     };
  529:   }
  530: 
  531:   if (kind === "bullet-list") {
  532:     return {
  533:       ...base,
  534:       title: "قائمة نقاط",
  535:       content: "النقطة الأولى\nالنقطة الثانية\nالنقطة الثالثة",
  536:     };
  537:   }
  538: 
  539:   if (kind === "closing-note") {
  540:     return {
  541:       ...base,
  542:       title: "خاتمة واعتماد",
  543:       content: "تم إعداد التقرير واعتماده وفق البيانات المتاحة في منصة التوجيه الطلابي.",
  544:     };
  545:   }
  546: 
  547:   if (kind === "report-one-table") {
  548:     return {
  549:       ...base,
  550:       title: "جدول",
  551:       columns: ["المجال", "الإجراء", "ملاحظات"],
  552:       rows: [
  553:         ["", "", ""],
  554:         ["", "", ""],
  555:         ["", "", ""],
  556:       ],
  557:       tableSettings: {
  558:         highlightHeader: true,
  559:         highlightFirstColumn: true,
  560:         stripedRows: true,
  561:         rounded: true,
  562:         compact: false,
  563:         repeatHeader: true,
  564:         colorTheme: "light-gray",
  565:       },
  566:     };
  567:   }
  568: 
  569:   if (kind === "structured-table") {
  570:     return {
  571:       ...base,
  572:       title: "جدول البيانات",
  573:       columns: [],
  574:       rows: [],
  575:       sourceTableId: "",
  576:       tableSettings: {
  577:         highlightHeader: true,
  578:         highlightFirstColumn: false,
  579:         stripedRows: true,
  580:         rounded: true,
  581:         compact: false,
  582:         repeatHeader: true,
  583:         colorTheme: "light-gray",
  584:       },
  585:     };
  586:   }
  587: 
  588:   return {
  589:     ...base,
  590:     title: kind === "section-text" ? "فقرة" : "نص",
  591:     content: "اكتب النص هنا.",
  592:   };
  593: }
  594: 
  595: function createPage(index: number): StudioPage {
  596:   return {
  597:     id: makeId("page"),
  598:     kind: "content",
  599:     title: `صفحة محتوى ${index}`,
  600:     description: "",
  601:     blocks: [],
  602:   };
  603: }
  604: 
  605: function normalizeBlock(value: any, index: number): StudioBlock {
  606:   const kind = cleanText(value?.kind || value?.settings?.smartBlockKind || "section-text") as StudioBlockKind;
  607: 
  608:   return {
  609:     ...createBlock(kind),
  610:     ...value,
  611:     id: cleanText(value?.id) || makeId(`block-${index + 1}`),
  612:     kind,
  613:     title: cleanText(value?.title) || `بلوك ${index + 1}`,
  614:     content: cleanText(value?.content ?? value?.defaultContent ?? value?.customContent ?? ""),
  615:     showTitle: value?.showTitle !== false,
  616:     showMeta: Boolean(value?.showMeta),
  617:     align: value?.align === "center" ? "center" : "right",
  618:     placement: cleanText(value?.placement) || "flow",
  619:   };
  620: }
  621: 
  622: function normalizePage(value: any, index: number): StudioPage {
  623:   const rawBlocks = asArray(value?.blocks);
  624: 
  625:   return {
  626:     id: cleanText(value?.id) || makeId(`page-${index + 1}`),
  627:     kind: cleanText(value?.kind || "content") as StudioPageKind,
  628:     title: cleanText(value?.title) || `صفحة ${index + 1}`,
  629:     description: cleanText(value?.description),
  630:     blocks: rawBlocks.length
  631:       ? rawBlocks.map(normalizeBlock)
  632:       : [createBlock("section-text")],
  633:   };
  634: }
  635: 
  636: function hydrateTemplate(template: TemplateOption | null): StudioTemplate {
  637:   const raw = asRecord(template?.templateJson);
  638:   const smartStudio = asRecord(raw.smartStudio);
  639:   const source = asArray(smartStudio.pages).length ? smartStudio : raw;
  640:   const pages = asArray(source.pages);
  641: 
  642:   return {
  643:     id: template?.id || "report-2-empty-template",
  644:     name: template?.name || "قالب report-2",
  645:     description: template?.description || "",
  646:     designTemplateId: normalizeDesignId(source.designTemplateId || raw.designTemplateId),
  647:     designConfig: asRecord(source.designConfig).header
  648:       ? {
  649:           header: asRecord(source.designConfig)
  650:             .header as unknown as ReportHeaderSettings,
  651:         }
  652:       : asRecord(raw.designConfig).header
  653:         ? {
  654:             header: asRecord(raw.designConfig)
  655:               .header as unknown as ReportHeaderSettings,
  656:           }
  657:         : undefined,
  658:     pages: pages.length
  659:       ? pages.map(normalizePage)
  660:       : [
  661:           {
  662:             id: "report-2-fallback-page",
  663:             kind: "content",
  664:             title: "صفحة التقرير",
  665:             description: "",
  666:             blocks: [
  667:               createBlock("hero-title"),
  668:               createBlock("dynamic-fields"),
  669:               createBlock("section-text"),
  670:             ],
  671:           },
  672:         ],
  673:   };
  674: }
  675: 
  676: 
  677: 
  678: function getDefaultReportTwoLogoSettings(
  679:   context: Record<string, string>,
  680: ): ReportTwoLogoSettings {
  681:   return {
  682:     url:
  683:       cleanText(context["report.logoUrl"]) ||
  684:       cleanText(context["identity.logoUrl"]) ||
  685:       "/uploads/school-logos/MOE.png",
  686:     width: 132,
  687:     height: 80,
  688:     fit: "contain",
  689:     filter: "invert",
  690:   };
  691: }

  693: function normalizeReportTwoLogoNumber(
  694:   value: number,
  695:   fallback: number,
  696:   min: number,
  697:   max: number,
  698: ) {
  699:   if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
  700:     return fallback;
  701:   }
  702: 
  703:   return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, Math.floor(value)));
  704: }

  876: function getReportTwoFieldLabel(key: string, label: string): string {
  877:   if (!key && !label) return "";
  878:   const trimmed = label?.trim();
  879:   if (trimmed && trimmed !== key && !/^[a-z0-9_./-]+$/i.test(trimmed)) {
  880:     return trimmed;
  881:   }
  882:   return REPORT_TWO_FIELD_LABELS[key] || REPORT_TWO_FIELD_LABELS[label] || trimmed || key || "";
  883: }
  884: 
  885: function getReportTwoFieldValue(value: unknown): string {
  886:   if (value === null || value === undefined) return "";
  887:   const raw = String(value).trim();
  888:   if (!raw) return "";
  889:   const lower = raw.toLowerCase();
  890:   const direct = REPORT_TWO_VALUE_LABELS[lower] || REPORT_TWO_VALUE_LABELS[raw];
  891:   if (direct) return direct;
  892:   const programMatch = lower.match(/^program_(\d+)$/);
  893:   if (programMatch) return `برنامج النشاط رقم ${programMatch[1]}`;
  894:   if (/^[a-z0-9_./-]+$/i.test(raw) && /[a-z_]/i.test(raw)) return raw;
  895:   return raw;
  896: }
