  898: function getPayloadAny(payload: SmartReportPayload) {
  899:   return payload as any;
  900: }
  901: 
  902: function getRuntimeContext(payload: SmartReportPayload) {
  903:   const data = getPayloadAny(payload);
  904:   const student = data.student || data.caseInfo?.student || {};
  905:   const fields = [...(payload.primaryFields || []), ...(payload.detailFields || [])];
  906: 
  907:   const context: Record<string, string> = {
  908:     "case.id": cleanText(data.caseInfo?.id),
  909:     "case.title": cleanText(data.caseInfo?.title || data.title),
  910:     "case.status": cleanText(data.caseInfo?.status),
  911:     "case.createdAt": cleanText(data.caseInfo?.createdAt),
  912:     "case.updatedAt": cleanText(data.caseInfo?.updatedAt),
  913: 
  914:     "service.name": cleanText(data.service?.name),
  915:     "service.slug": cleanText(data.service?.slug),
  916: 
  917:     "student.name": cleanText(student.name || student.fullName),
  918:     "student.grade": cleanText(student.grade),
  919:     "student.classroom": cleanText(student.classroom),
  920:     "student.stage": cleanText(student.stage),
  921:     "student.guardianName": cleanText(student.guardianName),
  922:     "student.guardianPhone": cleanText(student.guardianPhone),
  923: 
  924:     "identity.ministryName": cleanText(data.identity?.ministryName || "وزارة التعليم"),
  925:     "identity.educationDepartment": cleanText(data.identity?.educationDepartment || "الإدارة العامة للتعليم"),
  926:     "identity.schoolName": cleanText(data.identity?.schoolName || ""),
  927:   };
  928: 
  929:   fields.forEach((field: any, index: number) => {
  930:     const key = cleanText(field.key) || `field-${index + 1}`;
  931:     const label = cleanText(field.label);
  932:     const value = cleanText(field.value);
  933: 
  934:     context[key] = value;
  935:     context[`field.${key}`] = value;
  936: 
  937:     if (label) {
  938:       context[label] = value;
  939:       context[`field.${label}`] = value;
  940:     }
  941:   });
  942: 
  943:   return context;
  944: }
  945: 
  946: function normalizeEvidenceUrl(value: unknown) {
  947:   const url = cleanText(value).replaceAll("\\", "/");
  948: 
  949:   if (!url) return "";
  950:   if (url.startsWith("http://")) return url;
  951:   if (url.startsWith("https://")) return url;
  952:   if (url.startsWith("data:")) return url;
  953:   if (url.startsWith("/")) return url;
  954: 
  955:   return `/${url.replace(/^public\//, "")}`;
  956: }
  957: 
  958: const REPORT_TWO_IMAGE_EVIDENCE_EXTENSION_PATTERN =
  959:   /\.(png|jpe?g|webp|gif|avif)(?:[?#].*)?$/i;
  960: 
  961: function hasReportTwoImageEvidenceExtension(value: unknown) {
  962:   const text = cleanText(value).replaceAll("\\", "/");
  963: 
  964:   if (!text) return false;
  965: 
  966:   return REPORT_TWO_IMAGE_EVIDENCE_EXTENSION_PATTERN.test(text);
  967: }
  968: 
  969: function isReportTwoImageEvidence(item: any) {
  970:   const evidenceType = cleanText(item?.type).toUpperCase();
  971:   const mimeType = cleanText(item?.mimeType).toLowerCase();
  972: 
  973:   if (evidenceType === "IMAGE") return true;
  974:   if (mimeType.startsWith("image/")) return true;
  975: 
  976:   return [
  977:     item?.imageUrl,
  978:     item?.fileUrl,
  979:     item?.url,
  980:     item?.publicUrl,
  981:     item?.thumbnailUrl,
  982:     item?.previewUrl,
  983:     item?.fileName,
  984:     item?.originalName,
  985:     item?.name,
  986:   ].some((value) => hasReportTwoImageEvidenceExtension(value));
  987: }
  988: 
  989: function collectEvidences(payload: SmartReportPayload) {
  990:   const data = getPayloadAny(payload);
  991: 
  992:   const candidates = [
  993:     data.evidence,
  994:     data.evidence?.items,
  995:     data.evidence?.evidences,
  996:     data.evidence?.files,
  997:     data.evidence?.attachments,
  998:     data.evidenceItems,
  999:     data.evidences,
 1000:     data.attachments,
 1001:     data.files,
 1002:     data.caseInfo?.evidence,
 1003:     data.caseInfo?.evidence?.items,
 1004:     data.caseInfo?.evidence?.evidences,
 1005:     data.caseInfo?.evidence?.files,
 1006:     data.caseInfo?.evidence?.attachments,
 1007:     data.caseInfo?.evidenceItems,
 1008:     data.caseInfo?.evidences,
 1009:     data.caseInfo?.attachments,
 1010:     data.caseInfo?.files,
 1011:   ];
 1012: 
 1013:   const collected: any[] = [];
 1014: 
 1015:   candidates.forEach((candidate) => {
 1016:     if (Array.isArray(candidate)) {
 1017:       collected.push(...candidate);
 1018:       return;
 1019:     }
 1020: 
 1021:     if (candidate && typeof candidate === "object") {
 1022:       const record = candidate as Record<string, any>;
 1023: 
 1024:       [
 1025:         record.items,
 1026:         record.evidences,
 1027:         record.files,
 1028:         record.attachments,
 1029:         record.data,
 1030:       ].forEach((value) => {
 1031:         if (Array.isArray(value)) {
 1032:           collected.push(...value);
 1033:         }
 1034:       });
 1035:     }
 1036:   });
 1037: 
 1038:   const seen = new Set<string>();
 1039: 
 1040:   return filterValidReportEvidenceItems(collected)
 1041:     .map((item, index) => {
 1042:       const rawUrl =
 1043:         item.fileUrl ||
 1044:         item.url ||
 1045:         item.imageUrl ||
 1046:         item.publicUrl ||
 1047:         item.path ||
 1048:         item.filePath ||
 1049:         item.storagePath ||
 1050:         item.thumbnailUrl ||
 1051:         item.previewUrl ||
 1052:         item.downloadUrl ||
 1053:         item.secureUrl ||
 1054:         item.src ||
 1055:         "";
 1056: 
 1057:       const fileUrl = normalizeEvidenceUrl(rawUrl);
 1058:       const normalizedUrl = normalizeEvidenceUrl(item.url || "");
 1059:       const imageUrlCandidate = normalizeEvidenceUrl(
 1060:         item.imageUrl ||
 1061:           item.thumbnailUrl ||
 1062:           item.previewUrl ||
 1063:           item.publicUrl ||
 1064:           item.url ||
 1065:           item.fileUrl ||
 1066:           rawUrl,
 1067:       );
 1068:       const imageUrl = isReportTwoImageEvidence(item)
 1069:         ? imageUrlCandidate || fileUrl || normalizedUrl
 1070:         : "";
 1071:       const evidenceType: "IMAGE" | "FILE" = isReportTwoImageEvidence(item)
 1072:         ? "IMAGE"
 1073:         : "FILE";
 1074: 
 1075:       const id =
 1076:         cleanText(item.id) ||
 1077:         cleanText(item.evidenceId) ||
 1078:         cleanText(item.fileId) ||
 1079:         fileUrl ||
 1080:         imageUrl ||
 1081:         `evidence-${index + 1}`;
 1082: 
 1083:       return {
 1084:         id,
 1085:         title:
 1086:           cleanText(item.title || item.fileName || item.originalName || item.name) ||
 1087:           `شاهد ${index + 1}`,
 1088:         url: normalizedUrl || undefined,
 1089:         fileUrl,
 1090:         imageUrl,
 1091:         attachmentId: cleanText(item.attachmentId || item.fileId || item.evidenceId) || undefined,
 1092:         storagePath: cleanText(item.storagePath) || undefined,
 1093:         type: evidenceType,
 1094:         mimeType: cleanText(item.mimeType) || undefined,
 1095:         caption: cleanText(
 1096:           item.caption ||
 1097:             item.note ||
 1098:             item.description ||
 1099:             item.title ||
 1100:             item.fileName ||
 1101:             item.originalName,
 1102:         ),
 1103:       };
 1104:     })
 1105:     .filter((item) => filterValidReportEvidenceItems([item]).length > 0)
 1106:     .filter((item) => {
 1107:       const key = `${item.id}|${item.fileUrl}|${item.imageUrl}|${item.attachmentId || ""}`;
 1108: 
 1109:       if (seen.has(key)) {
 1110:         return false;
 1111:       }
 1112: 
 1113:       seen.add(key);
 1114:       return true;
 1115:     });
 1116: }
 1117: 
 1118: function getPreviewCase(payload: SmartReportPayload, template?: StudioTemplate) {
 1119:   const data = getPayloadAny(payload);
 1120:   const student = data.student || data.caseInfo?.student || {};
 1121:   const hasStudentDataTable =
 1122:     (payload.tables || []).some(isStudentDataTable) ||
 1123:     Boolean(
 1124:       template?.pages.some((page) =>
 1125:         page.blocks.some(
 1126:           (block) => block.kind === "structured-table" && isStudentDataTable(block),
 1127:         ),
 1128:       ),
 1129:     );
 1130:   const fields = [...(payload.primaryFields || []), ...(payload.detailFields || [])].filter(
 1131:     (field) => !hasStudentDataTable || !isStudentIdentityField(field),
 1132:   );
 1133: 
 1134:   return {
 1135:     found: true,
 1136:     caseId: cleanText(data.caseInfo?.id),
 1137:     serviceSlug: cleanText(data.service?.slug),
 1138:     serviceName: cleanText(data.service?.name),
 1139:     title: cleanText(data.caseInfo?.title || data.title),
 1140:     status: cleanText(data.caseInfo?.status),
 1141:     createdAt: cleanText(data.caseInfo?.createdAt),
 1142:     updatedAt: cleanText(data.caseInfo?.updatedAt),
 1143:     hasStudentDataTable,
 1144:     student: {
 1145:       name: cleanText(student.name || student.fullName),
 1146:       nationalId: cleanText(student.nationalId),
 1147:       grade: cleanText(student.grade),
 1148:       classroom: cleanText(student.classroom),
 1149:       stage: cleanText(student.stage),
 1150:       guardianName: cleanText(student.guardianName),
 1151:       guardianPhone: cleanText(student.guardianPhone),
 1152:     },
 1153:     values: fields.map((field: any, index: number) => {
 1154:       const fieldKey =
 1155:         cleanText(field.key) ||
 1156:         cleanText(field.fieldKey) ||
 1157:         cleanText(field.name) ||
 1158:         cleanText(field.id) ||
 1159:         `field-${index + 1}`;
 1160:       const valueItems = getReportTwoReadableFieldValueItems(field);
 1161:       const value =
 1162:         valueItems.length > 1
 1163:           ? valueItems.join("، ")
 1164:           : getReportTwoReadableFieldValue(field);
 1165: 
 1166:       return {
 1167:         fieldKey,
 1168:         fieldLabel: getReportTwoReadableFieldLabel(field, index),
 1169:         value,
 1170:         ...(valueItems.length > 1 ? { valueItems } : {}),
 1171:         rawValue: Array.isArray(field.value)
 1172:           ? field.value.map((item: unknown) => cleanText(item)).filter(Boolean).join("، ")
 1173:           : cleanText(field.value),
 1174:       };
 1175:     }),
 1176:     evidences: collectEvidences(payload),
 1177:   };
 1178: }

 1180: 
 1181: function getEvidencePerPageFromBlock(block: StudioBlock) {
 1182:   const explicitLimit = Number(block.evidenceLimit || 0);
 1183: 
 1184:   if (Number.isFinite(explicitLimit) && explicitLimit > 0) {
 1185:     return Math.max(1, Math.floor(explicitLimit));
 1186:   }
 1187: 
 1188:   if (block.evidenceLayout === "ONE_PER_PAGE") return 1;
 1189:   if (block.evidenceLayout === "GRID_2X2") return 4;
 1190:   if (block.evidenceLayout === "ATTACHMENT_LIST") return 6;
 1191: 
 1192:   return 2;
 1193: }
 1194: 
 1195: function getReportTwoSignatureCardsFromPayload(payload: SmartReportPayload) {
 1196:   const priority = ["teacher", "activity_leader", "counselor", "principal"];
 1197: 
 1198:   const signatures = [...(payload.signatures || [])].sort((a, b) => {
 1199:     const aIndex = priority.indexOf(String(a.key || ""));
 1200:     const bIndex = priority.indexOf(String(b.key || ""));
 1201: 
 1202:     return (aIndex === -1 ? 99 : aIndex) - (bIndex === -1 ? 99 : bIndex);
 1203:   });
 1204: 
 1205:   return signatures
 1206:     .filter((signature) => {
 1207:       return Boolean(
 1208:         signature?.label ||
 1209:           signature?.signerName ||
 1210:           signature?.signerTitle ||
 1211:           signature?.imageUrl,
 1212:       );
 1213:     })
 1214:     .map((signature) => ({
 1215:       key: String(signature.key || ""),
 1216:       label: String(signature.label || "التوقيع"),
 1217:       signerName: String(signature.signerName || ""),
 1218:       signerTitle: String(signature.signerTitle || ""),
 1219:       imageUrl: String(signature.imageUrl || ""),
 1220:       required: Boolean(signature.required),
 1221:     }));
 1222: }
 1223: 
 1224: function isReportTwoSignatureBlock(block: StudioBlock | null | undefined) {
 1225:   if (!block) return false;
 1226: 
 1227:   const kind = cleanText(block.kind);
 1228:   const title = cleanText(block.title);
 1229: 
 1230:   return (
 1231:     kind === "signature-grid" ||
 1232:     kind === "signatures" ||
 1233:     kind === "approval-signatures" ||
 1234:     title.includes("توقيع") ||
 1235:     title.includes("اعتماد") ||
 1236:     Array.isArray((block as any).signatures)
 1237:   );
 1238: }
 1239: 
 1240: function getReportTwoSignatureHiddenKeys(block: StudioBlock | null | undefined) {
 1241:   return new Set(
 1242:     Array.isArray((block as any)?.hiddenSignatureKeys)
 1243:       ? ((block as any).hiddenSignatureKeys as string[]).map((key) => cleanText(key))
 1244:       : [],
 1245:   );
 1246: }
 1247: 
 1248: function getReportTwoOrderedSignatureCards(
 1249:   signatures: ReturnType<typeof getReportTwoSignatureCardsFromPayload>,
 1250:   block: StudioBlock | null | undefined,
 1251: ) {
 1252:   const order = Array.isArray((block as any)?.signatureOrder)
 1253:     ? ((block as any).signatureOrder as string[]).map((key) => cleanText(key)).filter(Boolean)
 1254:     : [];
 1255: 
 1256:   const byKey = new Map(signatures.map((signature) => [signature.key, signature]));
 1257:   const used = new Set<string>();
 1258:   const ordered = order
 1259:     .map((key) => byKey.get(key))
 1260:     .filter((signature): signature is NonNullable<typeof signature> => {
 1261:       if (!signature || used.has(signature.key)) return false;
 1262: 
 1263:       used.add(signature.key);
 1264:       return true;
 1265:     });
 1266: 
 1267:   return [
 1268:     ...ordered,
 1269:     ...signatures.filter((signature) => !used.has(signature.key)),
 1270:   ];
 1271: }
 1272: 
 1273: function getReportTwoConfiguredSignatureCards(
 1274:   signatures: ReturnType<typeof getReportTwoSignatureCardsFromPayload>,
 1275:   block: StudioBlock | null | undefined,
 1276: ) {
 1277:   const hiddenKeys = getReportTwoSignatureHiddenKeys(block);
 1278: 
 1279:   return getReportTwoOrderedSignatureCards(signatures, block).filter(
 1280:     (signature) => !hiddenKeys.has(signature.key),
 1281:   );
 1282: }
 1283: 
 1284: function moveReportTwoSignatureOrder(
 1285:   currentOrder: unknown,
 1286:   allKeys: string[],
 1287:   key: string,
 1288:   direction: -1 | 1,
 1289: ) {
 1290:   const normalizedKeys = allKeys.map((item) => cleanText(item)).filter(Boolean);
 1291:   const current = Array.isArray(currentOrder)
 1292:     ? currentOrder.map((item) => cleanText(item)).filter(Boolean)
 1293:     : [];
 1294: 
 1295:   const order = [
 1296:     ...current.filter((item) => normalizedKeys.includes(item)),
 1297:     ...normalizedKeys.filter((item) => !current.includes(item)),
 1298:   ];
 1299: 
 1300:   const index = order.indexOf(key);
 1301:   const targetIndex = index + direction;
 1302: 
 1303:   if (index < 0 || targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= order.length) {
 1304:     return order;
 1305:   }
 1306: 
 1307:   const next = [...order];
 1308:   const currentItem = next[index];
 1309: 
 1310:   next[index] = next[targetIndex];
 1311:   next[targetIndex] = currentItem;
 1312: 
 1313:   return next;
 1314: }
 1315: 
 1316: function toggleReportTwoSignatureHiddenKey(currentHiddenKeys: unknown, key: string) {
 1317:   const hidden = new Set(
 1318:     Array.isArray(currentHiddenKeys)
 1319:       ? currentHiddenKeys.map((item) => cleanText(item)).filter(Boolean)
 1320:       : [],
 1321:   );
 1322: 
 1323:   if (hidden.has(key)) {
 1324:     hidden.delete(key);
 1325:   } else {
 1326:     hidden.add(key);
 1327:   }
 1328: 
 1329:   return Array.from(hidden);
 1330: }
 1331: 
 1332: function withReportTwoSignatureBlock(
 1333:   template: StudioTemplate,
 1334:   payload: SmartReportPayload,
 1335: ): StudioTemplate {
 1336:   const signatures = getReportTwoSignatureCardsFromPayload(payload);
 1337:   let signatureWasPrepared = false;
 1338: 
 1339:   return {
 1340:     ...template,
 1341:     pages: template.pages.map((page) => ({
 1342:       ...page,
 1343:       blocks: page.blocks.flatMap((block) => {
 1344:         if (!isReportTwoSignatureBlock(block)) {
 1345:           return [block];
 1346:         }
 1347: 
 1348:         if (signatureWasPrepared) {
 1349:           return [];
 1350:         }
 1351: 
 1352:         signatureWasPrepared = true;
 1353: 
 1354:         return [{
 1355:           ...block,
 1356:           kind: "signature-grid" as StudioBlockKind,
 1357:           title: block.title || "تواقيع الاعتماد",
 1358:           content: "",
 1359:           variant: block.variant || "soft",
 1360:           source: block.source || "manual",
 1361:           showTitle: block.showTitle ?? false,
 1362:           showMeta: false,
 1363:           align: "center",
 1364:           placement: "flow",
 1365:           signatures: getReportTwoConfiguredSignatureCards(signatures, block),
 1366:         } as StudioBlock];
 1367:       }),
 1368:     })),
 1369:   };
 1370: }
 1371: 
 1372: function getStructuredTableBlock(
 1373:   table: SmartReportTable,
 1374:   sourceBlock?: StudioBlock,
 1375: ): StudioBlock {
 1376:   const settings = table.settings || {};
 1377:   const savedTitle = cleanText(sourceBlock?.title);
 1378:   const normalizedSavedTitle = savedTitle
 1379:     .replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, "$1_$2")
 1380:     .toLowerCase()
 1381:     .replace(/[\s-]+/g, "_")
 1382:     .replace(/^selected_/, "")
 1383:     .replace(/_(?:json|values?)$/, "")
 1384:     .replace(/^_|_$/g, "");
 1385:   const normalizedSourceKey = cleanText(table.sourceFieldKey)
 1386:     .toLowerCase()
 1387:     .replace(/[\s-]+/g, "_")
 1388:     .replace(/^selected_/, "")
 1389:     .replace(/_(?:json|values?)$/, "")
 1390:     .replace(/^_|_$/g, "");
 1391:   const title =
 1392:     !savedTitle || normalizedSavedTitle === normalizedSourceKey ? table.title : savedTitle;
 1393:   const studentDataTable = isStudentDataTable({
 1394:     sourceFieldKey: table.sourceFieldKey,
 1395:     sourceTableId: table.id,
 1396:     columns: table.columns,
 1397:   });
 1398: 
 1399:   return {
 1400:     ...(sourceBlock || createBlock("structured-table")),
 1401:     id: sourceBlock?.id || `structured-table-${table.id.replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/gi, "-")}`,
 1402:     kind: "structured-table",
 1403:     title: studentDataTable ? table.title : title,
 1404:     ...(studentDataTable ? { studentTableTitle: table.title } : {}),
 1405:     visible: sourceBlock?.visible !== false,
 1406:     showTitle: studentDataTable ? true : sourceBlock?.showTitle !== false,
 1407:     source: sourceBlock?.source || "payload-table",
 1408:     sourceTableId: table.id,
 1409:     sourceFieldKey: table.sourceFieldKey,
 1410:     columns: table.columns.map((column) => column.label),
 1411:     columnWidths: table.columns.map((column) => column.width || 0),
 1412:     rows: table.rows.map((row) =>
 1413:       table.columns.map((column) => cleanText(row.cells[column.key])),
 1414:     ),
 1415:     rowMetadata: table.rows.map((row) => ({
 1416:       gender: row.metadata?.gender || null,
 1417:     })),
 1418:     tableSettings: {
 1419:       highlightHeader: true,
 1420:       rounded: true,
 1421:       colorTheme: "light-gray",
 1422:       repeatHeader: settings.repeatHeader !== false,
 1423:       compact: Boolean(settings.compact),
 1424:       stripedRows: settings.stripedRows !== false,
 1425:       highlightFirstColumn: Boolean(settings.highlightFirstColumn),
 1426:       ...(sourceBlock?.tableSettings || {}),
 1427:     },
 1428:   } as StudioBlock;
 1429: }
 1430: 
 1431: function withReportTwoStructuredTables(
 1432:   template: StudioTemplate,
 1433:   payload: SmartReportPayload,
 1434: ): StudioTemplate {
 1435:   const tables = Array.isArray(payload.tables)
 1436:     ? payload.tables.map(normalizeSmartReportTablePresentation)
 1437:     : [];
 1438:   if (!tables.length) {
 1439:     return normalizeStudentDataTableBlockOrder(template) as StudioTemplate;
 1440:   }
 1441: 
 1442:   const tableById = new Map(tables.map((table) => [table.id, table]));
 1443:   const boundTableIds = new Set<string>();
 1444:   let pages = template.pages.map((page) => ({
 1445:     ...page,
 1446:     blocks: page.blocks.flatMap((block) => {
 1447:       const canBindStructuredTable =
 1448:         block.kind === "structured-table" ||
 1449:         Boolean(cleanText(block.sourceTableId) || cleanText(block.sourceFieldKey));
 1450:       if (!canBindStructuredTable) return [block];
 1451:       const sourceTableId = cleanText(block.sourceTableId);
 1452:       const sourceFieldKey = cleanText(block.sourceFieldKey);
 1453:       const table =
 1454:         tableById.get(sourceTableId) ||
 1455:         tables.find((item) => item.sourceFieldKey === sourceFieldKey);
 1456:       if (!table) return [block];
 1457:       if (boundTableIds.has(table.id)) return [];
 1458:       boundTableIds.add(table.id);
 1459:       return [getStructuredTableBlock(table, block)];
 1460:     }),
 1461:   }));
 1462: 
 1463:   const missingTables = tables.filter((table) => !boundTableIds.has(table.id));
 1464:   if (!missingTables.length) {
 1465:     return normalizeStudentDataTableBlockOrder({ ...template, pages }) as StudioTemplate;
 1466:   }
 1467: 
 1468:   let targetPageIndex = -1;
 1469:   for (let index = pages.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
 1470:     if (pages[index].blocks.some((block) => block.kind === "dynamic-fields")) {
 1471:       targetPageIndex = index;
 1472:       break;
 1473:     }
 1474:   }
 1475:   if (targetPageIndex < 0) {
 1476:     targetPageIndex = pages.findIndex((page) => page.kind !== "evidence");
 1477:   }
 1478:   if (targetPageIndex < 0) targetPageIndex = 0;
 1479: 
 1480:   pages = pages.map((page, pageIndex) => {
 1481:     if (pageIndex !== targetPageIndex) return page;
 1482:     const insertionIndex = page.blocks.findIndex(
 1483:       (block) => block.kind === "evidence-gallery" || block.kind === "signature-grid",
 1484:     );
 1485:     const index = insertionIndex < 0 ? page.blocks.length : insertionIndex;
 1486:     return {
 1487:       ...page,
 1488:       blocks: [
 1489:         ...page.blocks.slice(0, index),
 1490:         ...missingTables.map((table) => getStructuredTableBlock(table)),
 1491:         ...page.blocks.slice(index),
 1492:       ],
 1493:     };
 1494:   });
 1495: 
 1496:   return normalizeStudentDataTableBlockOrder({ ...template, pages }) as StudioTemplate;
 1497: }
 1498: 
 1499: function normalizeReportTwoLogicalTemplate(
 1500:   template: StudioTemplate,
 1501:   payload: SmartReportPayload,
 1502: ) {
 1503:   return withSingleReportTwoEvidenceBlock(
 1504:     withReportTwoSignatureBlock(
 1505:       withReportTwoStructuredTables(template, payload),
 1506:       payload,
 1507:     ),
 1508:   );
 1509: }

 1511: function normalizeReportTwoBlockForRuntime(
 1512:   block: StudioBlock,
 1513:   previewCase: ReturnType<typeof getPreviewCase>,
 1514: ): StudioBlock {
 1515:   const sourceBlockId = cleanText(block.sourceBlockId) || block.id;
 1516: 
 1517:   if (isReportTwoDynamicFieldsBlock(block)) {
 1518:     return {
 1519:       ...block,
 1520:       sourceBlockId,
 1521:       kind: "dynamic-fields" as StudioBlockKind,
 1522:       dynamicFields: getDynamicFieldsForBlock(block, previewCase),
 1523:     };
 1524:   }
 1525: 
 1526:   if (block.kind === "evidence-gallery") {
 1527:     return {
 1528:       ...block,
 1529:       sourceBlockId,
 1530:       evidenceStartIndex: Math.max(0, Number(block.evidenceStartIndex || 0)),
 1531:       evidenceEmptyBehavior: previewCase.evidences.length
 1532:         ? block.evidenceEmptyBehavior
 1533:         : block.evidenceEmptyBehavior || "hide",
 1534:     };
 1535:   }
 1536: 
 1537:   return {
 1538:     ...block,
 1539:     sourceBlockId,
 1540:   };
 1541: }

 1544:   template: StudioTemplate,
 1545:   previewCase: ReturnType<typeof getPreviewCase>,
 1546: ): StudioTemplate {
 1547:   let evidenceWasPrepared = false;
 1548: 
 1549:   return {
 1550:     ...template,
 1551:     pages: template.pages.map((page) => ({
 1552:       ...page,
 1553:       blocks: page.blocks.flatMap((block) => {
 1554:         if (block.kind === "evidence-gallery") {
 1555:           if (evidenceWasPrepared) {
 1556:             return [];
 1557:           }

 1563:       }),
 1564:     })),

 1568: function withSingleReportTwoEvidenceBlock(template: StudioTemplate): StudioTemplate {
 1569:   let evidenceWasKept = false;
 1570: 
 1571:   return {
 1572:     ...template,
 1573:     pages: template.pages.map((page) => ({
 1574:       ...page,
 1575:       blocks: page.blocks.filter((block) => {
 1576:         if (block.kind !== "evidence-gallery") {
 1577:           return true;
 1578:         }
 1579: 
 1580:         if (evidenceWasKept) {
 1581:           return false;
 1582:         }
 1583: 
 1584:         evidenceWasKept = true;
 1585:         return true;
 1586:       }),
 1587:     })),
 1588:   };
 1589: }
 1590: function getWritableReportTwoPageId(
 1591:   activePageId: string,
 1592:   _activePage: StudioPage | undefined,
 1593:   template: StudioTemplate,
 1594: ) {
 1595:   const directPage = template.pages.find((page) => page.id === activePageId);
 1596: 
 1597:   if (directPage) return directPage.id;
 1598: 
 1599:   return template.pages[0]?.id || "";
 1600: }
 1601: 
 1602: function resolveReportTwoEquivalentPageId(
 1603:   pages: StudioPage[],
 1604:   activePageId: string,
 1605: ) {
 1606:   if (!pages.length) return "";
 1607:   if (pages.some((page) => page.id === activePageId)) return activePageId;
 1608: 
 1609:   return pages[0]?.id || "";
 1610: }
 1611: 
 1612: function reorderReportTwoPages(
 1613:   pages: StudioPage[],
 1614:   sourcePageId: string,
 1615:   direction: "previous" | "next",
 1616: ) {
 1617:   const index = pages.findIndex((page) => page.id === sourcePageId);
 1618: 
 1619:   if (index < 0) return pages;
 1620: 
 1621:   const targetIndex = direction === "previous" ? index - 1 : index + 1;
 1622: 
 1623:   if (targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= pages.length) {
 1624:     return pages;
 1625:   }
 1626: 
 1627:   const nextPages = [...pages];
 1628:   const [page] = nextPages.splice(index, 1);
 1629:   nextPages.splice(targetIndex, 0, page);
 1630: 
 1631:   return nextPages;
 1632: }

 1634: 
 1635: function isReportTwoDynamicFieldsBlock(block: StudioBlock | null | undefined) {
 1636:   if (!block) return false;
 1637: 
 1638:   const kind = cleanText(block.kind);
 1639:   const smartKind = cleanText((block as any).settings?.smartBlockKind);
 1640:   const title = cleanText(block.title);
 1641: 
 1642:   return (
 1643:     kind === "dynamic-fields" ||
 1644:     kind === "field-list" ||
 1645:     kind === "case-meta" ||
 1646:     kind === "student-summary" ||
 1647:     kind === "service-summary" ||
 1648:     smartKind === "dynamic-fields" ||
 1649:     smartKind === "field-list" ||
 1650:     title.includes("حقول") ||
 1651:     title.includes("بيانات الحالة")
 1652:   );
 1653: }
 1654: function getReportTwoLookupKey(value: unknown) {
 1655:   return cleanText(value).toLowerCase();
 1656: }
 1657: 
 1658: function getReportTwoDisplayText(value: unknown): string {
 1659:   if (Array.isArray(value)) {
 1660:     return value
 1661:       .map((item) => getReportTwoDisplayText(item))
 1662:       .filter(Boolean)
 1663:       .join("، ");
 1664:   }
 1665: 
 1666:   if (value && typeof value === "object") {
 1667:     const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
 1668: 
 1669:     const candidates = [
 1670:       record.displayValue,
 1671:       record.valueLabel,
 1672:       record.optionLabel,
 1673:       record.selectedOptionLabel,
 1674:       record.labelAr,
 1675:       record.nameAr,
 1676:       record.titleAr,
 1677:       record.label,
 1678:       record.name,
 1679:       record.title,
 1680:       record.text,
 1681:       record.value,
 1682:     ];
 1683: 
 1684:     for (const candidate of candidates) {
 1685:       const text = cleanText(candidate);
 1686: 
 1687:       if (text) return text;
 1688:     }
 1689: 
 1690:     return "";
 1691:   }
 1692: 
 1693:   return cleanText(value);
 1694: }
 1695: 
 1696: function getReportTwoOptionCollections(field: any) {
 1697:   return [
 1698:     field.options,
 1699:     field.dynamicFieldOptions,
 1700:     field.fieldOptions,
 1701:     field.choices,
 1702:     field.items,
 1703:     field.field?.options,
 1704:     field.field?.dynamicFieldOptions,
 1705:     field.dynamicField?.options,
 1706:     field.workflowField?.options,
 1707:   ].filter(Array.isArray) as any[][];
 1708: }
 1709: 
 1710: function getReportTwoOptionArabicLabel(field: any, rawValue: unknown) {
 1711:   const target = getReportTwoLookupKey(rawValue);
 1712: 
 1713:   if (!target) return "";
 1714: 
 1715:   for (const options of getReportTwoOptionCollections(field)) {
 1716:     for (const option of options) {
 1717:       const optionValues = [
 1718:         option.value,
 1719:         option.key,
 1720:         option.code,
 1721:         option.slug,
 1722:         option.id,
 1723:       ].map(getReportTwoLookupKey);
 1724: 
 1725:       if (!optionValues.includes(target)) {
 1726:         continue;
 1727:       }
 1728: 
 1729:       const label = getReportTwoDisplayText({
 1730:         displayValue: option.displayValue,
 1731:         valueLabel: option.valueLabel,
 1732:         optionLabel: option.optionLabel,
 1733:         labelAr: option.labelAr,
 1734:         nameAr: option.nameAr,
 1735:         titleAr: option.titleAr,
 1736:         label: option.label,
 1737:         name: option.name,
 1738:         title: option.title,
 1739:         text: option.text,
 1740:       });
 1741: 
 1742:       if (label) return label;
 1743:     }
 1744:   }
 1745: 
 1746:   return "";
 1747: }
 1748: 
 1749: function getReportTwoReadableFieldLabel(field: any, index: number) {
 1750:   const key =
 1751:     cleanText(field.key) ||
 1752:     cleanText(field.fieldKey) ||
 1753:     cleanText(field.name) ||
 1754:     cleanText(field.id) ||
 1755:     `field-${index + 1}`;
 1756: 
 1757:   const candidates = [
 1758:     field.displayLabel,
 1759:     field.fieldLabel,
 1760:     field.labelAr,
 1761:     field.nameAr,
 1762:     field.titleAr,
 1763:     field.label,
 1764:     field.title,
 1765:     field.field?.label,
 1766:     field.dynamicField?.label,
 1767:     field.workflowField?.label,
 1768:   ];
 1769: 
 1770:   for (const candidate of candidates) {
 1771:     const text = cleanText(candidate);
 1772: 
 1773:     if (text && text !== key) return text;
 1774:   }
 1775: 
 1776:   const fromMap = getReportTwoFieldLabel(key, field.label);
 1777: 
 1778:   if (fromMap && fromMap !== key) return fromMap;
 1779: 
 1780:   return key;
 1781: }
 1782: 
 1783: function uniqueReportTwoValueItems(items: string[]) {
 1784:   return Array.from(
 1785:     new Set(items.map((item) => cleanText(item)).filter(Boolean)),
 1786:   );
 1787: }
 1788: 
 1789: function getReportTwoReadableFieldValueItems(field: any) {
 1790:   const directArrays = [
 1791:     field.valueItems,
 1792:     field.values,
 1793:     field.items,
 1794:     field.selectedValues,
 1795:     field.selectedOptions,
 1796:     Array.isArray(field.value) ? field.value : null,
 1797:   ].filter(Array.isArray) as unknown[][];
 1798: 
 1799:   const items = uniqueReportTwoValueItems(
 1800:     directArrays.flatMap((entries) =>
 1801:       entries.map((entry) => getReportTwoDisplayText(entry)).filter(Boolean),
 1802:     ),
 1803:   );
 1804: 
 1805:   return items.length > 1 ? items : [];
 1806: }
 1807: 
 1808: function getReportTwoReadableFieldValue(field: any) {
 1809:   const readableItems = getReportTwoReadableFieldValueItems(field);
 1810: 
 1811:   if (readableItems.length > 1) {
 1812:     return readableItems.join("، ");
 1813:   }
 1814: 
 1815:   const explicitCandidates = [
 1816:     field.displayValue,
 1817:     field.formattedValue,
 1818:     field.valueLabel,
 1819:     field.labelValue,
 1820:     field.optionLabel,
 1821:     field.selectedOptionLabel,
 1822:     field.answerLabel,
 1823:     field.answerText,
 1824:     field.textValue,
 1825:     field.valueText,
 1826:   ];
 1827: 
 1828:   for (const candidate of explicitCandidates) {
 1829:     const text = getReportTwoDisplayText(candidate);
 1830: 
 1831:     if (text) return text;
 1832:   }
 1833: 
 1834:   const optionLabel = getReportTwoOptionArabicLabel(field, field.value);
 1835: 
 1836:   if (optionLabel) return optionLabel;
 1837: 
 1838:   const rawValue = cleanText(field.value);
 1839: 
 1840:   if (!rawValue) return "";
 1841: 
 1842:   const fromMap = getReportTwoFieldValue(rawValue);
 1843: 
 1844:   if (fromMap && fromMap !== rawValue) return fromMap;
 1845: 
 1846:   return rawValue;
 1847: }
 1848: 
 1849: function getReportTwoDynamicFieldsFromPreviewCase(
 1850:   previewCase: ReturnType<typeof getPreviewCase>,
 1851: ): ReportTwoDynamicField[] {
 1852:   const fields = (previewCase.values || [])
 1853:     .map((item: any, index: number) => {
 1854:       const key = cleanText(item.fieldKey) || `workflow-field-${index + 1}`;
 1855:       const label = cleanText(item.fieldLabel) || key || `حقل ${index + 1}`;
 1856:       const value = cleanText(item.value);
 1857:       const valueItems = Array.isArray(item.valueItems)
 1858:         ? uniqueReportTwoValueItems(item.valueItems)
 1859:         : [];
 1860: 
 1861:       return {
 1862:         id: key || `workflow-field-${index + 1}`,
 1863:         key,
 1864:         label,
 1865:         value,
 1866:         ...(valueItems.length > 1 ? { valueItems } : {}),
 1867:         visible: Boolean(label && (value || valueItems.length)),
 1868:       };
 1869:     })
 1870:     .filter((item) => item.label && (item.value || item.valueItems?.length))
 1871:     .filter(
 1872:       (item) =>
 1873:         !previewCase.hasStudentDataTable || !isStudentIdentityField(item),
 1874:     );
 1875: 
 1876:   return ensureUniqueReportTwoDynamicFieldIds(fields);
 1877: }
 1878: 
 1879: function getDynamicFieldsForBlock(
 1880:   block: StudioBlock | null,
 1881:   previewCase: ReturnType<typeof getPreviewCase>,
 1882: ) {
 1883:   const sourceFields = getReportTwoDynamicFieldsFromPreviewCase(previewCase);
 1884: 
 1885:   if (!block?.dynamicFields?.length) {
 1886:     return sourceFields;
 1887:   }
 1888: 
 1889:   const sourceByKey = new Map<string, ReportTwoDynamicField>();
 1890: 
 1891:   sourceFields.forEach((field) => {
 1892:     [field.id, field.key, field.label].forEach((value) => {
 1893:       const key = getReportTwoLookupKey(value);
 1894: 
 1895:       if (key) {
 1896:         sourceByKey.set(key, field);
 1897:       }
 1898:     });
 1899:   });
 1900: 
 1901:   const fields = block.dynamicFields
 1902:     .filter(
 1903:       (field) =>
 1904:         !previewCase.hasStudentDataTable || !isStudentIdentityField(field),
 1905:     )
 1906:     .map((field, index) => {
 1907:     const source =
 1908:       sourceByKey.get(getReportTwoLookupKey(field.key)) ||
 1909:       sourceByKey.get(getReportTwoLookupKey(field.id)) ||
 1910:       sourceByKey.get(getReportTwoLookupKey(field.label)) ||
 1911:       sourceFields[index];
 1912: 
 1913:     const key = field.key || source?.key || `dynamic-field-${index + 1}`;
 1914:     const configuredValue = cleanText(field.value);
 1915:     const configuredValueItems = Array.isArray(field.valueItems)
 1916:       ? uniqueReportTwoValueItems(field.valueItems)
 1917:       : [];
 1918:     const sourceValue = cleanText(source?.value);
 1919:     const sourceValueItems = Array.isArray(source?.valueItems)
 1920:       ? uniqueReportTwoValueItems(source.valueItems)
 1921:       : [];
 1922:     const matchesSourceValue =
 1923:       Boolean(configuredValue && sourceValue) &&
 1924:       getReportTwoLookupKey(configuredValue) === getReportTwoLookupKey(sourceValue);
 1925:     const shouldUseSourceValueItems =
 1926:       sourceValueItems.length > 1 &&
 1927:       ((configuredValueItems.length > 0 &&
 1928:         configuredValueItems.every((item) => isReportTwoTechnicalValue(item))) ||
 1929:         (configuredValueItems.length === 0 &&
 1930:           (!configuredValue ||
 1931:             isReportTwoTechnicalValue(configuredValue) ||
 1932:             matchesSourceValue)));
 1933:     const valueItems = shouldUseSourceValueItems
 1934:       ? sourceValueItems
 1935:       : configuredValueItems;
 1936:     const value =
 1937:       configuredValue && !shouldUseSourceValueItems
 1938:         ? configuredValue
 1939:         : sourceValue || getReportTwoFieldValue(field.value) || configuredValue;
 1940: 
 1941:     return {
 1942:       id: field.id || source?.id || `dynamic-field-${index + 1}`,
 1943:       key,
 1944:       label: field.label ?? cleanText(source?.label) ?? getReportTwoFieldLabel(field.key, field.label) ?? key,
 1945:       value,
 1946:       ...(valueItems.length > 1 ? { valueItems } : {}),
 1947:       visible: field.visible !== false,
 1948:     };
 1949:   });
 1950: 
 1951:   return ensureUniqueReportTwoDynamicFieldIds(fields);
 1952: }
 1953: function getReportTwoTableSettings(
 1954:   settings?: Record<string, any>,
 1955: ): ReportTwoTableSettings {
 1956:   const validThemes = ["light-gray", "soft-blue", "green", "none"] as const;
 1957:   const colorTheme = settings?.colorTheme;
 1958:   return {
 1959:     highlightHeader: settings?.highlightHeader !== false,
 1960:     highlightFirstColumn: settings?.highlightFirstColumn !== false,
 1961:     stripedRows: settings?.stripedRows !== false,
 1962:     rounded: settings?.rounded !== false,
 1963:     compact: Boolean(settings?.compact),
 1964:     repeatHeader: settings?.repeatHeader !== false,
 1965:     colorTheme: validThemes.includes(colorTheme) ? colorTheme : "light-gray",
 1966:   };
 1967: }
 1968: 
 1969: function normalizeReportTwoTableColumns(columns?: string[]) {
 1970:   const cleaned = Array.isArray(columns)
 1971:     ? columns.map((column) => cleanText(column)).filter(Boolean)
 1972:     : [];
 1973: 
 1974:   return cleaned.length ? cleaned : ["المجال", "الإجراء", "ملاحظات"];
 1975: }
 1976: 
 1977: function normalizeReportTwoTableRows(rows: unknown, columnsCount: number) {
 1978:   const sourceRows = Array.isArray(rows) && rows.length
 1979:     ? rows
 1980:     : [
 1981:         Array.from({ length: columnsCount }).map(() => ""),
 1982:         Array.from({ length: columnsCount }).map(() => ""),
 1983:         Array.from({ length: columnsCount }).map(() => ""),
 1984:       ];
 1985: 
 1986:   return sourceRows.map((row: any) => {
 1987:     const cells = Array.isArray(row) ? row : [];
 1988:     const normalized = Array.from({ length: columnsCount }).map((_, index) =>
 1989:       cleanText(cells[index]),
 1990:     );
 1991: 
 1992:     return normalized;
 1993:   });
 1994: }

 1996: function createReportTwoTableDraft(block: StudioBlock): ReportTwoTableDraft {
 1997:   const columns = normalizeReportTwoTableColumns(block.columns);
 1998:   const rows = normalizeReportTwoTableRows(block.rows, columns.length);
 1999: 
 2000:   return {
 2001:     blockId: block.id,
 2002:     title: cleanText(block.title) || "جدول",
 2003:     columns,
 2004:     rows,
 2005:     settings: getReportTwoTableSettings(block.tableSettings),
 2006:   };
 2007: }

 2009: function countReportTwoFilledCells(rows: string[][]) {
 2010:   return rows.reduce(
 2011:     (total, row) =>
 2012:       total + row.filter((cell) => cleanText(cell).length > 0).length,
 2013:     0,
 2014:   );
 2015: }

 2019:   title: string,
 2020: ): ReportTwoTableDraft {
 2021:   return {
 2022:     ...draft,
 2023:     title,
 2024:   };
 2025: }

 2027: function updateReportTwoTableColumn(
 2028:   draft: ReportTwoTableDraft,
 2029:   columnIndex: number,
 2030:   value: string,
 2031: ): ReportTwoTableDraft {
 2032:   const columns = draft.columns.map((column, index) =>
 2033:     index === columnIndex ? value : column,
 2034:   );
 2035: 
 2036:   return {
 2037:     ...draft,
 2038:     columns,
 2039:     rows: normalizeReportTwoTableRows(draft.rows, columns.length),
 2040:   };
 2041: }

 2043: function updateReportTwoTableCell(
 2044:   draft: ReportTwoTableDraft,
 2045:   rowIndex: number,
 2046:   columnIndex: number,
 2047:   value: string,
 2048: ): ReportTwoTableDraft {
 2049:   const rows = normalizeReportTwoTableRows(draft.rows, draft.columns.length);
 2050: 
 2051:   rows[rowIndex] = rows[rowIndex].map((cell, index) =>
 2052:     index === columnIndex ? value : cell,
 2053:   );
 2054: 
 2055:   return {
 2056:     ...draft,
 2057:     rows,
 2058:   };
 2059: }

 2061: function addReportTwoTableRow(draft: ReportTwoTableDraft): ReportTwoTableDraft {
 2062:   return {
 2063:     ...draft,
 2064:     rows: [
 2065:       ...normalizeReportTwoTableRows(draft.rows, draft.columns.length),
 2066:       Array.from({ length: draft.columns.length }).map(() => ""),
 2067:     ],
 2068:   };
 2069: }
 2070: 
 2071: function removeReportTwoTableRow(
 2072:   draft: ReportTwoTableDraft,
 2073:   rowIndex: number,
 2074: ): ReportTwoTableDraft {
 2075:   const rows = normalizeReportTwoTableRows(draft.rows, draft.columns.length);
 2076: 
 2077:   if (rows.length <= 1) {
 2078:     return draft;
 2079:   }
 2080: 
 2081:   return {
 2082:     ...draft,
 2083:     rows: rows.filter((_, index) => index !== rowIndex),
 2084:   };
 2085: }

 2087: function addReportTwoTableColumn(
 2088:   draft: ReportTwoTableDraft,
 2089: ): ReportTwoTableDraft {
 2090:   const columns = [...draft.columns, `عمود ${draft.columns.length + 1}`];
 2091: 
 2092:   return {
 2093:     ...draft,
 2094:     columns,
 2095:     rows: normalizeReportTwoTableRows(draft.rows, draft.columns.length).map(
 2096:       (row) => [...row, ""],
 2097:     ),
 2098:   };
 2099: }
 2100: 
 2101: function removeReportTwoTableColumn(
 2102:   draft: ReportTwoTableDraft,
 2103:   columnIndex: number,
 2104: ): ReportTwoTableDraft {
 2105:   if (draft.columns.length <= 1) {
 2106:     return draft;
 2107:   }
 2108: 
 2109:   const columns = draft.columns.filter((_, index) => index !== columnIndex);
 2110:   const rows = normalizeReportTwoTableRows(draft.rows, draft.columns.length).map(
 2111:     (row) => row.filter((_, index) => index !== columnIndex),
 2112:   );
 2113: 
 2114:   return {
 2115:     ...draft,
 2116:     columns,
 2117:     rows: normalizeReportTwoTableRows(rows, columns.length),
 2118:   };
 2119: }

 2135: function isReportTwoTechnicalValue(value: unknown) {
 2136:   const text = cleanText(value);
 2137: 
 2138:   if (!text) return false;
 2139: 
 2140:   return /^[a-z0-9_./-]+$/i.test(text) && /[a-z_]/i.test(text);
 2141: }

 2143: function getReportTwoFilledTableCells(block: StudioBlock) {
 2144:   const columns = normalizeReportTwoTableColumns(block.columns);
 2145:   const rows = normalizeReportTwoTableRows(block.rows, columns.length);
 2146: 
 2147:   return countReportTwoFilledCells(rows);
 2148: }

 2150: function getReportTwoSmartAlertSortValue(type: ReportTwoSmartAlertType) {
 2151:   if (type === "error") return 0;
 2152:   if (type === "warning") return 1;
 2153:   if (type === "info") return 2;
 2154: 
 2155:   return 3;
 2156: }

 2158: function getReportTwoSmartAlerts({
 2159:   visiblePreviewTemplate,
 2160:   previewCase,
 2161:   activeHeaderValues,
 2162:   activeLogoSettings,
 2163: }: {
 2164:   visiblePreviewTemplate: StudioTemplate;
 2165:   previewCase: ReturnType<typeof getPreviewCase>;
 2166:   activeHeaderValues: ReportTwoHeaderValues;
 2167:   activeLogoSettings: ReportTwoLogoSettings;
 2168: }) {
 2169:   const alerts: ReportTwoSmartAlert[] = [];
 2170:   const pages = visiblePreviewTemplate.pages || [];
 2171: 
 2172:   if (!pages.length) {
 2173:     alerts.push({
 2174:       id: "no-pages",
 2175:       type: "error",
 2176:       title: "لا توجد صفحات في التقرير",
 2177:       description: "أضف صفحة محتوى حتى يظهر التقرير بشكل صحيح.",
 2178:       action: "open-page",
 2179:     });
 2180: 
 2181:     return alerts;
 2182:   }
 2183: 
 2184:   if (!cleanText(activeLogoSettings.url)) {
 2185:     alerts.push({
 2186:       id: "missing-logo",
 2187:       type: "warning",
 2188:       title: "الشعار غير محدد",
 2189:       description: "ضع شعارًا للتقرير أو استخدم الشعار الافتراضي.",
 2190:       action: "restore-header",
 2191:     });
 2192:   }
 2193: 
 2194:   const missingHeaderFields = Object.entries(activeHeaderValues).filter(
 2195:     ([, value]) => !cleanText(value),
 2196:   );
 2197: 
 2198:   if (missingHeaderFields.length) {
 2199:     alerts.push({
 2200:       id: "missing-header-values",
 2201:       type: "warning",
 2202:       title: "الترويسة غير مكتملة",
 2203:       description: "بعض قيم أعلى الصفحة فارغة. يمكن استعادتها من بيانات الحالة.",
 2204:       action: "restore-header",
 2205:     });
 2206:   }
 2207: 
 2208:   pages.forEach((page, pageIndex) => {
 2209:     if (!page.blocks.length) {
 2210:       alerts.push({
 2211:         id: `empty-page-${page.id}`,
 2212:         type: "warning",
 2213:         title: "صفحة بدون محتوى",
 2214:         description: `الصفحة ${pageIndex + 1} لا تحتوي على بلوكات.`,
 2215:         pageId: page.id,
 2216:         action: "open-page",
 2217:       });
 2218:     }
 2219: 
 2220:     page.blocks.forEach((block) => {
 2221:       if (isReportTwoDynamicFieldsBlock(block)) {
 2222:         const fields = getDynamicFieldsForBlock(block, previewCase);
 2223:         const visibleFields = fields.filter((field) => field.visible !== false);
 2224:         const technicalFields = visibleFields.filter(
 2225:           (field) =>
 2226:             isReportTwoTechnicalValue(field.label) ||
 2227:             isReportTwoTechnicalValue(field.value),
 2228:         );
 2229: 
 2230:         if (!visibleFields.length) {
 2231:           alerts.push({
 2232:             id: `no-visible-fields-${page.id}-${block.id}`,
 2233:             type: "warning",
 2234:             title: "لا توجد حقول ظاهرة",
 2235:             description: "بلوك الحقول موجود، لكن كل الحقول مخفية أو فارغة.",
 2236:             pageId: page.id,
 2237:             blockId: block.id,
 2238:             action: "open-block",
 2239:           });
 2240:         }
 2241: 
 2242:         if (visibleFields.length > 14) {
 2243:           alerts.push({
 2244:             id: `too-many-fields-${page.id}-${block.id}`,
 2245:             type: "warning",
 2246:             title: "حقول كثيرة في صفحة واحدة",
 2247:             description: "عدد الحقول كبير وقد يصعّب قراءة التقرير. أخفِ غير المهم أو انقل جزءًا لصفحة أخرى.",
 2248:             pageId: page.id,
 2249:             blockId: block.id,
 2250:             action: "open-block",
 2251:           });
 2252:         }
 2253: 
 2254:         if (technicalFields.length) {
 2255:           alerts.push({
 2256:             id: `technical-fields-${page.id}-${block.id}`,
 2257:             type: "warning",
 2258:             title: "حقول تقنية ظاهرة",
 2259:             description: "توجد أسماء أو قيم تقنية مثل activity_domain أو program_02. يمكن إخفاؤها تلقائيًا.",
 2260:             pageId: page.id,
 2261:             blockId: block.id,
 2262:             action: "hide-technical-fields",
 2263:           });
 2264:         }
 2265:       }
 2266: 
 2267:       if (block.kind === "evidence-gallery") {
 2268:         const evidenceCount = previewCase.evidences.length;
 2269:         const perPage = getEvidencePerPageFromBlock(block);
 2270: 
 2271:         if (!evidenceCount) {
 2272:           alerts.push({
 2273:             id: `no-evidence-${page.id}-${block.id}`,
 2274:             type: "info",
 2275:             title: "لا توجد شواهد",
 2276:             description: "التقرير يحتوي على بلوك شواهد، لكن لا توجد مرفقات في الحالة.",
 2277:             pageId: page.id,
 2278:             blockId: block.id,
 2279:             action: "open-block",
 2280:           });
 2281:         }
 2282: 
 2283:         if (evidenceCount > perPage) {
 2284:           alerts.push({
 2285:             id: `evidence-overflow-${page.id}-${block.id}`,
 2286:             type: "info",
 2287:             title: "الشواهد موزعة على صفحات",
 2288:             description: `يوجد ${evidenceCount} شواهد، ويظهر ${perPage} في الصفحة. الباقي ينتقل تلقائيًا لصفحات شواهد.`,
 2289:             pageId: page.id,
 2290:             blockId: block.id,
 2291:             action: "focus-preview",
 2292:           });
 2293:         }
 2294:       }
 2295: 
 2296:       if (block.kind === "signature-grid") return 34;
 2297: 
 2298:   if (block.kind === "report-one-table") {
 2299:         const columns = normalizeReportTwoTableColumns(block.columns);
 2300:         const rows = normalizeReportTwoTableRows(block.rows, columns.length);
 2301:         const filledCells = getReportTwoFilledTableCells(block);
 2302: 
 2303:         if (!filledCells) {
 2304:           alerts.push({
 2305:             id: `empty-table-${page.id}-${block.id}`,
 2306:             type: "info",
 2307:             title: "جدول فارغ",
 2308:             description: "يوجد جدول بدون محتوى. عدّله أو احذفه إذا لم يكن مطلوبًا.",
 2309:             pageId: page.id,
 2310:             blockId: block.id,
 2311:             action: "open-block",
 2312:           });
 2313:         }
 2314: 
 2315:         if (columns.length > 4 || rows.length > 7) {
 2316:           alerts.push({
 2317:             id: `large-table-${page.id}-${block.id}`,
 2318:             type: "warning",
 2319:             title: "جدول كبير",
 2320:             description: "الجدول كبير وقد لا يكون مريحًا داخل A4. يفضّل تقليل الأعمدة أو نقله لصفحة مستقلة.",
 2321:             pageId: page.id,
 2322:             blockId: block.id,
 2323:             action: "open-block",
 2324:           });
 2325:         }
 2326:       }
 2327: 
 2328:       if (
 2329:         (block.kind === "section-text" || block.kind === "multi-paragraph") &&
 2330:         cleanText(block.content).length > 700
 2331:       ) {
 2332:         alerts.push({
 2333:           id: `long-text-${page.id}-${block.id}`,
 2334:           type: "warning",
 2335:           title: "فقرة طويلة",
 2336:           description: "الفقرة طويلة وقد تضغط الصفحة. اختصرها أو انقل جزءًا منها لبلوك جديد.",
 2337:           pageId: page.id,
 2338:           blockId: block.id,
 2339:           action: "open-block",
 2340:         });
 2341:       }
 2342:     });
 2343:   });
 2344: 
 2345:   if (!alerts.length) {
 2346:     alerts.push({
 2347:       id: "ready",
 2348:       type: "success",
 2349:       title: "التقرير يبدو جيدًا",
 2350:       description: "لا توجد تنبيهات مهمة حاليًا. يمكنك المتابعة بأمان.",
 2351:       action: "focus-preview",
 2352:     });
 2353:   }
 2354: 
 2355:   return alerts.sort(
 2356:     (firstAlert, secondAlert) =>
 2357:       getReportTwoSmartAlertSortValue(firstAlert.type) -
 2358:       getReportTwoSmartAlertSortValue(secondAlert.type),
 2359:   );
 2360: }

 2362: function getReportTwoFinalCheckItems({
 2363:   smartAlerts,
 2364:   visiblePreviewTemplate,
 2365:   activeHeaderValues,
 2366:   activeLogoSettings,
 2367:   lastAutoSavedAt,
 2368: }: {
 2369:   smartAlerts: ReportTwoSmartAlert[];
 2370:   visiblePreviewTemplate: StudioTemplate;
 2371:   activeHeaderValues: ReportTwoHeaderValues;
 2372:   activeLogoSettings: ReportTwoLogoSettings;
 2373:   lastAutoSavedAt: string;
 2374: }): ReportTwoFinalCheckItem[] {
 2375:   const errors = smartAlerts.filter((alert) => alert.type === "error");
 2376:   const warnings = smartAlerts.filter((alert) => alert.type === "warning");
 2377:   const pages = visiblePreviewTemplate.pages || [];
 2378:   const hasPages = pages.length > 0;
 2379:   const hasBlocks = pages.some((page) => page.blocks.length > 0);
 2380:   const headerComplete = Object.values(activeHeaderValues).every((value) =>
 2381:     Boolean(cleanText(value)),
 2382:   );
 2383:   const logoReady = Boolean(cleanText(activeLogoSettings.url));
 2384: 
 2385:   return [
 2386:     {
 2387:       id: "pages",
 2388:       label: "صفحات التقرير موجودة",
 2389:       description: hasPages
 2390:         ? `عدد الصفحات الحالية: ${pages.length}.`
 2391:         : "لا توجد صفحات داخل التقرير.",
 2392:       passed: hasPages,
 2393:       required: true,
 2394:     },
 2395:     {
 2396:       id: "blocks",
 2397:       label: "يوجد محتوى داخل التقرير",
 2398:       description: hasBlocks
 2399:         ? "التقرير يحتوي على بلوكات ومحتوى قابل للعرض."
 2400:         : "أضف بلوكًا واحدًا على الأقل قبل الاعتماد.",
 2401:       passed: hasBlocks,
 2402:       required: true,
 2403:     },
 2404:     {
 2405:       id: "header",
 2406:       label: "ترويسة التقرير مكتملة",
 2407:       description: headerComplete
 2408:         ? "قيم الترويسة الأساسية مكتملة."
 2409:         : "هناك قيمة أو أكثر فارغة في الترويسة.",
 2410:       passed: headerComplete,
 2411:       required: true,
 2412:     },
 2413:     {
 2414:       id: "logo",
 2415:       label: "الشعار جاهز",
 2416:       description: logoReady
 2417:         ? "يوجد شعار مستخدم في الترويسة."
 2418:         : "اختر شعارًا أو استعد الشعار الافتراضي.",
 2419:       passed: logoReady,
 2420:       required: false,
 2421:     },
 2422:     {
 2423:       id: "errors",
 2424:       label: "لا توجد أخطاء مانعة",
 2425:       description: errors.length
 2426:         ? `يوجد ${errors.length} خطأ مانع يحتاج إصلاحًا.`
 2427:         : "لا توجد أخطاء مانعة حاليًا.",
 2428:       passed: errors.length === 0,
 2429:       required: true,
 2430:     },
 2431:     {
 2432:       id: "warnings",
 2433:       label: "التنبيهات تمت مراجعتها",
 2434:       description: warnings.length
 2435:         ? `يوجد ${warnings.length} تنبيه. يمكن الاعتماد بعد مراجعتها.`
 2436:         : "لا توجد تنبيهات مهمة.",
 2437:       passed: warnings.length === 0,
 2438:       required: false,
 2439:     },
 2440:     {
 2441:       id: "draft",
 2442:       label: "المسودة محفوظة",
 2443:       description: lastAutoSavedAt
 2444:         ? `آخر حفظ: ${formatReportTwoSavedAt(lastAutoSavedAt)}.`
 2445:         : "سيتم حفظ المسودة تلقائيًا قبل التأكيد.",
 2446:       passed: Boolean(lastAutoSavedAt),
 2447:       required: false,
 2448:     },
 2449:   ];
 2450: }
 2451: 
 2452: function getReportTwoFinalCheckPassed(items: ReportTwoFinalCheckItem[]) {
 2453:   return items.every((item) => !item.required || item.passed);
 2454: }
 2455: function getReportTwoPreparedExecutionSummary(payload: SmartReportPayload) {
 2456:   return cleanText(
 2457:     (payload as any)?.narrative?.body ||
 2458:       (payload as any)?.executionSummary ||
 2459:       (payload as any)?.summary ||
 2460:       "",
 2461:   );
 2462: }
 2463: 
 2464: function isReportTwoExecutionSummaryPlaceholderText(input: unknown) {
 2465:   const content = cleanText(input).toLowerCase();
 2466: 
 2467:   if (!content) return true;
 2468:   if (content.includes("workflow")) return true;
 2469:   if (content.includes("تم توثيق هذا الجزء")) return true;
 2470:   if (content.includes("اكتب النص هنا")) return true;
 2471:   if (content.includes("{{execution")) return true;
 2472:   if (content.includes("{{report.execution")) return true;
 2473:   if (content.includes("{{case.execution")) return true;
 2474: 
 2475:   return false;
 2476: }
 2477: 
 2478: function isReportTwoExecutionSummaryBlock(block: StudioBlock) {
 2479:   if (
 2480:     block.kind !== "section-text" &&
 2481:     block.kind !== "multi-paragraph" &&
 2482:     block.kind !== "plain-text"
 2483:   ) {
 2484:     return false;
 2485:   }
 2486: 
 2487:   const title = cleanText(block.title).toLowerCase();
 2488:   const content = cleanText(block.content).toLowerCase();
 2489: 
 2490:   if (title.includes("وصف") && title.includes("تنفيذ")) return true;
 2491:   if (content.includes("workflow")) return true;
 2492:   if (content.includes("تم توثيق هذا الجزء")) return true;
 2493: 
 2494:   return false;
 2495: }
 2496: 
 2497: function shouldAutoApplyReportTwoExecutionSummary(block: StudioBlock) {
 2498:   if (!isReportTwoExecutionSummaryBlock(block)) {
 2499:     return false;
 2500:   }
 2501: 
 2502:   const rawBlock = block as any;
 2503: 
 2504:   if (rawBlock.autoFilledFromExecutionSummary === true) {
 2505:     return true;
 2506:   }
 2507: 
 2508:   return isReportTwoExecutionSummaryPlaceholderText(block.content);
 2509: }
 2510: 
 2511: function applyReportTwoPreparedExecutionSummary(
 2512:   template: StudioTemplate,
 2513:   payload: SmartReportPayload,
 2514: ): StudioTemplate {
 2515:   const showExecutionDescriptionInReport =
 2516:     (payload as any)?.narrative?.visible !== false;
 2517: 
 2518:   if (!showExecutionDescriptionInReport) {
 2519:     return {
 2520:       ...template,
 2521:       pages: template.pages.map((page) => ({
 2522:         ...page,
 2523:         blocks: page.blocks.filter(
 2524:           (block) => !isReportTwoExecutionSummaryBlock(block),
 2525:         ),
 2526:       })),
 2527:     };
 2528:   }
 2529: 
 2530:   const summary = getReportTwoPreparedExecutionSummary(payload);
 2531: 
 2532:   if (!summary) return template;
 2533: 
 2534:   let replaced = false;
 2535: 
 2536:   return {
 2537:     ...template,
 2538:     pages: template.pages.map((page) => ({
 2539:       ...page,
 2540:       blocks: page.blocks.map((block) => {
 2541:         if (replaced || !shouldAutoApplyReportTwoExecutionSummary(block)) {
 2542:           return block;
 2543:         }
 2544: 
 2545:         replaced = true;
 2546: 
 2547:         return {
 2548:           ...block,
 2549:           content: summary,
 2550:           autoFilledFromExecutionSummary: true,
 2551:         };
 2552:       }),
 2553:     })),
 2554:   };
 2555: }
 2556: function getBlockKindName(kind: StudioBlockKind) {
 2557:   if (kind === "hero-title") return "عنوان رئيسي";
 2558:   if (kind === "meta-strip") return "شريط بيانات";
 2559:   if (kind === "plain-text") return "نص بسيط";
 2560:   if (kind === "section-text") return "فقرة";
 2561:   if (kind === "multi-paragraph") return "فقرات";
 2562:   if (kind === "highlight-note") return "ملاحظة بارزة";
 2563:   if (kind === "bullet-list") return "قائمة";
 2564:   if (kind === "dynamic-fields") return "حقول الحالة";
 2565:   if (kind === "evidence-gallery") return "الشواهد";
 2566:   if (kind === "closing-note") return "خاتمة";
 2567:   if (kind === "report-one-table") return "جدول";
 2568:   if (kind === "structured-table") return "جدول بيانات";
 2569:   if (kind === "signature-grid") return "تواقيع الاعتماد";
 2570: 
 2571:   return "بلوك";
 2572: }

 2897:   function fixReportTwoSmartAlert(alert: ReportTwoSmartAlert) {
 2898:     if (alert.action === "hide-technical-fields") {
 2899:       setTemplate((current) => ({
 2900:         ...current,
 2901:         pages: current.pages.map((page) => ({

 2919:               ...block,

 2926:       return;

 2930:       setHeaderValues(null);
 2931:       setHeaderAlignments(null);
 2932:       setLogoSettings(null);
 2933:       return;
 2934:     }
 2935:

 3138:           }
 3139: 
 3140:           .report-two-snapshot-approved-root .pdf-report-page:last-child {
 3141:             break-after: auto;
 3142:             page-break-after: auto;
 3143:           }
 3144:         }
 3145:       </style>
 3146:       ${clone.outerHTML}
 3147:     `;
 3148:   }

 3150:   async function createReportTwoApprovedSnapshotInternal(options?: {
 3151:     showSuccessPopup?: boolean;
 3152:     allowReuseExisting?: boolean;
 3153:   }) {
 3154:     const showSuccessPopup = options?.showSuccessPopup !== false;
 3155:     const allowReuseExisting = options?.allowReuseExisting !== false;
 3156: 
 3157:     if (reportTwoApprovalSubmitting) return null;
 3158: 
 3159:     if (approvedSnapshot && allowReuseExisting) {
 3160:       return approvedSnapshot;
 3161:     }
 3162: 
 3163:     if (!signedVisiblePreviewTemplate.pages.length) {
 3164:       setReportTwoActionModal({
 3165:         title: "اعتماد التقرير",
 3166:         message: "لا توجد صفحات جاهزة للاعتماد.",
 3167:       });
 3168:       return null;
 3169:     }
 3170: 
 3171:     if (!signedVisiblePreviewTemplate.pages.length) {
 3172:       setPopup({
 3173:         type: "alert",
 3174:         title: "اعتماد التقرير",
 3175:         message: "لا توجد صفحات جاهزة للاعتماد.",
 3176:       });
 3177:       return null;
 3178:     }
 3179: 
 3180:     const snapshotHtml = buildReportTwoSnapshotHtml();
 3181: 
 3182:     if (!snapshotHtml.trim()) {
 3183:       setReportTwoActionModal({
 3184:         title: "اعتماد التقرير",
 3185:         message: "تعذر التقاط معاينة التقرير الحالية للاعتماد.",
 3186:       });
 3187:       return null;
 3188:     }
 3189: 
 3190:     if (!snapshotHtml.trim()) {
 3191:       setPopup({
 3192:         type: "alert",
 3193:         title: "اعتماد التقرير",
 3194:         message: "تعذر التقاط معاينة التقرير الحالية للاعتماد.",
 3195:       });
 3196:       return null;
 3197:     }
 3198: 
 3199:     setReportTwoApprovalSubmitting(true);
 3200: 
 3201:     try {
 3202:       const activeTemplate = templates.find(
 3203:         (item) => item.id === selectedTemplateOptionId,
 3204:       );
 3205:       const response = await fetch(
 3206:         `/api/dashboard/report-2/cases/${encodeURIComponent(caseId)}/approve`,
 3207:         {
 3208:           method: "POST",
 3209:           headers: {
 3210:             "Content-Type": "application/json",
 3211:           },
 3212:           body: JSON.stringify({
 3213:             reportTitle: getReportTwoSnapshotTitle(),
 3214:             templateId: selectedTemplateOptionId || null,
 3215:             templateName: activeTemplate?.name || template.name || null,
 3216:             variantId: selectedVariantId || null,
 3217:             snapshotPayload: preparedPayload,
 3218:             snapshotTemplateJson: signedVisiblePreviewTemplate,
 3219:             snapshotPagesJson: signedVisiblePreviewTemplate.pages,
 3220:             snapshotHtml,
 3221:             editorState: createReportTwoDraftSnapshot(),
 3222:             approvedEditConfirmed: persistedReport?.status === "APPROVED",
 3223:           }),
 3224:         },
 3225:       );
 3226:       const text = await response.text();
 3227:       let result: any = {};
 3228: 
 3229:       try {
 3230:         result = text ? JSON.parse(text) : {};
 3231:       } catch {
 3232:         result = {
 3233:           error: text || `HTTP ${response.status}`,
 3234:         };
 3235:       }
 3236: 
 3237:       if (!response.ok) {
 3238:         throw new Error(
 3239:           result?.details ||
 3240:             result?.error ||
 3241:             `APPROVAL_FAILED_${response.status}`,
 3242:         );
 3243:       }
 3244: 
 3245:       const nextApprovedSnapshot = {
 3246:         id: result.snapshot?.id,
 3247:         previewUrl:
 3248:           result.previewUrl ||
 3249:           `/dashboard/report-2/snapshots/${result.snapshot?.id}/preview`,
 3250:       };
 3251: 
 3252:       setApprovedSnapshot(nextApprovedSnapshot);
 3253:       setPersistedReport({
 3254:         id: result.snapshot.id,
 3255:         status: "APPROVED",
 3256:         version: result.snapshot.version,
 3257:         approvedAt: result.snapshot.approvedAt,
 3258:         editorState: result.snapshot.editorState,
 3259:         previewUrl: nextApprovedSnapshot.previewUrl,
 3260:       });
 3261: 
 3262:       if (showSuccessPopup) {
 3263:         setReportTwoActionModal({
 3264:           title: "تم اعتماد التقرير",
 3265:           message: "تم حفظ نسخة ثابتة من التقرير في أرشيف التقارير المعتمدة.",
 3266:           linkHref: nextApprovedSnapshot.previewUrl,
 3267:           linkLabel: "استعراض التقرير المعتمد",
 3268:         });
 3269:         return nextApprovedSnapshot;
 3270:       }
 3271: 
 3272:       if (showSuccessPopup) {
 3273:         setPopup({
 3274:           type: "alert",
 3275:           title: "تم اعتماد التقرير",
 3276:           message: "تم حفظ نسخة ثابتة من التقرير في أرشيف التقارير المعتمدة.",
 3277:         });
 3278:       }
 3279: 
 3280:       return nextApprovedSnapshot;
 3281:     } catch (error) {
 3282:       console.error(error);
 3283:       setReportTwoActionModal({
 3284:         title: "اعتماد التقرير",
 3285:         message: "تعذر اعتماد التقرير. حاول مرة أخرى.",
 3286:       });
 3287:       return null;
 3288:       setPopup({
 3289:         type: "alert",
 3290:         title: "اعتماد التقرير",
 3291:         message: "تعذر اعتماد التقرير. حاول مرة أخرى.",
 3292:       });
 3293:       return null;
 3294:     } finally {
 3295:       setReportTwoApprovalSubmitting(false);
 3296:     }
 3297:   }

 3299:   async function approveReportTwoSnapshot() {
 3300:     if (approvedSnapshot) return;
 3301: 
 3302:     await createReportTwoApprovedSnapshotInternal({
 3303:       showSuccessPopup: true,
 3304:       allowReuseExisting: false,
 3305:     });

 3310:         type: "alert",
 3311:         title: "اعتماد التقرير",

 3318:

 3321:         type: "alert",

 3324:       });
 3325:       return;
 3326:     }

 3328:     setReportTwoApprovalSubmitting(true);
 3329:

 3331:       const activeTemplate = templates.find(
 3332:         (item) => item.id === selectedTemplateOptionId,
 3333:       );
 3334:       const response = await fetch(
 3335:         `/api/dashboard/report-2/cases/${encodeURIComponent(caseId)}/approve`,
 3336:         {
 3337:           method: "POST",
 3338:           headers: {
 3339:             "Content-Type": "application/json",
 3340:           },

 3342:             reportTitle:
 3343:               (previewCase as any)?.title ||
 3344:               (previewCase as any)?.caseTitle ||
 3345:               (preparedPayload as any)?.caseInfo?.title ||
 3346:               "تقرير معتمد",
 3347:             templateId: selectedTemplateOptionId || null,
 3348:             templateName: activeTemplate?.name || template.name || null,
 3349:             variantId: selectedVariantId || null,
 3350:             snapshotPayload: preparedPayload,
 3351:             snapshotTemplateJson: signedVisiblePreviewTemplate,
 3352:             snapshotPagesJson: signedVisiblePreviewTemplate.pages,
 3353:             snapshotHtml,
 3354:           }),
 3355:         },
 3356:       );
 3357:       const text = await response.text();
 3358:       let result: any = {};
 3359: 
 3360:       try {
 3361:         result = text ? JSON.parse(text) : {};
 3362:       } catch {
 3363:         result = {
 3364:           error: text || `HTTP ${response.status}`,
 3365:         };
 3366:       }
 3367: 
 3368:       if (!response.ok) {
 3369:         throw new Error(
 3370:           result?.details ||
 3371:             result?.error ||
 3372:             `APPROVAL_FAILED_${response.status}`,
 3373:         );
 3374:       }
 3375: 
 3376:       setApprovedSnapshot({
 3377:         id: result.snapshot?.id,
 3378:         previewUrl:
 3379:           result.previewUrl ||
 3380:           `/dashboard/report-2/snapshots/${result.snapshot?.id}/preview`,
 3381:       });
 3382:       setPopup({
 3383:         type: "alert",
 3384:         title: "تم اعتماد التقرير",
 3385:         message: "تم حفظ نسخة ثابتة من التقرير في أرشيف التقارير المعتمدة.",
 3386:       });
 3387:     } catch (error) {
 3388:       console.error(error);
 3389:       setPopup({
 3390:         type: "alert",
 3391:         title: "اعتماد التقرير",
 3392:         message: "تعذر اعتماد التقرير. حاول مرة أخرى.",
 3393:       });
 3394:     } finally {
 3395:       setReportTwoApprovalSubmitting(false);
 3396:     }

 3399:   async function exportReportTwoPdfInternal(options?: {
 3400:     fileName?: string;
 3401:     snapshot?: ReturnType<typeof buildReportTwoPdfExportSnapshot>;
 3402:   }) {
 3403:     if (reportTwoPdfExporting) return null;
 3404: 
 3405:     if (!visiblePreviewTemplate.pages.length) {
 3406:       setReportTwoActionModal({
 3407:         title: "تصدير PDF",
 3408:         message: "لا توجد صفحات جاهزة للتصدير.",
 3409:       });
 3410:       return null;
 3411:     }
 3412: 
 3413:     try {
 3414:       const fileName = options?.fileName || getReportTwoPdfFileName();
 3415:       const result = await runPrintExport({
 3416:         exportUrl: `/api/dashboard/report-2/cases/${encodeURIComponent(caseId)}/export/pdf`,
 3417:         fileName,
 3418:         method: "POST",
 3419:         body: {
 3420:           fileName,
 3421:           snapshot: options?.snapshot || buildReportTwoPdfExportSnapshot(),
 3422:         },
 3423:         blockedTitle: "معاينة الطباعة",
 3424:         blockedMessage:
 3425:           "تم حظر فتح نافذة المعاينة تلقائياً. استخدم الزر أدناه لفتح معاينة الطباعة في نافذة جديدة.",
 3426:         errorTitle: "تصدير PDF",
 3427:         errorMessage: "تعذر تصدير التقرير. حاول مرة أخرى.",
 3428:       });
 3429: 
 3430:       return result === "downloaded"
 3431:         ? ("downloaded" as const)
 3432:         : result === "opened" || result === "blocked"
 3433:           ? ("preview-fallback" as const)
 3434:           : null;
 3435:     } catch (error) {
 3436:       console.error(error);
 3437:       setReportTwoActionModal({
 3438:         title: "تصدير PDF",
 3439:         message: "تعذر تصدير التقرير. حاول مرة أخرى.",
 3440:       });
 3441:       return null;
 3442:     }
 3443:   }

 3445:   async function exportReportTwoPdf() {
 3446:     await exportReportTwoPdfInternal();
 3447:   }

 3450:     if (reportTwoApprovalSubmitting || reportTwoPdfExporting) return;
 3451: 
 3452:     const approval = await createReportTwoApprovedSnapshotInternal({
 3453:       showSuccessPopup: false,
 3454:       allowReuseExisting: true,
 3455:     });
 3456: 
 3457:     if (!approval) {
 3458:       return;

 3462:       fileName: getReportTwoSnapshotTitle(),

 3467:       setReportTwoActionModal({

 3469:         message: "تم حفظ التقرير واعتماده، وبدأ تحميل PDF.",
 3470:         linkHref: approval.previewUrl,
 3471:         linkLabel: "استعراض التقرير المعتمد",
 3472:       });
 3473:       return;

 3475:       setPopup({
 3476:         type: "alert",
 3477:         title: "حفظ وتحميل التقرير",
 3478:         message: "تم حفظ نسخة التقرير وبدأ تنزيل ملف PDF.",
 3479:       });

 3496:   function uploadReportTwoLogo(file: File | null) {
 3497:     if (!file) return;
 3498: 
 3499:     const reader = new FileReader();
 3500: 
 3501:     reader.onload = () => {
 3502:       const result = String(reader.result || "");
 3503: 
 3504:       if (!result) return;
 3505: 
 3506:       updateReportTwoLogoSettings({
 3507:         url: result,
 3508:         filter: "none",
 3509:       });
 3510:     };
 3511: 
 3512:     reader.readAsDataURL(file);
 3513:   }

 3551:   function createReportTwoDraftSnapshot(): ReportTwoDraftSnapshot {
 3552:     return {
 3553:       version: 1,
 3554:       savedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
 3555:       selectedTemplateOptionId,
 3556:       activeSavedRuntimeTemplateId,
 3557:       runtimeTemplateName,
 3558:       template: cloneReportTwoTemplate(template),
 3559:       headerValues,
 3560:       headerAlignments,
 3561:       logoSettings,
 3562:       activePageId,
 3563:       selectedBlockId,
 3564:       finalCheckConfirmedAt,
 3565:     };
 3566:   }

 3568:   function restoreReportTwoDraftSnapshot(snapshot: ReportTwoDraftSnapshot) {
 3569:     restoringDraftRef.current = true;
 3570: 
 3571:     setSelectedTemplateOptionId(snapshot.selectedTemplateOptionId || selectedTemplateOptionId);
 3572:     setActiveSavedRuntimeTemplateId(snapshot.activeSavedRuntimeTemplateId || "");
 3573:     setRuntimeTemplateName(snapshot.runtimeTemplateName || "");
 3574:     const restoredTemplate = normalizeReportTwoLogicalTemplate(
 3575:       cloneReportTwoTemplate(snapshot.template),
 3576:       preparedPayload,
 3577:     );
 3578:     setTemplate(restoredTemplate);
 3579:     setHeaderValues(snapshot.headerValues || null);
 3580:     setHeaderAlignments(snapshot.headerAlignments || null);
 3581:     setLogoSettings(snapshot.logoSettings || null);
 3582:     setActivePageId(
 3583:       resolveReportTwoEquivalentPageId(
 3584:         restoredTemplate.pages,
 3585:         snapshot.activePageId || activePageId,
 3586:       ),
 3587:     );
 3588:     setSelectedBlockId(snapshot.selectedBlockId || "");
 3589:     setFinalCheckConfirmedAt(snapshot.finalCheckConfirmedAt || null);
 3590:     setFinalChecklistConfirmed(Boolean(snapshot.finalCheckConfirmedAt));
 3591:   }

 3593:   function saveReportTwoDraftNow(showMessage = true) {
 3594:     const snapshot = createReportTwoDraftSnapshot();
 3595:     const serialized = JSON.stringify(snapshot);
 3596: 
 3597:     window.localStorage.setItem(reportTwoDraftStorageKey, serialized);
 3598:     lastDraftSerializedRef.current = serialized;
 3599:     setLastAutoSavedAt(snapshot.savedAt);
 3600: 
 3601:     if (showMessage) {
 3602:       setPopup({ type: "alert", title: "حفظ المسودة", message: "تم حفظ المسودة الحالية." });
 3603:     }
 3604:   }

 3606:   async function persistReportTwoDraft(approvedEditConfirmed = false) {
 3607:     if (reportTwoSaveSubmitting) return;
 3608:     const snapshotHtml = buildReportTwoSnapshotHtml();
 3609:     if (!snapshotHtml.trim()) {
 3610:       setPopup({ type: "alert", title: "حفظ التقرير", message: "تعذر التقاط معاينة التقرير الحالية." });
 3611:       return;
 3612:     }
 3613: 
 3614:     if (persistedReport?.status === "APPROVED" && !approvedEditConfirmed) {
 3615:       setPopup({
 3616:         type: "confirm",
 3617:         title: "تحرير تقرير معتمد",
 3618:         message: "سيتم تعديل محتوى التقرير مع بقاء حالة الاعتماد وتاريخ الاعتماد كما هما.",
 3619:         onConfirm: () => void persistReportTwoDraft(true),
 3620:       });
 3621:       return;
 3622:     }
 3623: 
 3624:     saveReportTwoDraftNow(false);
 3625:     setReportTwoSaveSubmitting(true);
 3626:     try {
 3627:       const activeTemplate = templates.find((item) => item.id === selectedTemplateOptionId);
 3628:       const response = await fetch(
 3629:         `/api/dashboard/report-2/cases/${encodeURIComponent(caseId)}/save`,
 3630:         {
 3631:           method: "PUT",
 3632:           headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
 3633:           body: JSON.stringify({
 3634:             reportTitle: getReportTwoSnapshotTitle(),
 3635:             templateId: selectedTemplateOptionId || null,
 3636:             templateName: activeTemplate?.name || template.name || null,
 3637:             variantId: selectedVariantId || null,
 3638:             snapshotPayload: preparedPayload,
 3639:             snapshotTemplateJson: signedVisiblePreviewTemplate,
 3640:             snapshotPagesJson: signedVisiblePreviewTemplate.pages,
 3641:             snapshotHtml,
 3642:             editorState: createReportTwoDraftSnapshot(),
 3643:             expectedVersion: persistedReport?.version ?? null,
 3644:             approvedEditConfirmed,
 3645:           }),
 3646:         },
 3647:       );
 3648:       const result = await response.json();
 3649:       if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.error || "تعذر حفظ التقرير.");
 3650:       setPersistedReport({
 3651:         id: result.report.id,
 3652:         status: result.report.status,
 3653:         version: result.report.version,
 3654:         approvedAt: result.report.approvedAt,
 3655:         editorState: result.report.editorState,
 3656:         previewUrl: result.previewUrl,
 3657:       });
 3658:       setReportTwoActionModal({
 3659:         title: result.report.status === "APPROVED" ? "تم حفظ التقرير المعتمد" : "تم حفظ التقرير",
 3660:         message: result.message,
 3661:         linkHref: result.previewUrl,
 3662:         linkLabel: "معاينة التقرير",
 3663:       });
 3664:     } catch (error) {
 3665:       setPopup({ type: "alert", title: "حفظ التقرير", message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "تعذر حفظ التقرير." });
 3666:     } finally {
 3667:       setReportTwoSaveSubmitting(false);
 3668:     }
 3669:   }

 3671:   function undoReportTwoLastChange() {
 3672:     const previous = undoSnapshots[0];
 3673: 
 3674:     if (!previous) {
 3675:       setPopup({ type: "alert", title: "التراجع", message: "لا يوجد تعديل سابق للرجوع إليه." });
 3676:       return;
 3677:     }
 3678: 
 3679:     restoreReportTwoDraftSnapshot(previous);
 3680: 
 3681:     const serialized = JSON.stringify(previous);
 3682:     window.localStorage.setItem(reportTwoDraftStorageKey, serialized);
 3683:     lastDraftSerializedRef.current = serialized;
 3684:     setLastAutoSavedAt(previous.savedAt);
 3685:     setUndoSnapshots((current) => current.slice(1));
 3686:   }
 3687:   function persistSavedRuntimeTemplates(items: ReportTwoSavedRuntimeTemplate[]) {
 3688:     setSavedRuntimeTemplates(items);
 3689:     writeReportTwoSavedTemplates(serviceSlugForSavedTemplates, items);
 3690:   }

 3692:   function saveCurrentRuntimeTemplate() {
 3693:     const name = runtimeTemplateName.trim();
 3694: 
 3695:     if (!name) {
 3696:       setPopup({ type: "alert", title: "حفظ القالب", message: "اكتب اسم القالب أولًا." });
 3697:       return;
 3698:     }
 3699: 
 3700:     const now = new Date().toISOString();
 3701:     const existingId = activeSavedRuntimeTemplateId || "";
 3702:     const id = existingId || makeId("saved-runtime-template");
 3703: 
 3704:     const item: ReportTwoSavedRuntimeTemplate = {
 3705:       id,
 3706:       name,
 3707:       serviceSlug: serviceSlugForSavedTemplates,
 3708:       sourceTemplateId: selectedTemplateOptionId,
 3709:       createdAt:
 3710:         savedRuntimeTemplates.find((template) => template.id === id)?.createdAt ||
 3711:         now,
 3712:       updatedAt: now,
 3713:       template: cloneReportTwoTemplate(template),
 3714:       headerValues,
 3715:       headerAlignments,
 3716:       logoSettings,
 3717:     };
 3718: 
 3719:     const nextItems = [
 3720:       item,
 3721:       ...savedRuntimeTemplates.filter((template) => template.id !== id),
 3722:     ];
 3723: 
 3724:     persistSavedRuntimeTemplates(nextItems);
 3725:     setActiveSavedRuntimeTemplateId(id);
 3726:     setRuntimeTemplateName(name);
 3727: 
 3728:     setPopup({ type: "alert", title: "حفظ القالب", message: "تم حفظ قالب التقرير الحالي." });
 3729:   }
 3730: 
 3731:   function applySavedRuntimeTemplate(templateId: string) {
 3732:     const saved = savedRuntimeTemplates.find((item) => item.id === templateId);
 3733: 
 3734:     if (!saved) return;
 3735: 
 3736:     const restoredTemplate = normalizeReportTwoLogicalTemplate(
 3737:       cloneReportTwoTemplate(saved.template),
 3738:       preparedPayload,
 3739:     );
 3740:     setTemplate(restoredTemplate);
 3741:     setSelectedTemplateOptionId(saved.sourceTemplateId || selectedTemplateOptionId);
 3742:     setHeaderValues(saved.headerValues || null);
 3743:     setHeaderAlignments(saved.headerAlignments || null);
 3744:     setLogoSettings(saved.logoSettings || null);
 3745:     setActiveSavedRuntimeTemplateId(saved.id);
 3746:     setSelectedQuickSavedTemplateId(saved.id);
 3747:     setRuntimeTemplateName(saved.name);
 3748: 
 3749:     setActivePageId(
 3750:       resolveReportTwoEquivalentPageId(restoredTemplate.pages, activePageId),
 3751:     );
 3752:     setSelectedBlockId("");
