 3814:   function updateBlock(blockId: string, updater: (block: StudioBlock) => StudioBlock) {
 3815:     const runtimeBlock =
 3816:       visiblePreviewTemplate.pages
 3817:         .flatMap((page) => page.blocks)
 3818:         .find((block) => block.id === blockId) || null;

 3828:           block.id === sourceBlockId ? updater(block) : block,
 3829:         ),
 3830:       })),
 3831:     }));

 3846:   function canDeleteReportTwoPage(pageId: string) {
 3847:     if (!template.pages.some((page) => page.id === pageId)) return false;
 3848:     if (protectedPageIds.includes(pageId)) return false;
 3849:     if (template.pages.length <= 1) return false;
 3850: 
 3851:     return true;
 3852:   }
 3853: 
 3854:   function canMoveReportTwoPage(
 3855:     pageId: string,
 3856:     direction: "previous" | "next",
 3857:   ) {
 3858:     const pageIds = visiblePreviewTemplate.pages.map((page) => page.id);
 3859:     const index = pageIds.indexOf(pageId);
 3860: 
 3861:     if (index < 0) return false;
 3862: 
 3863:     return direction === "previous"
 3864:       ? index > 0
 3865:       : index < pageIds.length - 1;
 3866:   }
 3867: 
 3868:   function moveReportTwoPage(
 3869:     pageId: string,
 3870:     direction: "previous" | "next",
 3871:   ) {
 3872:     if (!canMoveReportTwoPage(pageId, direction)) return;
 3873: 
 3874:     setTemplate((current) => ({
 3875:       ...current,
 3876:       pages: reorderReportTwoPages(current.pages, pageId, direction),
 3877:     }));
 3878:     setActivePageId(pageId);
 3879:   }

 3881:   function deleteReportTwoPage(pageId: string) {
 3882:     if (!template.pages.some((page) => page.id === pageId)) return;
 3883: 
 3884:     if (!canDeleteReportTwoPage(pageId)) {
 3885:       setPopup({ type: "alert", title: "حذف الصفحة", message: "لا يمكن حذف صفحة قادمة من قالب الاستديو الأصلي." });
 3886:       return;
 3887:     }
 3888: 
 3889:     setPopup({
 3890:       type: "confirm",
 3891:       title: "حذف الصفحة",
 3892:       message: "هل تريد حذف هذه الصفحة من التقرير؟",
 3893:       onConfirm: () => {
 3894:         const remainingPages = template.pages.filter(
 3895:           (page) => page.id !== pageId,
 3896:         );

 3899:           ...current,
 3900:           pages: remainingPages,
 3901:         }));
 3902:

 3904:         setSelectedBlockId("");

 3913:   function addBlock(kind: StudioBlockKind) {
 3914:     if (kind === "evidence-gallery" || kind === "signature-grid") {
 3915:       const existing = template.pages
 3916:         .flatMap((page) => page.blocks.map((block) => ({ block, pageId: page.id })))
 3917:         .find(({ block }) =>
 3918:           kind === "evidence-gallery"
 3919:             ? block.kind === "evidence-gallery"
 3920:             : isReportTwoSignatureBlock(block),
 3921:         );
 3922: 
 3923:       if (existing) {
 3924:         setActivePageId(existing.pageId);
 3925:         setSelectedBlockId(existing.block.id);
 3926:         setSemanticPlacementNotice(
 3927:           kind === "evidence-gallery"
 3928:             ? "كتلة الشواهد موجودة بالفعل في النموذج المنطقي."
 3929:             : "كتلة التوقيعات موجودة بالفعل في النموذج المنطقي.",
 3930:         );
 3931:         return;
 3932:       }
 3933:     }
 3934: 
 3935:     const targetPageId = getWritableReportTwoPageId(
 3936:       activePageId,
 3937:       activePage,
 3938:       template,
 3939:     );
 3940: 
 3941:     const targetPage = template.pages.find((page) => page.id === targetPageId);
 3942: 
 3943:     if (!targetPage) return;
 3944: 
 3945:     const block = createBlock(kind);
 3946:     const nextTemplate = {
 3947:       ...template,
 3948:       pages: template.pages.map((page) =>
 3949:         page.id === targetPage.id
 3950:           ? {
 3951:               ...page,
 3952:               blocks: [...page.blocks, block],
 3953:             }
 3954:           : page,
 3955:       ),
 3956:     };
 3957:     setTemplate(applyReportTwoPreparedExecutionSummary(nextTemplate, preparedPayload));
 3958: 
 3959:     setActivePageId(targetPage.id);
 3960:     setSelectedBlockId(block.id);
 3961:     setSemanticPlacementNotice("");
 3962:   }
 3963: 
 3964:   function removeSelectedBlock() {
 3965:     if (!selectedBlock || !activePage) return;
 3966: 
 3967:     const writablePageId = getWritableReportTwoPageId(activePageId, activePage, template);
 3968:     const sourcePage = template.pages.find((page) => page.id === writablePageId);
 3969:     if (!sourcePage) return;
 3970: 
 3971:     const blockExists = sourcePage.blocks.some((b) => b.id === selectedBlock.id);
 3972:     if (!blockExists) return;
 3973: 
 3974:     if (sourcePage.blocks.length <= 1) {
 3975:       setPopup({ type: "alert", title: "حذف البلوك", message: "لا يمكن حذف آخر بلوك داخل الصفحة." });
 3976:       return;
 3977:     }
 3978: 
 3979:     const remainingBlocks = sourcePage.blocks.filter(
 3980:       (block) => block.id !== selectedBlock.id,
 3981:     );
 3982: 
 3983:     updatePage(sourcePage.id, (page) => ({
 3984:       ...page,
 3985:       blocks: remainingBlocks,
 3986:     }));
 3987: 
 3988:     setSelectedBlockId(remainingBlocks[0]?.id || "");
 3989:   }
 3990: 
 3991:   function moveBlock(direction: "up" | "down") {
 3992:     if (!selectedBlock) return;
 3993: 
 3994:     const sourcePage = template.pages.find((page) =>
 3995:       page.blocks.some((block) => block.id === selectedBlock.id),
 3996:     );
 3997:     if (!sourcePage) return;
 3998: 
 3999:     const index = sourcePage.blocks.findIndex(
 4000:       (block) => block.id === selectedBlock.id,
 4001:     );
 4002: 
 4003:     if (index < 0) return;
 4004: 
 4005:     const selectedIsStudentTable =
 4006:       selectedBlock.kind === "structured-table" && isStudentDataTable(selectedBlock);
 4007:     if (selectedIsStudentTable) {
 4008:       setSemanticPlacementNotice("جدول بيانات الطلاب يظهر دائمًا في بداية التقرير.");
 4009:       return;
 4010:     }
 4011:     if (isReportTwoSignatureBlock(selectedBlock)) {
 4012:       setSemanticPlacementNotice("كتلة التوقيعات تظهر دائمًا في نهاية التقرير.");
 4013:       return;
 4014:     }
 4015: 
 4016:     const targetIndex = direction === "up" ? index - 1 : index + 1;
 4017: 
 4018:     if (targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= sourcePage.blocks.length) return;
 4019:     const targetBlock = sourcePage.blocks[targetIndex];
 4020:     if (direction === "down" && isReportTwoSignatureBlock(targetBlock)) {
 4021:       setSemanticPlacementNotice("لا يمكن وضع محتوى بعد كتلة التوقيعات.");
 4022:       return;
 4023:     }
 4024:     if (
 4025:       direction === "up" &&
 4026:       targetBlock.kind === "structured-table" &&
 4027:       isStudentDataTable(targetBlock)
 4028:     ) {
 4029:       setSemanticPlacementNotice("جدول بيانات الطلاب يظهر دائمًا في بداية التقرير.");
 4030:       return;
 4031:     }
 4032: 
 4033:     const nextBlocks = [...sourcePage.blocks];
 4034:     const [block] = nextBlocks.splice(index, 1);
 4035:     nextBlocks.splice(targetIndex, 0, block);
 4036: 
 4037:     updatePage(sourcePage.id, (page) => ({
 4038:       ...page,
 4039:       blocks: nextBlocks,
 4040:     }));
 4041:   }

 4044:   function updateDynamicField(
 4045:     blockId: string,
 4046:     fieldId: string,
 4047:     patch: Partial<ReportTwoDynamicField>,
 4048:   ) {
 4049:     updateBlock(blockId, (block) => ({
 4050:       ...block,
 4051:       dynamicFields: getDynamicFieldsForBlock(block, previewCase).map((field) =>
 4052:         field.id === fieldId
 4053:           ? {
 4054:               ...field,
 4055:               ...patch,
 4056:             }
 4057:           : field,
 4058:       ),
 4059:     }));
 4060:   }
 4061: 
 4062:   function moveDynamicField(
 4063:     blockId: string,
 4064:     fieldId: string,
 4065:     direction: "up" | "down",
 4066:   ) {
 4067:     updateBlock(blockId, (block) => {
 4068:       const fields = getDynamicFieldsForBlock(block, previewCase);
 4069:       const index = fields.findIndex((field) => field.id === fieldId);
 4070: 
 4071:       if (index < 0) return block;
 4072: 
 4073:       const targetIndex = direction === "up" ? index - 1 : index + 1;
 4074: 
 4075:       if (targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= fields.length) return block;
 4076: 
 4077:       const nextFields = [...fields];
 4078:       const [field] = nextFields.splice(index, 1);
 4079:       nextFields.splice(targetIndex, 0, field);
 4080: 
 4081:       return {
 4082:         ...block,
 4083:         dynamicFields: nextFields,
 4084:       };
 4085:     });
 4086:   }

 4092:     }));
 4093:   }
